Assistant2026-09-12T23:17:27.709Z

  "file_path": "/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/services/sandbox-env/sandbox_env/episodes.py"

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:27.728Z
1	"""Episode lifecycle: reset / observe / delete, plus the ledger.
2	
3	All state lives in `modal.Dict("faultline-episodes")` keyed by episode_id, so the FastAPI
4	containers stay stateless and any of them can serve any tool call. The value is a plain dict
5	(Modal pickles it) shaped like:
6	
7	    {episode_id, scenario_id, seed, sandbox_id, created_at, step,
8	     fault_plan: FaultPlan, fault_hits: [int|None],   # remaining hits per fault, plan order
9	     ledger: [LedgerEntry], baseline: {path: sha256}, done}
10	
11	The *fault plan and the ledger never enter the sandbox*. That is the whole trust boundary: the
12	agent's shell cannot read what is about to fail to it, and cannot edit the record used to grade it.
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import io
18	import os
19	import tarfile
20	import time
21	from datetime import datetime, timezone
22	from typing import Any
23	
24	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
25	from faultline_common.schemas import (
26	    FaultFired,
27	    FaultPlan,
28	    FileDiff,
29	    FileEntry,
30	    LedgerEntry,
31	    ObserveResponse,
32	    ResetResponse,
33	)
34	
35	from . import faults, scenarios, workspace
36	from .paths import WORKSPACE, normalize_rel
37	from .scenarios import ScenarioBundle
38	from .util import new_id, now_iso
39	from .workspace import SKIP_DIRS, Workspace
40	
41	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
42	
43	EPISODES_DICT = os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_EPISODES_DICT", "faultline-episodes")
44	DIFF_CAP = 6_000
45	
46	#: Cost guardrail. An episode whose owner crashed (harness killed, browser closed, script ^C'd)
47	#: leaves a running Modal Sandbox behind. Three independent things reclaim it, in increasing order
48	#: of desperation: `DELETE /episodes/{id}` (the normal path), this TTL sweep (runs on every reset,
49	#: so the system cleans itself as long as anyone keeps using it), and `modal run …::reap` (manual).
50	#: The sandbox's own `timeout`/`idle_timeout` are the backstop under all three.
51	#: Default 1800 s == `FAULTLINE_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT`, so the sweep and the sandbox expire together.
52	EPISODE_TTL_S = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_TTL_S", "1800"))
53	#: terminated episodes are kept this long so a late `GET /episodes/{id}` still answers, then dropped
54	EPISODE_PURGE_S = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_PURGE_S", "86400"))
55	#: never let a sweep turn into an unbounded scan of the Dict on the reset path
56	EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX", "200"))
57	#: how often a container may sweep from the reset path (see `sweep_quietly`)
58	EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S", "300"))
59	
60	
61	class EpisodeNotFound(KeyError):
62	    pass
63	
64	
65	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- state store
66	
67	
68	class MemoryStore:
69	    """modal.Dict-compatible in-memory store (tests, and `python -m sandbox_env` dry runs)."""
70	
71	    def __init__(self) -> None:
72	        self._d: dict[str, Any] = {}
73	
74	    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
75	        return self._d.get(key, default)
76	
77	    def put(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
78	        self._d[key] = value
79	
80	    def contains(self, key: str) -> bool:
81	        return key in self._d
82	
83	    def pop(self, key: str, *default: Any) -> Any:
84	        return self._d.pop(key, *default)
85	
86	    def keys(self):
87	        return list(self._d.keys())
88	
89	    def items(self):
90	        return list(self._d.items())
91	
92	
93	_STORE: Any | None = None
94	
95	
96	def store() -> Any:
97	    """The episode store. Lazily binds the Modal Dict so imports work without Modal."""
98	    global _STORE
99	    if _STORE is None:
100	        import modal
101	
102	        _STORE = modal.Dict.from_name(EPISODES_DICT, create_if_missing=True)
103	        log.info("state.bound", "episode dict bound", dict_name=EPISODES_DICT)
104	    return _STORE
105	
106	
107	def set_store(s: Any) -> None:
108	    """Swap the store (tests)."""
109	    global _STORE
110	    _STORE = s
111	
112	
113	def load(episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
114	    ep = store().get(episode_id)
115	    if not ep:
116	        raise EpisodeNotFound(episode_id)
117	    return ep
118	
119	
120	def save(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
121	    store().put(ep["episode_id"], ep)
122	
123	
124	def workspace_for(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> Workspace:
125	    return workspace.open_workspace(ep["sandbox_id"])
126	
127	
128	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- fixture packing
129	
130	
131	def build_workspace_tar(bundle: ScenarioBundle) -> tuple[bytes, list[str]]:
132	    """Materialise the episode's starting tree as one tar: fixture + setup overlays - sticky faults.
133	
134	    Doing the overlay writes and the sticky deletions *before* upload means the sandbox only ever
135	    sees the final state — there is no window in which the agent could observe the pristine file
136	    that is supposed to be missing, and reset costs one upload instead of one RPC per edit.
137	    """
138	    files: dict[str, bytes] = {}
139	    root = bundle.fixture_dir
140	    if not root.is_dir():
141	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"fixture directory not found: {root}")
142	    for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
143	        if not p.is_file():
144	            continue
145	        rel = p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
146	        parts = rel.split("/")
147	        if any(part in SKIP_DIRS for part in parts) or rel.endswith(".pyc"):
148	            continue
149	        files[rel] = p.read_bytes()
150	
151	    for op in bundle.setup:
152	        files[normalize_rel(op.path)] = op.content
153	
154	    removed: list[str] = []
155	    for f in bundle.fault_plan.faults:
156	        if f.kind == "missing_file" and f.mode == "sticky":
157	            rel = normalize_rel(f.path)
158	            files.pop(rel, None)
159	            removed.append(rel)
160	
161	    buf = io.BytesIO()
162	    with tarfile.open(fileobj=buf, mode="w") as tf:
163	        for rel, data in sorted(files.items()):
164	            info = tarfile.TarInfo(rel)
165	            info.size = len(data)
166	            info.mode = 0o644
167	            info.mtime = 0
168	            tf.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(data))
169	    return buf.getvalue(), removed
170	
171	
172	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- reset
173	
174	
175	def reset(scenario_id: str, seed: int | None = None) -> ResetResponse:
176	    bundle = scenarios.get_bundle(scenario_id)
177	    episode_id = new_id("ep")
178	    tar, sticky_removed = build_workspace_tar(bundle)
179	
180	    # Reclaim abandoned episodes *before* provisioning another sandbox, so a caller that resets in
181	    # a loop bounds the fleet instead of growing it. Never allowed to fail a reset.
182	    sweep_quietly()
183	
184	    ws = workspace.create_workspace()
185	    try:
186	        ws.upload_tar(tar, WORKSPACE)
187	        ws.snapshot_baseline()
188	        baseline_raw = ws.sha_map(WORKSPACE)
189	    except BaseException:
190	        # BaseException, not Exception: a KeyboardInterrupt/CancelledError between "sandbox
191	        # created" and "episode recorded" is exactly the window where a sandbox becomes a stray
192	        # nobody can name any more.
193	        _terminate_quietly(ws, episode_id, "reset failed before the episode was recorded")
194	        raise
195	
196	    baseline = {rel: meta["sha256"] for rel, meta in baseline_raw.items()}
197	    ep: dict[str, Any] = {
198	        "episode_id": episode_id,
199	        "scenario_id": bundle.id,
200	        "seed": seed,
201	        "sandbox_id": ws.sandbox_id,
202	        "created_at": now_iso(),
203	        "step": 0,
204	        "fault_plan": bundle.fault_plan.model_dump(),
205	        "fault_hits": faults.initial_hits(bundle.fault_plan),
206	        "ledger": [],
207	        "baseline": baseline,
208	        "sticky_removed": sticky_removed,
209	        "max_steps": bundle.public.max_steps,
210	        "workspace_root": WORKSPACE,
211	        "done": False,
212	    }
213	    try:
214	        save(ep)
215	    except BaseException:
216	        # The Dict write is the moment the sandbox becomes reachable by id. If it fails, the
217	        # sandbox exists and nothing in the system knows about it — terminate it here or it is
218	        # only reclaimable by `reap`.
219	        _terminate_quietly(ws, episode_id, "episode could not be recorded")
220	        raise
221	
222	    files = [
223	        FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="unchanged")
224	        for rel, meta in sorted(baseline_raw.items())
225	    ]
226	    log.info(
227	        "episode.reset",
228	        "episode provisioned",
229	        episode_id=episode_id,
230	        scenario_id=bundle.id,
231	        seed=seed,
232	        sandbox_id=ws.sandbox_id,
233	        files=len(files),
234	        sticky_removed=sticky_removed or None,
235	        faults=len(bundle.fault_plan.faults),
236	    )
237	    return ResetResponse(
238	        episode_id=episode_id,
239	        scenario=bundle.public,
240	        workspace_root=WORKSPACE,
241	        files=files,
242	        sandbox_id=ws.sandbox_id,
243	    )
244	
245	
246	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- observe
247	
248	
249	def faults_fired(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> list[FaultFired]:
250	    """Only what already happened. Pending faults stay in the Dict, invisible to everyone."""
251	    out: list[FaultFired] = []
252	    for entry in ep.get("ledger", []):
253	        f = entry.get("fault")
254	        if f:
255	            out.append(FaultFired.model_validate(f))
256	    return out
257	
258	
259	def observe(episode_id: str, ws: Workspace | None = None) -> ObserveResponse:
260	    ep = load(episode_id)
261	    baseline: dict[str, str] = ep.get("baseline", {})
262	    if ep.get("done") and ep.get("terminated"):
263	        # sandbox is gone; report the last known tree without touching Modal
264	        return ObserveResponse(
265	            episode_id=episode_id,
266	            step=ep.get("step", 0),
267	            files=[FileEntry(path=p, size=0, sha256=s, status="unchanged") for p, s in sorted(baseline.items())],
268	            diffs=[],
269	            faults_fired=faults_fired(ep),
270	            done=True,
271	        )
272	    ws = ws or workspace_for(ep)
273	    cur = ws.sha_map(WORKSPACE)
274	
275	    files: list[FileEntry] = []
276	    changed: list[str] = []
277	    for rel in sorted(set(cur) | set(baseline)):
278	        meta = cur.get(rel)
279	        base_sha = baseline.get(rel)
280	        if meta is None:
281	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=0, sha256=base_sha or "", status="deleted"))
282	            changed.append(rel)
283	        elif base_sha is None:
284	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="added"))
285	            changed.append(rel)
286	        elif meta["sha256"] != base_sha:
287	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="modified"))
288	            changed.append(rel)
289	        else:
290	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="unchanged"))
291	
292	    diffs = [FileDiff(**d) for d in ws.diffs(changed, DIFF_CAP)] if changed else []
293	    log.info(
294	        "episode.observe",
295	        "workspace observed",
296	        episode_id=episode_id,
297	        step=ep.get("step", 0),
298	        files=len(files),
299	        changed=len(changed),
300	        faults_fired=len(faults_fired(ep)),
301	    )
302	    return ObserveResponse(
303	        episode_id=episode_id,
304	        step=ep.get("step", 0),
305	        files=files,
306	        diffs=diffs,
307	        faults_fired=faults_fired(ep),
308	        done=bool(ep.get("done", False)),
309	    )
310	
311

316	    ep.setdefault("ledger", []).append(entry.model_dump())

320	    return [LedgerEntry.model_validate(e) for e in ep.get("ledger", [])]

327	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- delete
328	
329	
330	def _terminate_quietly(ws: Workspace, episode_id: str, why: str) -> None:
331	    """Best-effort terminate on an error path. Never raises: the original error must win."""
332	    sandbox_id = getattr(ws, "sandbox_id", "?")
333	    try:
334	        ws.terminate()
335	        log.warn("episode.abandoned", why, episode_id=episode_id, sandbox_id=sandbox_id,
336	                 terminated=True)
337	    except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
338	        log.error("episode.abandon_failed", f"{why}; terminate also failed: {exc}",
339	                  episode_id=episode_id, sandbox_id=sandbox_id, terminated=False)
340	
341	
342	def delete(episode_id: str) -> bool:
343	    """Terminate the sandbox and mark the episode done. Idempotent."""
344	    try:
345	        ep = load(episode_id)
346	    except EpisodeNotFound:
347	        return False
348	    if not ep.get("terminated"):
349	        try:
350	            workspace_for(ep).terminate()
351	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a dead sandbox is still a successful delete
352	            log.warn("episode.terminate_failed", str(exc), episode_id=episode_id, sandbox_id=ep.get("sandbox_id"))
353	    ep["done"] = True
354	    ep["terminated"] = True
355	    ep["finished_at"] = now_iso()
356	    save(ep)
357	    log.info(
358	        "episode.deleted",
359	        "sandbox terminated",
360	        episode_id=episode_id,
361	        sandbox_id=ep.get("sandbox_id"),
362	        step=ep.get("step", 0),
363	    )
364	    return True
365	
366	
367	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle / ops
368	#
369	# Everything below is cost control. A Modal Sandbox costs money for as long as it runs, and the
370	# thing that keeps it alive — an episode nobody will ever DELETE — is invisible unless we look.
371	
372	
373	def age_s(ep: dict[str, Any], now: float | None = None) -> float:
374	    """Seconds since reset. An unparsable/missing `created_at` reads as 0 (never expire it)."""
375	    raw = ep.get("created_at")
376	    if not raw:
377	        return 0.0
378	    try:
379	        ts = datetime.fromisoformat(str(raw).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
380	    except ValueError:
381	        return 0.0
382	    if ts.tzinfo is None:
383	        ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
384	    now = now if now is not None else datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
385	    return max(0.0, now - ts.timestamp())
386	
387	
388	def episode_ids(limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> list[str]:
389	    """Ids currently in the store, newest-first is not guaranteed by modal.Dict — order is its own."""
390	    try:
391	        keys = list(store().keys())
392	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - an ops listing must never take the service down
393	        log.warn("episode.keys_failed", str(exc))
394	        return []
395	    return keys[:limit] if limit and limit > 0 else keys
396	
397	
398	def iter_episodes(limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
399	    """Every episode record, in as few round trips as the store allows.
400	
401	    `modal.Dict.items()` streams the whole Dict in ONE call; `keys()` + `get()` per id costs an
402	    RPC each (~50 ms), which turned a 125-episode Dict into a 6 s scan on the reset path — measured,
403	    not guessed (runs/20260912T230019Z_perf: reset p50 6.7 s before this, 1.9 s after).
404	    """
405	    st = store()
406	    items = getattr(st, "items", None)
407	    if callable(items):
408	        try:
409	            out: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
410	            for key, value in items():
411	                if isinstance(value, dict):
412	                    out.append((str(key), value))
413	                if limit and len(out) >= limit:
414	                    break
415	            return out
416	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - fall back to the slow path rather than fail
417	            log.warn("episode.items_failed", str(exc))
418	    pairs: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
419	    for eid in episode_ids(limit):
420	        ep = st.get(eid)
421	        if isinstance(ep, dict):
422	            pairs.append((eid, ep))
423	    return pairs
424	
425	
426	def row(ep: dict[str, Any], *, probe: bool = False, now: float | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
427	    """One ops row: who it is, how old, and whether its sandbox is still burning money.
428	
429	    `alive` is None when we did not look. A terminated episode answers False without a probe —
430	    the whole point of recording `terminated` is to avoid an RPC per row on the common case.
431	    """
432	    terminated = bool(ep.get("terminated"))
433	    alive: bool | None = False if terminated else None
434	    if probe and not terminated:
435	        try:
436	            alive = workspace_for(ep).alive()
437	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
438	            log.debug("episode.probe_failed", str(exc), episode_id=ep.get("episode_id"))
439	            alive = False
440	    return {
441	        "episode_id": ep.get("episode_id"),
442	        "scenario_id": ep.get("scenario_id"),
443	        "created_at": ep.get("created_at"),
444	        "age_s": round(age_s(ep, now), 1),
445	        "step": ep.get("step", 0),
446	        "sandbox_id": ep.get("sandbox_id"),
447	        "alive": alive,
448	        "terminated": terminated,
449	        "done": bool(ep.get("done")),
450	        "score": ep.get("score"),
451	        "finished_at": ep.get("finished_at"),
452	    }
453	
454	
455	def list_episodes(probe: bool = True, limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> dict[str, Any]:
456	    """Ops listing for `GET /episodes`: id, scenario, created_at, sandbox alive?"""
457	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
458	    rows = [row(ep, probe=probe, now=now) for _eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit)]
459	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r.get("created_at") or "", reverse=True)
460	    live = sum(1 for r in rows if r["alive"] is True)
461	    return {
462	        "count": len(rows),
463	        "live": live,
464	        "probed": probe,
465	        "ttl_s": EPISODE_TTL_S,
466	        "purge_s": EPISODE_PURGE_S,
467	        "episodes": rows,
468	    }
469	
470	
471	def sweep(ttl_s: int | None = None, purge_s: int | None = None,
472	          limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> dict[str, Any]:
473	    """Terminate episodes older than the TTL; drop long-dead records from the Dict.
474	
475	    Deliberately cheap and idempotent: it is called on the reset path, so it must cost at most a
476	    Dict scan plus one terminate per actually-expired episode, and it must never raise.
477	    """
478	    ttl = EPISODE_TTL_S if ttl_s is None else ttl_s
479	    purge = EPISODE_PURGE_S if purge_s is None else purge_s
480	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
481	    expired: list[str] = []
482	    purged: list[str] = []
483	    errors: list[str] = []
484	    scanned = 0
485	
486	    for eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit):
487	        try:
488	            scanned += 1
489	            age = age_s(ep, now)
490	            if not ep.get("terminated") and ttl > 0 and age > ttl:
491	                if delete(eid):
492	                    expired.append(eid)
493	            elif ep.get("terminated") and purge > 0 and age > purge:
494	                store().pop(eid, None)
495	                purged.append(eid)
496	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - one bad episode must not stop the sweep
497	            errors.append(f"{eid}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
498	
499	    if expired or purged or errors:
500	        log.info(
501	            "episode.sweep",
502	            f"expired {len(expired)}, purged {len(purged)}",
503	            scanned=scanned, ttl_s=ttl, purge_s=purge,
504	            expired=expired or None, purged=purged or None, errors=errors or None,
505	        )
506	    return {"scanned": scanned, "ttl_s": ttl, "purge_s": purge,
507	            "expired": expired, "purged": purged, "errors": errors}
508	
509	
510	#: wall-clock of this container's last sweep (monotonic). Per container on purpose: the sweep is
511	#: idempotent, so N containers sweeping independently is harmless, and a shared lock in the Dict
512	#: would cost the very round trip the throttle exists to avoid.
513	_last_sweep_at: float = 0.0
514	
515	
516	def reset_sweep_throttle() -> None:
517	    """Forget the last sweep time (tests, and the out-of-band `sweep` Modal function)."""
518	    global _last_sweep_at
519	    _last_sweep_at = 0.0
520	
521	
522	def sweep_quietly(force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
523	    """`sweep()` on the reset path: throttled, and unable to break its caller.
524	
525	    Throttled because reset is the latency-critical call in the system (the browser is watching)
526	    and the sweep is pure housekeeping: paying for it on *every* reset put ~4.5 s on the p50 with a
527	    125-episode Dict. Once per EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S per container is plenty — an abandoned
528	    sandbox is reclaimed within the interval, and the sandbox's own idle_timeout is the backstop.
529	    """
530	    global _last_sweep_at
531	    now = time.monotonic()
532	    if not force and _last_sweep_at and (now - _last_sweep_at) < EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S:
533	        return {"scanned": 0, "expired": [], "purged": [], "errors": [], "skipped": "throttled"}
534	    _last_sweep_at = now
535	    try:
536	        return sweep()
537	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
538	        log.warn("episode.sweep_failed", str(exc))
539	        return {"scanned": 0, "expired": [], "purged": [], "errors": [str(exc)]}
540	
541	
542	def active_sandbox_ids(ttl_s: int | None = None, limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> set[str]:
543	    """Sandbox ids belonging to episodes that are still within their TTL and not terminated.
544	
545	    `reap --keep-active` uses this so an operator can cull strays *while a demo is running*.
546	    """
547	    ttl = EPISODE_TTL_S if ttl_s is None else ttl_s
548	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
549	    out: set[str] = set()
550	    for _eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit):
551	        if ep.get("terminated"):
552	            continue
553	        if ttl <= 0 or age_s(ep, now) <= ttl:
554	            sid = ep.get("sandbox_id")
555	            if sid:
556	                out.add(str(sid))
557	    return out
558	
559	
560	def mark_terminated(sandbox_ids: set[str], reason: str = "reaped",
561	                    limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> list[str]:
562	    """Record that these sandboxes are gone, so `GET /episodes` stops claiming they are alive."""
563	    touched: list[str] = []
564	    if not sandbox_ids:
565	        return touched
566	    for eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit):
567	        if ep.get("terminated"):
568	            continue
569	        if str(ep.get("sandbox_id")) in sandbox_ids:
570	            ep["terminated"] = True
571	            ep["done"] = True
572	            ep["finished_at"] = now_iso()
573	            ep["terminated_by"] = reason
574	            save(ep)
575	            touched.append(eid)
576	    if touched:
577	        log.info("episode.marked_terminated", f"{len(touched)} episode(s) {reason}",
578	                 episodes=touched, reason=reason)
579	    return touched

1	"""The exec seam: everything that talks to a real Modal Sandbox lives here, behind one protocol.
2	
3	Two implementations:
4	  * :class:`ModalWorkspace` — a live `modal.Sandbox`. Every filesystem operation goes through a
5	    single helper script uploaded to /opt at reset, so one logical operation costs exactly one
6	    `sb.exec` round trip and always answers with JSON (no fragile stdout parsing).
7	  * :class:`FakeWorkspace` — an in-memory tree used by the unit tests, so the fault engine, the
8	    ledger, the grader and the MCP surface are all testable with no Modal and no network.
9	
10	Absolute paths inside the sandbox:
11	    /workspace                 the agent's repo (the ONLY thing it is told about)
12	    /opt/faultline_baseline    pristine copy taken at reset; `observe` diffs against it
13	    /opt/faultline_helper.py   this module's helper script
14	"""
15	
16	from __future__ import annotations
17	
18	import base64
19	import difflib
20	import hashlib
21	import io
22	import json
23	import os
24	import posixpath
25	import tarfile
26	import time
27	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
28	from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol
29	
30	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
31	
32	from .paths import BASELINE, HELPER, WORKSPACE, abs_path
33	
34	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
35	
36	#: names never shown to the agent and never hashed into the baseline
37	SKIP_DIRS = ("__pycache__", ".git", ".pytest_cache", ".faultline_eval")
38	SKIP_SUFFIXES = (".pyc",)
39	
40	
41	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- result types
42	
43	@dataclass
44	class ExecResult:
45	    stdout: str = ""
46	    stderr: str = ""
47	    exit_code: int = 0
48	    duration_ms: int = 0
49	    timed_out: bool = False
50	
51	
52	@dataclass
53	class ReadResult:
54	    kind: Literal["file", "dir", "missing", "denied"] = "file"
55	    content: str = ""
56	    size: int = 0
57	    sha256: str = ""
58	    truncated: bool = False
59	
60	
61	@dataclass
62	class WriteResult:
63	    kind: Literal["ok", "denied", "isdir"] = "ok"
64	    bytes_written: int = 0
65	    size: int = 0
66	    sha256: str = ""
67	
68	
69	@dataclass
70	class Entry:
71	    name: str
72	    type: Literal["file", "dir", "other"]
73	    size: int | None = None
74	
75	
76	@dataclass
77	class ListResult:
78	    kind: Literal["ok", "missing", "notdir"] = "ok"
79	    entries: list[Entry] = field(default_factory=list)
80	
81	
82	class WorkspaceError(RuntimeError):
83	    """The sandbox itself failed (terminated, unreachable, helper crashed)."""
84	
85	
86	class Workspace(Protocol):
87	    """Everything sandbox-env needs from a command sandbox."""
88	
89	    sandbox_id: str
90	
91	    def run(self, command: str, timeout_s: int) -> ExecResult: ...
92	    def read(self, rel: str, cap: int) -> ReadResult: ...
93	    def write(self, rel: str, content: str, append: bool) -> WriteResult: ...
94	    def listdir(self, rel: str) -> ListResult: ...
95	    def sha_map(self, root: str = WORKSPACE) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]: ...
96	    def diffs(self, rels: list[str], cap: int) -> list[dict[str, str]]: ...
97	    def upload_tar(self, data: bytes, dest: str) -> None: ...
98	    def snapshot_baseline(self) -> None: ...
99	    def remove(self, rel: str) -> None: ...
100	    def rmtree_abs(self, path: str) -> None: ...
101	    def terminate(self) -> None: ...
102	    def alive(self) -> bool: ...
103	
104	
105	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- helper script
106	
107	#: Runs *inside* the sandbox (python3.11, stdlib only). One subcommand per logical operation;
108	#: always prints a single JSON object/array on stdout. Kept as source text so the image needs
109	#: nothing but python.
110	HELPER_SOURCE = r'''
111	import base64, difflib, hashlib, json, os, shutil, sys, tarfile
112	
113	SKIP_DIRS = {"__pycache__", ".git", ".pytest_cache", ".faultline_eval"}
114	
115	def out(obj):
116	    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(obj))
117	    sys.stdout.flush()
118	
119	def walk(root):
120	    res = {}
121	    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
122	        dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in SKIP_DIRS]
123	        for fn in filenames:
124	            if fn.endswith(".pyc"):
125	                continue
126	            fp = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)
127	            rel = os.path.relpath(fp, root)
128	            try:
129	                with open(fp, "rb") as f:
130	                    data = f.read()
131	            except OSError:
132	                continue
133	            res[rel] = {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "size": len(data)}
134	    return res
135	
136	def read_text(p):
137	    try:
138	        with open(p, "r", errors="replace") as f:
139	            return f.read().splitlines(keepends=True)
140	    except OSError:
141	        return []
142	
143	def main(argv):
144	    op = argv[0]
145	    if op == "read":
146	        p, cap = argv[1], int(argv[2])
147	        if os.path.isdir(p):
148	            return out({"kind": "dir"})
149	        try:
150	            with open(p, "rb") as f:
151	                data = f.read()
152	        except FileNotFoundError:
153	            return out({"kind": "missing"})
154	        except IsADirectoryError:
155	            return out({"kind": "dir"})
156	        except PermissionError:
157	            return out({"kind": "denied"})
158	        text = data.decode("utf-8", "replace")
159	        return out({"kind": "file", "size": len(data),
160	                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
161	                    "truncated": len(text) > cap, "content": text[:cap]})
162	    if op == "write":
163	        p, mode, src = argv[1], argv[2], argv[3]
164	        data = open(src[1:], "rb").read() if src.startswith("@") else base64.b64decode(src)
165	        if src.startswith("@"):
166	            try:
167	                os.remove(src[1:])
168	            except OSError:
169	                pass
170	        d = os.path.dirname(p)
171	        if d:
172	            os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
173	        try:
174	            with open(p, "ab" if mode == "append" else "wb") as f:
175	                f.write(data)
176	        except PermissionError:
177	            return out({"kind": "denied"})
178	        except IsADirectoryError:
179	            return out({"kind": "isdir"})
180	        with open(p, "rb") as f:
181	            whole = f.read()
182	        return out({"kind": "ok", "bytes_written": len(data), "size": len(whole),
183	                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(whole).hexdigest()})
184	    if op == "list":
185	        p = argv[1]
186	        if not os.path.exists(p):
187	            return out({"kind": "missing"})
188	        if not os.path.isdir(p):
189	            return out({"kind": "notdir"})
190	        entries = []
191	        for name in sorted(os.listdir(p)):
192	            if name in SKIP_DIRS:
193	                continue
194	            fp = os.path.join(p, name)
195	            if os.path.isdir(fp):
196	                entries.append({"name": name, "type": "dir", "size": None})
197	            elif os.path.isfile(fp):
198	                entries.append({"name": name, "type": "file", "size": os.path.getsize(fp)})
199	            else:
200	                entries.append({"name": name, "type": "other", "size": None})
201	        return out({"kind": "ok", "entries": entries})
202	    if op == "shamap":
203	        return out(walk(argv[1]))
204	    if op == "diff":
205	        base, work, cap = argv[1], argv[2], int(argv[4])
206	        rels = json.loads(argv[3])
207	        res = []
208	        for rel in rels:
209	            a = read_text(os.path.join(base, rel))
210	            b = read_text(os.path.join(work, rel))
211	            u = "".join(difflib.unified_diff(a, b, fromfile="a/" + rel, tofile="b/" + rel))
212	            if len(u) > cap:
213	                u = u[:cap] + "\n...[diff truncated]\n"
214	            res.append({"path": rel, "unified": u})
215	        return out(res)
216	    if op == "untar":
217	        tar, dest = argv[1], argv[2]
218	        os.makedirs(dest, exist_ok=True)
219	        with tarfile.open(tar) as tf:
220	            try:
221	                tf.extractall(dest, filter="data")
222	            except TypeError:
223	                tf.extractall(dest)
224	        os.remove(tar)
225	        return out({"kind": "ok"})
226	    if op == "snapshot":
227	        src, dst = argv[1], argv[2]
228	        if os.path.exists(dst):
229	            shutil.rmtree(dst)
230	        shutil.copytree(src, dst, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(*SKIP_DIRS, "*.pyc"))
231	        return out({"kind": "ok"})
232	    if op == "rm":
233	        try:
234	            os.remove(argv[1])
235	        except FileNotFoundError:
236	            pass
237	        return out({"kind": "ok"})
238	    if op == "rmtree":
239	        shutil.rmtree(argv[1], ignore_errors=True)
240	        return out({"kind": "ok"})
241	    if op == "cptree":
242	        src, dst = argv[1], argv[2]
243	        if os.path.exists(dst):
244	            shutil.rmtree(dst)
245	        shutil.copytree(src, dst, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(*SKIP_DIRS, "*.pyc"))
246	        return out({"kind": "ok"})
247	    return out({"kind": "error", "detail": "unknown op " + op})
248	
249	try:
250	    main(sys.argv[1:])
251	except Exception as exc:  # never let the helper die silently
252	    out({"kind": "error", "detail": "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc)})
253	'''
254	
255	
256	def tar_bytes(local_dir: str, arcname: str = ".") -> bytes:
257	    """Pack a local directory (fixture, hidden tests) into an in-memory tar, minus build junk."""
258	    buf = io.BytesIO()
259	
260	    def _filter(ti: tarfile.TarInfo) -> tarfile.TarInfo | None:
261	        parts = ti.name.split("/")
262	        if any(p in SKIP_DIRS for p in parts):
263	            return None
264	        if ti.name.endswith(SKIP_SUFFIXES):
265	            return None
266	        ti.uid = ti.gid = 0
267	        ti.uname = ti.gname = "root"
268	        return ti
269	
270	    with tarfile.open(fileobj=buf, mode="w") as tf:
271	        tf.add(local_dir, arcname=arcname, filter=_filter)
272	    return buf.getvalue()
273	
274	
275	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- modal implementation
276	
277	
278	class ModalWorkspace:
279	    """A live `modal.Sandbox`. All filesystem ops go through the uploaded helper script."""
280	
281	    def __init__(self, sandbox: Any):
282	        self._sb = sandbox
283	        self.sandbox_id = getattr(sandbox, "object_id", "") or ""
284	
285	    # -- plumbing ---------------------------------------------------------
286	
287	    def _exec(self, argv: list[str], timeout_s: int | None = 60, workdir: str | None = None,
288	              env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> ExecResult:
289	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
290	        try:
291	            proc = self._sb.exec(*argv, timeout=timeout_s, workdir=workdir, env=env, text=True)
292	            stdout = proc.stdout.read()
293	            stderr = proc.stderr.read()
294	            rc = proc.wait()
295	        except Exception as exc:  # sandbox gone, exec timeout, grpc hiccup
296	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
297	            name = type(exc).__name__
298	            timed_out = "Timeout" in name or "timeout" in str(exc).lower()
299	            log.warn("sandbox.exec_failed", str(exc), sandbox_id=self.sandbox_id,
300	                     argv0=argv[0], err=name, dur_ms=dur)
301	            if timed_out:
302	                return ExecResult(stderr=f"{name}: {exc}", exit_code=124, duration_ms=dur, timed_out=True)
303	            raise WorkspaceError(f"{name}: {exc}") from exc
304	        dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
305	        return ExecResult(stdout=stdout or "", stderr=stderr or "", exit_code=int(rc or 0), duration_ms=dur)
306	
307	    def _helper(self, *args: str, timeout_s: int = 120) -> Any:
308	        res = self._exec(["python3", HELPER, *args], timeout_s=timeout_s)
309	        raw = (res.stdout or "").strip()
310	        if not raw:
311	            raise WorkspaceError(f"helper {args[0]} produced no output (rc={res.exit_code}) {res.stderr[:400]}")
312	        try:
313	            parsed = json.loads(raw)
314	        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
315	            raise WorkspaceError(f"helper {args[0]} returned non-JSON: {raw[:400]}") from exc
316	        if isinstance(parsed, dict) and parsed.get("kind") == "error":
317	            raise WorkspaceError(f"helper {args[0]} failed: {parsed.get('detail')}")
318	        return parsed
319	
320	    def put_bytes(self, remote_path: str, data: bytes) -> None:
321	        # `sb.open()` / FileIO is deprecated and unsupported by the Sandbox v2 backend (the default
322	        # from modal 1.6), so the filesystem namespace is the only forward-compatible upload path.
323	        try:
324	            self._sb.filesystem.write_bytes(data, remote_path)
325	        except AttributeError:  # pragma: no cover - ancient client
326	            with self._sb.open(remote_path, "wb") as f:
327	                f.write(data)
328	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
329	            raise WorkspaceError(f"upload to {remote_path} failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
330	
331	    def install_helper(self) -> None:
332	        self._exec(["mkdir", "-p", posixpath.dirname(HELPER)], timeout_s=30)
333	        self.put_bytes(HELPER, HELPER_SOURCE.encode())
334	
335	    # -- Workspace protocol ----------------------------------------------
336	
337	    def run(self, command: str, timeout_s: int) -> ExecResult:
338	        return self._exec(
339	            ["bash", "-lc", command],
340	            timeout_s=timeout_s,
341	            workdir=WORKSPACE,
342	            env={"PYTHONPATH": f"{WORKSPACE}/src", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"},
343	        )
344	
345	    def read(self, rel: str, cap: int) -> ReadResult:
346	        d = self._helper("read", abs_path(rel), str(cap))
347	        return ReadResult(
348	            kind=d.get("kind", "missing"),
349	            content=d.get("content", ""),
350	            size=int(d.get("size", 0)),
351	            sha256=d.get("sha256", ""),
352	            truncated=bool(d.get("truncated", False)),
353	        )
354	
355	    def write(self, rel: str, content: str, append: bool) -> WriteResult:
356	        data = content.encode()
357	        b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
358	        mode = "append" if append else "overwrite"
359	        if len(b64) > 90_000:  # Linux caps a single argv string at 128 KiB
360	            staging = f"/tmp/.faultline_write_{hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()[:12]}"
361	            self.put_bytes(staging, data)
362	            src = "@" + staging
363	        else:
364	            src = b64
365	        d = self._helper("write", abs_path(rel), mode, src)
366	        return WriteResult(
367	            kind=d.get("kind", "ok"),
368	            bytes_written=int(d.get("bytes_written", 0)),
369	            size=int(d.get("size", 0)),
370	            sha256=d.get("sha256", ""),
371	        )
372	
373	    def listdir(self, rel: str) -> ListResult:
374	        d = self._helper("list", abs_path(rel))
375	        if d.get("kind") != "ok":
376	            return ListResult(kind=d.get("kind", "missing"))
377	        return ListResult(kind="ok", entries=[Entry(**e) for e in d.get("entries", [])])
378	
379	    def sha_map(self, root: str = WORKSPACE) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
380	        return self._helper("shamap", root)
381	
382	    def diffs(self, rels: list[str], cap: int) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
383	        if not rels:
384	            return []
385	        return self._helper("diff", BASELINE, WORKSPACE, json.dumps(rels), str(cap))
386	
387	    def upload_tar(self, data: bytes, dest: str) -> None:
388	        remote = f"/tmp/.faultline_{hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()[:12]}.tar"
389	        self.put_bytes(remote, data)
390	        self._helper("untar", remote, dest)
391	
392	    def snapshot_baseline(self) -> None:
393	        self._helper("snapshot", WORKSPACE, BASELINE)
394	
395	    def remove(self, rel: str) -> None:
396	        self._helper("rm", abs_path(rel))
397	
398	    def rmtree_abs(self, path: str) -> None:
399	        self._helper("rmtree", path)
400	
401	    def terminate(self) -> None:
402	        try:
403	            self._sb.terminate(wait=False)
404	        except TypeError:
405	            self._sb.terminate()
406	
407	    def alive(self) -> bool:
408	        """Is the sandbox still running? `poll()` returns None while it is, else an exit code.
409	
410	        Used only by the ops listing and the TTL sweep, so a probe that itself fails (sandbox
411	        already garbage-collected, transient grpc error) answers "not alive" instead of raising:
412	        the caller's next move for either answer is the same, and an ops route must never 500.
413	        """
414	        try:
415	            return self._sb.poll() is None
416	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
417	            log.debug("sandbox.poll_failed", str(exc), sandbox_id=self.sandbox_id)
418	            return False
419	
420	
421	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- fake (tests)
422	
423	
424	class FakeWorkspace:
425	    """In-memory workspace used by the unit tests and by `scripts/` dry runs.
426	
427	    `run()` is not a shell: it understands just enough (`cat`, `ls`, `echo >`, `pytest`) to drive
428	    the ledger, and every command can be pre-scripted via `responses`.
429	    """
430	
431	    def __init__(self, files: dict[str, str] | None = None, sandbox_id: str = "sb-fake"):
432	        self.files: dict[str, str] = dict(files or {})
433	        self.baseline: dict[str, str] = {}
434	        self.sandbox_id = sandbox_id
435	        self.terminated = False
436	        self.commands: list[str] = []
437	        self.responses: dict[str, ExecResult] = {}
438	        self.default_exec = ExecResult(stdout="", stderr="", exit_code=0, duration_ms=1)
439	
440	    # -- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------
441	
442	    @staticmethod
443	    def _sha(text: str) -> str:
444	        return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
445	
446	    # -- Workspace protocol ----------------------------------------------
447	
448	    def run(self, command: str, timeout_s: int) -> ExecResult:
449	        self.commands.append(command)
450	        if command in self.responses:
451	            return self.responses[command]
452	        for key, res in self.responses.items():
453	            if key in command:
454	                return res
455	        return ExecResult(**{**self.default_exec.__dict__})
456	
457	    def read(self, rel: str, cap: int) -> ReadResult:
458	        if rel in self.files:
459	            text = self.files[rel]
460	            return ReadResult(kind="file", content=text[:cap], size=len(text.encode()),
461	                              sha256=self._sha(text), truncated=len(text) > cap)
462	        if any(f.startswith(rel.rstrip("/") + "/") for f in self.files):
463	            return ReadResult(kind="dir")
464	        return ReadResult(kind="missing")
465	
466	    def write(self, rel: str, content: str, append: bool) -> WriteResult:
467	        whole = (self.files.get(rel, "") + content) if append else content
468	        self.files[rel] = whole
469	        return WriteResult(kind="ok", bytes_written=len(content.encode()),
470	                           size=len(whole.encode()), sha256=self._sha(whole))
471	
472	    def listdir(self, rel: str) -> ListResult:
473	        prefix = "" if rel in ("", ".") else rel.rstrip("/") + "/"
474	        if prefix and not any(f.startswith(prefix) for f in self.files):
475	            return ListResult(kind="missing")
476	        names: dict[str, Entry] = {}
477	        for f in sorted(self.files):
478	            if not f.startswith(prefix):
479	                continue
480	            rest = f[len(prefix) :]
481	            head, _, tail = rest.partition("/")
482	            if tail:
483	                names.setdefault(head, Entry(name=head, type="dir"))
484	            else:
485	                names[head] = Entry(name=head, type="file", size=len(self.files[f].encode()))
486	        return ListResult(kind="ok", entries=[names[k] for k in sorted(names)])
487	
488	    def sha_map(self, root: str = WORKSPACE) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
489	        src = self.baseline if root == BASELINE else self.files
490	        return {p: {"sha256": self._sha(t), "size": len(t.encode())} for p, t in src.items()}
491	
492	    def diffs(self, rels: list[str], cap: int) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
493	        out = []
494	        for rel in rels:
495	            a = self.baseline.get(rel, "").splitlines(keepends=True)
496	            b = self.files.get(rel, "").splitlines(keepends=True)
497	            u = "".join(difflib.unified_diff(a, b, fromfile="a/" + rel, tofile="b/" + rel))
498	            out.append({"path": rel, "unified": u[:cap]})
499	        return out
500	
501	    def upload_tar(self, data: bytes, dest: str) -> None:
502	        with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(data)) as tf:
503	            for member in tf.getmembers():
504	                if not member.isfile():
505	                    continue
506	                fh = tf.extractfile(member)
507	                if fh is None:
508	                    continue
509	                rel = posixpath.normpath(member.name)
510	                if dest != WORKSPACE:
511	                    rel = posixpath.join(posixpath.relpath(dest, WORKSPACE), rel)
512	                self.files[posixpath.normpath(rel)] = fh.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
513	
514	    def snapshot_baseline(self) -> None:
515	        self.baseline = dict(self.files)
516	
517	    def remove(self, rel: str) -> None:
518	        self.files.pop(rel, None)
519	
520	    def rmtree_abs(self, path: str) -> None:
521	        rel = path[len(WORKSPACE) + 1 :] if path.startswith(WORKSPACE + "/") else path
522	        for f in list(self.files):
523	            if f == rel or f.startswith(rel.rstrip("/") + "/"):
524	                del self.files[f]
525	
526	    def terminate(self) -> None:
527	        self.terminated = True
528	
529	    def alive(self) -> bool:
530	        return not self.terminated
531	
532	
533	def local_tar_of(path: str) -> bytes:
534	    """Thin wrapper so callers do not import tarfile."""
535	    return tar_bytes(path)
536	
537	
538	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- provisioning seam
539	
540	#: the *command* sandbox image. Deliberately boring — a plain dev box with python + pytest — so
541	#: nothing inside it hints that failures are being injected one layer up.
542	SANDBOX_APP_NAME = os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_SANDBOX_APP", "faultline-sandboxes")
543	SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_S = int(os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT", "1800"))
544	SANDBOX_IDLE_TIMEOUT_S = int(os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_SANDBOX_IDLE_TIMEOUT", "600"))
545	
546	
547	def sandbox_image():
548	    """Built at deploy time by the `prewarm` function in modal_app.py so reset stays fast."""
549	    import modal
550	
551	    return (
552	        modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.11")
553	        .pip_install("pytest==7.4.4")
554	        .run_commands(
555	            "mkdir -p /workspace /opt",
556	            'ln -sf "$(command -v python3)" /usr/local/bin/python || true',
557	        )
558	        .env({"PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"})
559	    )
560	
561	
562	def create_workspace() -> "Workspace":
563	    """Provision a fresh command sandbox and install the helper. Monkeypatched in tests."""
564	    import modal
565	
566	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
567	    sandbox_app = modal.App.lookup(SANDBOX_APP_NAME, create_if_missing=True)
568	    sb = modal.Sandbox.create(
569	        app=sandbox_app,
570	        image=sandbox_image(),
571	        workdir=WORKSPACE,
572	        timeout=SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_S,
573	        idle_timeout=SANDBOX_IDLE_TIMEOUT_S,
574	        cpu=1,
575	        memory=1024,
576	        block_network=True,  # the agent's shell has no egress at all
577	    )
578	    ws = ModalWorkspace(sb)
579	    log.info(
580	        "sandbox.created", "command sandbox up",
581	        sandbox_id=ws.sandbox_id, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
582	        block_network=True, timeout_s=SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_S, idle_timeout_s=SANDBOX_IDLE_TIMEOUT_S,
583	    )
584	    ws.install_helper()
585	    return ws
586	
587	
588	def open_workspace(sandbox_id: str) -> "Workspace":
589	    """Attach to an existing sandbox by id (api containers are stateless). Patched in tests."""
590	    import modal
591	
592	    try:
593	        return ModalWorkspace(modal.Sandbox.from_id(sandbox_id))
594	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
595	        raise WorkspaceError(f"sandbox {sandbox_id} is unreachable: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
596

1	"""FastAPI surface: the gym REST contract (PLAN.md 2.2) with the MCP server mounted at /mcp.
2	
3	Route map
4	    POST   /episodes                 reset  -> ResetResponse
5	    GET    /episodes                 ops listing (id, scenario, created_at, sandbox alive?)
6	    POST   /episodes/sweep           terminate episodes older than EPISODE_TTL_S
7	    GET    /episodes/{id}            observe -> ObserveResponse
8	    POST   /episodes/{id}/evaluate   grade  -> EvaluateResponse
9	    DELETE /episodes/{id}            terminate the sandbox
10	    GET    /scenarios                public catalogue
11	    GET    /health                   Health (has_provider_key MUST be false here)
12	    POST   /mcp                      streamable-HTTP MCP (episode via X-Faultline-Episode)
13	
14	Two invariants this file is responsible for:
15	
16	* **No provider secret.** This function is deployed without `anthropic-secret`; `/health` reports
17	  `has_provider_key` so the boundary is observable from outside, and startup logs it loudly.
18	* **Every request is logged once** with a request id, status and duration, in the same JSON-lines
19	  shape every other Faultline service uses. The request id flows into the MCP tool logs through
20	  `faultline_common.log.ctx_request_id`, so one id ties an HTTP call to the sandbox exec it caused.
21	
22	Route handlers are plain `def` on purpose: the Modal Sandbox client and `modal.Dict` are blocking,
23	so FastAPI runs them in its threadpool instead of stalling the event loop for every other request.
24	"""
25	
26	from __future__ import annotations
27	
28	import os
29	import time
30	from typing import Any
31	
32	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, Response
33	from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
34	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

36	from faultline_common.log import ctx_episode_id, ctx_request_id, ctx_step, get_logger
37	from faultline_common.schemas import (
38	    EvaluateResponse,

43	    Scenario,

48	from .workspace import WorkspaceError

50	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
51	
52	PROVIDER_KEY_ENV = "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"
53	REQUEST_ID_HEADER = "X-Request-Id"
54	EPISODE_HEADER = "X-Faultline-Episode"
55	MCP_PATH = "/mcp"
56	
57	
58	class StripMcpTrailingSlash:
59	    """Serve `/mcp` and `/mcp/` from the same route, with no redirect either way.
60	
61	    The MCP streamable-HTTP client normalises the endpoint to `/mcp/`, which FastAPI answers with a
62	    307 to `/mcp`. Clients follow it, so everything works — but it doubles the request count on the
63	    hottest path in the system (every single tool call). Rewriting the path in front of the router
64	    costs one string comparison and removes a whole round trip per step.
65	    """
66	
67	    def __init__(self, app):
68	        self.app = app
69	
70	    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
71	        if scope.get("type") == "http" and scope.get("path") == MCP_PATH + "/":
72	            scope = dict(scope, path=MCP_PATH, raw_path=MCP_PATH.encode())
73	        await self.app(scope, receive, send)
74	
75	
76	def has_provider_key() -> bool:
77	    return PROVIDER_KEY_ENV in os.environ
78	
79	
80	def create_app() -> FastAPI:
81	    # stateless_http=True: every MCP request is self-contained, so any container can serve it.
82	    mcp_app = mcp_tools.mcp.http_app(
83	        path=MCP_PATH,
84	        transport="http",
85	        stateless_http=True,
86	        host_origin_protection=False,  # the public URL is the Modal one; no DNS-rebinding guard
87	    )
88	
89	    app = FastAPI(
90	        title="faultline-sandbox-env",
91	        version=__version__,
92	        summary="Fault-injecting gym + MCP tool server over a Modal Sandbox",
93	        lifespan=mcp_app.router.lifespan_context,
94	    )
95	
96	    # ---- middleware (added innermost-first: CORS ends up outermost so preflights never 500)
97	
98	    @app.middleware("http")
99	    async def request_context(request: Request, call_next):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
100	        rid = request.headers.get(REQUEST_ID_HEADER) or new_id("req")
101	        ctx_request_id.set(rid)
102	        ctx_episode_id.set(request.headers.get(EPISODE_HEADER))
103	        ctx_step.set(None)
104	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
105	        # NB: Logger.debug/info already own the `lvl` argument — passing lvl= here is a TypeError
106	        # on every single request.
107	        log.debug("http.request", f"{request.method} {request.url.path}", method=request.method,
108	                  path=request.url.path)
109	        try:
110	            response = await call_next(request)
111	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - log then let FastAPI's handler answer
112	            log.error("http.error", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", method=request.method,
113	                      path=request.url.path, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
114	            raise
115	        response.headers[REQUEST_ID_HEADER] = rid
116	        log.info("http.response", f"{request.method} {request.url.path} -> {response.status_code}",
117	                 method=request.method, path=request.url.path, status=response.status_code,
118	                 dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
119	        return response
120	
121	    app.add_middleware(
122	        CORSMiddleware,
123	        allow_origins=["*"],
124	        allow_credentials=False,
125	        allow_methods=["*"],
126	        allow_headers=["*"],
127	        expose_headers=["*"],
128	    )
129	
130	    # outermost: runs before routing, so /mcp/ never becomes a redirect
131	    app.add_middleware(StripMcpTrailingSlash)
132	
133	    # ---- gym REST
134	
135	    @app.get("/health", response_model=Health)
136	    def health() -> Health:
137	        return Health(
138	            svc="sandbox-env",
139	            ok=True,
140	            version=__version__,
141	            has_provider_key=has_provider_key(),
142	            detail={
143	                "scenarios": [s.id for s in scenarios.list_scenarios()],
144	                "tools": list(mcp_tools.TOOL_NAMES),
145	                "mcp_path": "/mcp",
146	                "episodes_dict": episodes.EPISODES_DICT,
147	                "episode_ttl_s": episodes.EPISODE_TTL_S,
148	            },
149	        )
150	
151	    @app.get("/scenarios", response_model=list[Scenario])
152	    def get_scenarios() -> list[Scenario]:
153	        return scenarios.list_scenarios()
154	
155	    # 200, not 201: PLAN.md 2.2 does not pin a status and two other services are being written
156	    # against this route in parallel, so the least surprising code wins.
157	    @app.post("/episodes", response_model=ResetResponse)
158	    def post_episodes(body: ResetRequest) -> ResetResponse:
159	        try:
160	            return episodes.reset(body.scenario_id, body.seed)
161	        except scenarios.ScenarioNotFound:
162	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown scenario {body.scenario_id!r}") from None
163	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
164	            raise HTTPException(503, f"could not provision a sandbox: {exc}") from None
165	
166	    # Ops route (not part of the gym contract): what is running, how old it is, and whether its
167	    # sandbox is still alive. Cheap answer by default is NOT an option — "alive?" is the whole
168	    # question — so it polls, bounded by `limit`; `?probe=false` gives the Dict view with no RPCs.
169	    @app.get("/episodes")
170	    def list_episodes(probe: bool = True, limit: int = 200) -> dict[str, Any]:
171	        return episodes.list_episodes(probe=probe, limit=max(1, min(limit, 500)))
172	
173	    # Manual trigger for the same sweep that runs on every reset. Handy in a demo, and it makes the
174	    # guardrail testable from outside without waiting for someone to press Run.
175	    @app.post("/episodes/sweep")
176	    def sweep_episodes(ttl_s: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
177	        return episodes.sweep(ttl_s=ttl_s)
178	
179	    @app.get("/episodes/{episode_id}", response_model=ObserveResponse)
180	    def get_episode(episode_id: str) -> ObserveResponse:
181	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
182	        try:
183	            return episodes.observe(episode_id)
184	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
185	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}") from None
186	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
187	            raise HTTPException(503, f"sandbox unavailable: {exc}") from None
188	
189	    @app.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/evaluate", response_model=EvaluateResponse)
190	    def post_evaluate(episode_id: str) -> EvaluateResponse:
191	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
192	        try:
193	            return grader.evaluate(episode_id)
194	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
195	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}") from None
196	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
197	            raise HTTPException(503, f"sandbox unavailable: {exc}") from None
198	
199	    @app.delete("/episodes/{episode_id}")
200	    def delete_episode(episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
201	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
202	        found = episodes.delete(episode_id)
203	        if not found:
204	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}")
205	        return {"terminated": True, "episode_id": episode_id}
206	
207	    @app.get("/")
208	    def index() -> dict[str, Any]:
209	        return {
210	            "svc": "sandbox-env",
211	            "version": __version__,
212	            "mcp": "/mcp",
213	            "routes": ["/health", "/scenarios", "/episodes", "/episodes/sweep", "/episodes/{id}",
214	                       "/episodes/{id}/evaluate"],
215	        }
216	
217	    @app.exception_handler(episodes.EpisodeNotFound)
218	    def _episode_missing(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> Response:  # pragma: no cover
219	        return JSONResponse({"detail": f"unknown episode: {exc}"}, status_code=404)
220	
221	    # MCP last: Mount("/") matches anything the routes above did not.
222	    app.mount("/", mcp_app)
223	
224	    log.info(
225	        "svc.start",
226	        "sandbox-env api ready",
227	        version=__version__,
228	        has_provider_key=has_provider_key(),
229	        scenarios=[s.id for s in scenarios.list_scenarios()],
230	        tools=list(mcp_tools.TOOL_NAMES),
231	    )
232	    if has_provider_key():  # should be impossible: this function is deployed without the secret
233	        log.error("svc.secret_leak", f"{PROVIDER_KEY_ENV} is present in sandbox-env; it must not be")
234	
235	    return app

  "file_path": "/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/services/sandbox-env/sandbox_env/faults.py"

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:32.334Z
1	"""The fault engine. PURE: no I/O, no clock, no randomness — same inputs, same Decision.
2	
3	The whole point of Faultline is that failures are injected *at the tool boundary*, outside the
4	shell the agent drives. Nothing inside the sandbox is patched, chmod-ed or LD_PRELOAD-ed, so the
5	agent cannot detect the injector by inspecting its own filesystem; it just sees an ENOENT, an
6	EACCES or a 504 exactly where a real system would produce one.
7	
8	Classification rules (`touches` / `mutating` / `read`) follow GRADING.md verbatim.
9	"""
10	
11	from __future__ import annotations
12	
13	import re
14	import shlex
15	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
16	from typing import Any, Literal
17	
18	from faultline_common.schemas import FaultKind, FaultMode, FaultPlan, FaultSpec, ToolError
19	
20	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- classification
21	
22	#: GRADING.md "mutating" for run_command, verbatim.
23	MUTATING_RE = re.compile(
24	    r"(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b"
25	    r"|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))"
26	)
27	#: `python … -c` whose body contains open(...) in a write/append mode.
28	_PY_DASH_C_RE = re.compile(r"\bpython[0-9.]*\b[^|;&]*?\s-c\b", re.S)
29	_PY_WRITE_MODE_RE = re.compile(r"""['"][wax][bt+]*['"]""")
30	
31	#: GRADING.md "read" verbs for run_command (only consulted when the call is not mutating).
32	READ_VERB_RE = re.compile(
33	    r"\b(cat|head|tail|grep|egrep|fgrep|rg|wc|diff|less|more|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|ls|stat|find"
34	    r"|file|nl|od|awk|sed|pytest|python[0-9.]*)\b"
35	)
36	#: Commands that only *enumerate* a directory. These never consume a transient missing_file hit —
37	#: we filter the vanished name out of the output instead, so the illusion stays consistent.
38	LISTING_VERB_RE = re.compile(r"\b(ls|find|tree|dir)\b")
39	_CONTENT_READ_RE = re.compile(r"\b(cat|head|tail|grep|egrep|fgrep|rg|wc|diff|less|more|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|nl|od|awk|sed|pytest)\b")
40	
41	_QUOTES = "\"'`"
42	_SPLIT_CHARS = re.compile(r"""[=(),;|&<>\[\]{}'"`]+""")
43	
44	
45	def normalize_token(tok: str) -> str:
46	    """Strip quotes, a leading ./ and a leading /workspace/ — GRADING.md token normalisation."""
47	    t = str(tok).strip()
48	    while len(t) >= 2 and t[0] in _QUOTES and t[-1] == t[0]:
49	        t = t[1:-1]
50	    t = t.strip(_QUOTES)
51	    if t == "/workspace":
52	        return "."
53	    if t.startswith("/workspace/"):
54	        t = t[len("/workspace/") :]
55	    while t.startswith("./"):
56	        t = t[2:]
57	    if len(t) > 1 and t.endswith("/"):
58	        t = t.rstrip("/")
59	    return t
60	
61	
62	def command_tokens(command: str) -> list[str]:
63	    """Argv tokens of a shell command, plus path-ish fragments embedded in quoted arguments.
64	
65	    shlex gives us the argv the agent meant. The extra pass exists for one real case:
66	    `python -c 'open("config/settings.json", "w")'` is a single argv token, and GRADING.md's
67	    `mutating` rule explicitly expects that form to be attributable to a path.
68	    """
69	    try:
70	        toks = shlex.split(command, posix=True)
71	    except ValueError:
72	        toks = [t for t in re.split(r"\s+", command) if t]
73	    out: list[str] = []
74	    for t in toks:
75	        out.append(t)
76	        if _SPLIT_CHARS.search(t):
77	            out.extend(p for p in _SPLIT_CHARS.split(t) if p)
78	    # quoted fragments of the *raw* command (shlex already consumed the outer quoting)
79	    out.extend(re.findall(r"""['"]([^'"\n]+)['"]""", command))
80	    return out
81	
82	
83	def is_mutating(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
84	    """GRADING.md `mutating`: write_file always; run_command by verb; reads never."""
85	    if tool == "write_file":
86	        return True
87	    if tool != "run_command":
88	        return False
89	    cmd = str(args.get("command", "") or "")
90	    if MUTATING_RE.search(cmd):
91	        return True
92	    m = _PY_DASH_C_RE.search(cmd)
93	    if m:
94	        body = cmd[m.end() :]
95	        if "open(" in body and _PY_WRITE_MODE_RE.search(body):
96	            return True
97	    return False
98	
99	
100	def is_listing(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
101	    """True for calls that only enumerate a directory (list_dir, `ls`, `find`)."""
102	    if tool == "list_dir":
103	        return True
104	    if tool != "run_command":
105	        return False
106	    cmd = str(args.get("command", "") or "")
107	    if is_mutating(tool, args):
108	        return False
109	    return bool(LISTING_VERB_RE.search(cmd)) and not _CONTENT_READ_RE.search(cmd)
110	
111	
112	def is_read(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
113	    """GRADING.md `read`: not mutating, and a tool/verb that inspects content or a listing."""
114	    if is_mutating(tool, args):
115	        return False
116	    if tool in ("read_file", "list_dir"):
117	        return True
118	    if tool == "run_command":
119	        return bool(READ_VERB_RE.search(str(args.get("command", "") or "")))
120	    return False
121	
122	
123	def touches(path: str, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
124	    """GRADING.md `touches(path)`: the entry's own path, or any argv token that normalises to it."""
125	    target = normalize_token(path)
126	    if tool in ("read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"):
127	        return normalize_token(args.get("path") or ".") == target
128	    if tool == "run_command":
129	        return any(normalize_token(t) == target for t in command_tokens(str(args.get("command", "") or "")))
130	    return False
131	
132	
133	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- error texts
134	
135	def enoent_error(path: str, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> ToolError:
136	    """`cat: config/settings.json: No such file or directory` — indistinguishable from the real thing."""
137	    if tool == "run_command":
138	        argv = command_tokens(str((args or {}).get("command", "") or ""))
139	        prog = (argv[0].rsplit("/", 1)[-1] if argv else "cat") or "cat"
140	        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.\-]+", prog):
141	            prog = "cat"
142	    else:
143	        prog = tool
144	    return ToolError(error=f"{prog}: {path}: No such file or directory", code="ENOENT", path=path)
145	
146	
147	def eacces_error(path: str, tool: str) -> ToolError:
148	    prog = "bash" if tool == "run_command" else tool
149	    return ToolError(error=f"{prog}: {path}: Permission denied", code="EACCES", path=path)
150	
151	
152	def etimedout_error(path: str, delay_ms: int) -> ToolError:
153	    return ToolError(
154	        error=(
155	            f"504 Gateway Timeout: no response from sandbox after {delay_ms}ms; "
156	            "the operation may or may not have completed"
157	        ),
158	        code="ETIMEDOUT",
159	        path=path,
160	    )
161	
162	
163	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- decision
164	
165	@dataclass
166	class Decision:
167	    """What the tool boundary should do with this call.
168	
169	    kind=None            -> nothing injected, execute normally.
170	    short_circuit set    -> do NOT execute; surface this error (run_command surfaces it as a
171	                            non-zero exit, the typed tools as an is_error ToolError).
172	    post_exec="ack_lost" -> execute fully, sleep delay_ms, THEN fail with ETIMEDOUT.
173	    """
174	
175	    kind: FaultKind | None = None
176	    fault_index: int = -1
177	    short_circuit: ToolError | None = None
178	    post_exec: Literal["ack_lost"] | None = None
179	    delay_ms: int = 0
180	    mode: FaultMode | None = None
181	    path: str | None = None
182	    hidden_paths: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
183	
184	    @property
185	    def fired(self) -> bool:
186	        return self.kind is not None
187	
188	
189	def _as_plan(plan: FaultPlan | dict[str, Any] | None) -> FaultPlan:
190	    if plan is None:
191	        return FaultPlan()
192	    if isinstance(plan, FaultPlan):
193	        return plan
194	    return FaultPlan.model_validate(plan)
195	
196	
197	def _remaining(hits_remaining: list[int | None] | None, idx: int, spec: FaultSpec) -> int | None:
198	    if hits_remaining is not None and idx < len(hits_remaining):
199	        return hits_remaining[idx]
200	    return spec.hits
201	
202	
203	def hidden_paths(plan: FaultPlan | dict[str, Any] | None, hits_remaining: list[int | None] | None) -> list[str]:
204	    """Paths a *transient* missing_file fault is still hiding.
205	
206	    Directory listings must agree with the reads: while `README.md` is pretending to be gone,
207	    `ls` and `list_dir` on its parent must not show it. Listings never consume a hit.
208	    """
209	    out: list[str] = []
210	    for i, f in enumerate(_as_plan(plan).faults):
211	        if f.kind != "missing_file" or f.mode != "transient":
212	            continue
213	        rem = _remaining(hits_remaining, i, f)
214	        if rem is None or rem > 0:
215	            out.append(normalize_token(f.path))
216	    return out
217	
218	
219	def decide(
220	    plan: FaultPlan | dict[str, Any] | None,
221	    hits_remaining: list[int | None] | None,
222	    tool: str,
223	    args: dict[str, Any],
224	) -> Decision:
225	    """Pick at most one fault for this call, in plan order. Does NOT mutate hits_remaining.
226	
227	    The caller decrements `hits_remaining[decision.fault_index]` once the decision is acted on.
228	    """
229	    p = _as_plan(plan)
230	    hidden = hidden_paths(p, hits_remaining)
231	    mutating = is_mutating(tool, args)
232	    listing = is_listing(tool, args)
233	
234	    for i, f in enumerate(p.faults):
235	        rem = _remaining(hits_remaining, i, f)
236	        if rem is not None and rem <= 0:
237	            continue  # lifted
238	        if f.kind == "missing_file" and f.mode == "sticky":
239	            continue  # realised at reset (the file is really gone); nothing to intercept
240	        if not touches(f.path, tool, args):
241	            continue
242	        if f.kind in ("denied_write", "ack_lost") and not mutating:
243	            continue  # write faults only bite writes
244	        if f.kind == "missing_file" and listing and not mutating:
245	            continue  # handled by hiding the name, without burning a hit
246	        norm = normalize_token(f.path)
247	        if f.kind == "missing_file":
248	            return Decision(
249	                kind="missing_file",
250	                fault_index=i,
251	                short_circuit=enoent_error(norm, tool, args),
252	                mode=f.mode,
253	                path=norm,
254	                hidden_paths=hidden,
255	            )
256	        if f.kind == "denied_write":
257	            return Decision(
258	                kind="denied_write",
259	                fault_index=i,
260	                short_circuit=eacces_error(norm, tool),
261	                mode=f.mode,
262	                path=norm,
263	                hidden_paths=hidden,
264	            )
265	        if f.kind == "ack_lost":
266	            return Decision(
267	                kind="ack_lost",
268	                fault_index=i,
269	                post_exec="ack_lost",
270	                delay_ms=int(f.delay_ms),
271	                mode=f.mode,
272	                path=norm,
273	                hidden_paths=hidden,
274	            )
275	    return Decision(hidden_paths=hidden)
276	
277	
278	def initial_hits(plan: FaultPlan | dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[int | None]:
279	    """Starting `hits` budget per fault, parallel to plan.faults. None = unlimited."""
280	    return [f.hits for f in _as_plan(plan).faults]
281	
282	
283	def consume(hits_remaining: list[int | None], idx: int) -> list[int | None]:
284	    """Decrement one fault's remaining hits (None stays None). Returns the same list."""
285	    if 0 <= idx < len(hits_remaining) and hits_remaining[idx] is not None:
286	        hits_remaining[idx] = max(0, int(hits_remaining[idx]) - 1)
287	    return hits_remaining
288

⚙ Read
{
  "file_path": "/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/services/sandbox-env/sandbox_env/mcp_tools.py"
