Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:33.024Z
1	"""The MCP tool surface — the gym's `step()`.
2	
3	Four tools (`run_command`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `list_dir`) over streamable HTTP. Three
4	properties matter more than the tools themselves:
5	
6	1. **The episode is not a tool argument.** It comes from the `X-Faultline-Episode` request header,
7	   so a model cannot address another run's sandbox by guessing an id, and cannot see the id in its
8	   own tool schema. No header -> ENOEPISODE.
9	2. **Faults are injected here, not in the shell.** `faults.decide()` runs before the sandbox is
10	   touched; a short-circuited call never reaches the sandbox at all, and an `ack_lost` call reaches
11	   it *fully* and then loses its acknowledgement. Nothing inside the sandbox is patched.
12	3. **Every call lands in the ledger** before it returns — including the ones that fail — because
13	   the ledger is what the grader scores recovery from.
14	
15	Error shape: real and injected failures are indistinguishable. A failing typed tool raises
16	`fastmcp.exceptions.ToolError` carrying the `ToolError` JSON, so the client sees `is_error=True`
17	with a machine-readable body. A `run_command` fault instead comes back as a normal result with a
18	non-zero `exit_code` and an OS-style stderr, because that is what a shell would really do.
19	"""
20	
21	from __future__ import annotations
22	
23	import asyncio
24	import time
25	import traceback
26	from typing import Any, NoReturn
27	
28	from fastmcp import FastMCP
29	from fastmcp.exceptions import ToolError as MCPToolError
30	from fastmcp.server.dependencies import get_http_headers
31	
32	from faultline_common.log import ctx_episode_id, ctx_step, get_logger
33	from faultline_common.schemas import (
34	    CONTENT_CAP,
35	    DirEntry,
36	    FaultFired,
37	    LedgerEntry,
38	    ListDirOutput,
39	    ReadFileOutput,
40	    RunCommandOutput,
41	    ToolError,
42	    WriteFileOutput,
43	)
44	
45	from . import episodes, faults
46	from .paths import PathError, basename_of, normalize_rel, parent_of
47	from .util import args_digest, drop_lines_mentioning, now_iso, truncate_content, truncate_stdio
48	from .workspace import Workspace, WorkspaceError
49	
50	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
51	
52	#: lookup key (fastmcp lowercases request headers) and the canonical spelling we echo to humans
53	EPISODE_HEADER = "x-faultline-episode"
54	EPISODE_HEADER_DISPLAY = "X-Faultline-Episode"
55	
56	mcp = FastMCP(
57	    "faultline-sandbox-env",
58	    instructions=(
59	        "Shell and file tools for a small Python repo mounted at /workspace. Paths are workspace-"
60	        "relative. Tool calls can fail or time out the way real infrastructure does: check what "
61	        "actually landed on disk before retrying a write."
62	    ),
63	)
64	
65	
66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- error plumbing
67	
68	
69	def _fail(err: ToolError) -> NoReturn:
70	    """Surface a typed failure to the client as is_error=True with a JSON body."""
71	    raise MCPToolError(err.model_dump_json())
72	
73	
74	def _episode_id_from_headers() -> str:
75	    headers = get_http_headers() or {}
76	    ep_id = (headers.get(EPISODE_HEADER) or "").strip()
77	    if not ep_id:
78	        _fail(
79	            ToolError(
80	                error="no episode selected: set the X-Faultline-Episode request header",
81	                code="ENOEPISODE",
82	                detail=f"the harness must send {EPISODE_HEADER_DISPLAY} on every MCP request",
83	            )
84	        )
85	    return ep_id
86	
87	
88	def _safe_rel(path: str | None, tool: str) -> str:
89	    try:
90	        return normalize_rel(path)
91	    except PathError as exc:
92	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"{tool}: {exc.path}: {exc.reason}", code="EINVAL", path=str(path)))
93	
94	
95	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the step machinery
96	
97	
98	class _Step:
99	    """One tool call: load episode -> consult the fault plan -> maybe exec -> record the ledger."""
100	
101	    def __init__(self, episode_id: str, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]):
102	        self.episode_id = episode_id
103	        self.tool = tool
104	        self.args = args
105	        try:
106	            self.ep = episodes.load(episode_id)
107	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
108	            _fail(
109	                ToolError(
110	                    error=f"unknown episode {episode_id}",
111	                    code="ENOEPISODE",
112	                    detail="reset an episode with POST /episodes first",
113	                )
114	            )
115	        if self.ep.get("terminated"):
116	            _fail(ToolError(error=f"episode {episode_id} has been terminated", code="EINVAL"))
117	
118	        self.ep["step"] = int(self.ep.get("step", 0)) + 1
119	        self.step = self.ep["step"]
120	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
121	        ctx_step.set(self.step)
122	
123	        self.plan = episodes.fault_plan_of(self.ep)
124	        self.hits: list[int | None] = list(self.ep.get("fault_hits") or faults.initial_hits(self.plan))
125	        self.decision = faults.decide(self.plan, self.hits, tool, args)
126	        self.mutating = faults.is_mutating(tool, args)
127	        self.t0 = time.perf_counter()
128	        log.info(
129	            "tool.call",
130	            f"{tool} step {self.step}",
131	            tool=tool,
132	            mutating=self.mutating or None,
133	            fault=self.decision.kind,
134	            args=str(args)[:300],
135	        )
136	
137	    # -- bookkeeping -------------------------------------------------------
138	
139	    @property
140	    def elapsed_ms(self) -> int:
141	        return int((time.perf_counter() - self.t0) * 1000)
142	
143	    def workspace(self) -> Workspace:
144	        try:
145	            return episodes.workspace_for(self.ep)
146	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
147	            self.record("error")
148	            _fail(ToolError(error=f"{self.tool}: sandbox unavailable", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
149	
150	    def record(self, outcome: str, *, exit_code: int | None = None, fault: bool = False) -> None:
151	        """Append the ledger entry and persist. Always called exactly once per tool call."""
152	        fired = None
153	        if fault and self.decision.fired:
154	            fired = FaultFired(
155	                step=self.step,
156	                kind=self.decision.kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
157	                path=self.decision.path or "",
158	                mode=self.decision.mode or "transient",
159	            )
160	            faults.consume(self.hits, self.decision.fault_index)
161	        entry = LedgerEntry(
162	            step=self.step,
163	            ts=now_iso(),
164	            tool=self.tool,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
165	            args_digest=args_digest(self.args),
166	            path=None if self.tool == "run_command" else self.args.get("path"),
167	            command=self.args.get("command") if self.tool == "run_command" else None,
168	            mutating=self.mutating,
169	            fault=fired,
170	            outcome=outcome,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
171	            exit_code=exit_code,
172	            duration_ms=self.elapsed_ms,
173	        )
174	        self.ep["fault_hits"] = self.hits
175	        episodes.append_ledger(self.ep, entry)
176	        episodes.save(self.ep)
177	        log.info(
178	            "tool.result",
179	            f"{self.tool} -> {outcome}",
180	            tool=self.tool,
181	            outcome=outcome,
182	            exit_code=exit_code,
183	            fault=fired.kind if fired else None,
184	            fault_path=fired.path if fired else None,
185	            fault_mode=fired.mode if fired else None,
186	            dur_ms=entry.duration_ms,
187	        )
188	
189	    def fault_error(self) -> NoReturn:
190	        """Short-circuit: record the fault and raise it as the tool's error."""
191	        err = self.decision.short_circuit
192	        assert err is not None
193	        self.record("short_circuit", fault=True)
194	        _fail(err)
195	
196	    def ack_lost(self, exit_code: int | None = None) -> NoReturn:
197	        """The write landed. Hold the response, then lose it — the agent cannot know which."""
198	        delay_ms = max(0, int(self.decision.delay_ms))
199	        log.warn(
200	            "fault.ack_lost",
201	            "write applied; withholding acknowledgement",
202	            tool=self.tool,
203	            path=self.decision.path,
204	            delay_ms=delay_ms,
205	        )
206	        time.sleep(delay_ms / 1000.0)
207	        self.record("ack_lost", exit_code=exit_code, fault=True)
208	        _fail(faults.etimedout_error(self.decision.path or "", delay_ms))
209	
210	
211	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- tool implementations
212	
213	
214	def run_command_sync(episode_id: str, command: str, timeout_s: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
215	    args = {"command": command, "timeout_s": timeout_s}
216	    st = _Step(episode_id, "run_command", args)
217	
218	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
219	        # A shell does not raise; it prints to stderr and exits non-zero. Keep the illusion.
220	        err = st.decision.short_circuit
221	        st.record("short_circuit", exit_code=1, fault=True)
222	        return RunCommandOutput(
223	            stdout="", stderr=err.error + "\n", exit_code=1, duration_ms=st.elapsed_ms, truncated=False
224	        ).model_dump()
225	
226	    ws = st.workspace()
227	    try:
228	        res = ws.run(command, timeout_s=timeout_s)
229	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
230	        st.record("error")
231	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"run_command: sandbox error", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
232	
233	    stdout = res.stdout
234	    if st.decision.hidden_paths and faults.is_listing("run_command", args):
235	        stdout = drop_lines_mentioning(stdout, st.decision.hidden_paths)
236	    stdout, t1 = truncate_stdio(stdout)
237	    stderr, t2 = truncate_stdio(res.stderr)
238	
239	    if st.decision.post_exec == "ack_lost":
240	        st.ack_lost(exit_code=res.exit_code)
241	    if res.timed_out:
242	        st.record("error", exit_code=res.exit_code)
243	        _fail(
244	            ToolError(
245	                error=f"run_command: command timed out after {timeout_s}s",
246	                code="ETIMEDOUT",
247	                detail=stderr[:400],
248	            )
249	        )
250	    st.record("ok", exit_code=res.exit_code)
251	    return RunCommandOutput(
252	        stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, exit_code=res.exit_code, duration_ms=res.duration_ms, truncated=t1 or t2
253	    ).model_dump()
254	
255	
256	def read_file_sync(episode_id: str, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
257	    rel = _safe_rel(path, "read_file")
258	    st = _Step(episode_id, "read_file", {"path": rel})
259	
260	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
261	        st.fault_error()
262	
263	    ws = st.workspace()
264	    try:
265	        res = ws.read(rel, CONTENT_CAP)
266	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
267	        st.record("error")
268	        _fail(ToolError(error="read_file: sandbox error", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
269	
270	    if res.kind == "missing":
271	        st.record("error")
272	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"read_file: {rel}: No such file or directory", code="ENOENT", path=rel))
273	    if res.kind == "dir":
274	        st.record("error")
275	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"read_file: {rel}: Is a directory", code="EINVAL", path=rel))
276	    if res.kind == "denied":
277	        st.record("error")
278	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"read_file: {rel}: Permission denied", code="EACCES", path=rel))
279	
280	    content, truncated = truncate_content(res.content)
281	    st.record("ok")
282	    return ReadFileOutput(
283	        path=rel, content=content, size=res.size, sha256=res.sha256, truncated=truncated or res.truncated
284	    ).model_dump()
285	
286	
287	def write_file_sync(episode_id: str, path: str, content: str, mode: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
288	    rel = _safe_rel(path, "write_file")
289	    if mode not in ("overwrite", "append"):
290	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"write_file: mode must be 'overwrite' or 'append', got {mode!r}", code="EINVAL"))
291	    st = _Step(episode_id, "write_file", {"path": rel, "content": content, "mode": mode})
292	
293	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
294	        st.fault_error()
295	
296	    ws = st.workspace()
297	    try:
298	        res = ws.write(rel, content, append=(mode == "append"))
299	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
300	        st.record("error")
301	        _fail(ToolError(error="write_file: sandbox error", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
302	
303	    if res.kind == "denied":
304	        st.record("error")
305	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"write_file: {rel}: Permission denied", code="EACCES", path=rel))
306	    if res.kind == "isdir":
307	        st.record("error")
308	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"write_file: {rel}: Is a directory", code="EINVAL", path=rel))
309	
310	    # the write really happened; only now do we decide whether the agent gets to hear about it
311	    if st.decision.post_exec == "ack_lost":
312	        st.ack_lost()
313	
314	    st.record("ok")
315	    return WriteFileOutput(path=rel, bytes_written=res.bytes_written, sha256=res.sha256).model_dump()
316	
317	
318	def list_dir_sync(episode_id: str, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
319	    rel = _safe_rel(path, "list_dir")
320	    st = _Step(episode_id, "list_dir", {"path": rel})
321	
322	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
323	        st.fault_error()
324	
325	    ws = st.workspace()
326	    try:
327	        res = ws.listdir(rel)
328	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
329	        st.record("error")
330	        _fail(ToolError(error="list_dir: sandbox error", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
331	
332	    if res.kind == "missing":
333	        st.record("error")
334	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"list_dir: {rel}: No such file or directory", code="ENOENT", path=rel))
335	    if res.kind == "notdir":
336	        st.record("error")
337	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"list_dir: {rel}: Not a directory", code="EINVAL", path=rel))
338	
339	    # a file that read_file is currently pretending is gone must not show up in its own directory
340	    hidden = {basename_of(h) for h in st.decision.hidden_paths if parent_of(h) == rel}
341	    entries = [DirEntry(name=e.name, type=e.type, size=e.size) for e in res.entries if e.name not in hidden]
342	    st.record("ok")
343	    return ListDirOutput(path=rel, entries=entries).model_dump()
344	
345	
346	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tools
347	
348	
349	async def _dispatch(fn, *args: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
350	    """Run a blocking step off the event loop, and never let a bare exception escape.
351	
352	    A raw exception would reach the client as an opaque 'Error executing tool', which would hide
353	    an injector bug behind something that looks like a model mistake.
354	    """
355	    try:
356	        return await asyncio.to_thread(fn, *args)
357	    except MCPToolError:
358	        raise
359	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
360	        log.error("tool.crash", f"{fn.__name__} raised", err=type(exc).__name__, tb=traceback.format_exc()[-1500:])
361	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"{fn.__name__.removesuffix('_sync')}: internal error", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
362	
363	
364	@mcp.tool(
365	    name="run_command",
366	    description=(
367	        "Run a shell command with `bash -lc` in /workspace (PYTHONPATH=/workspace/src). Returns "
368	        "stdout, stderr, exit_code and duration_ms; output over 8000 chars per stream is elided in "
369	        "the middle and `truncated` is set. A non-zero exit_code is a normal result, not an error."
370	    ),
371	)
372	async def run_command(command: str, timeout_s: int = 30) -> dict:
373	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
374	    timeout_s = max(1, min(60, int(timeout_s)))
375	    return await _dispatch(run_command_sync, ep_id, command, timeout_s)
376	
377	
378	@mcp.tool(
379	    name="read_file",
380	    description=(
381	        "Read a UTF-8 text file by workspace-relative path. Returns content, size and sha256 "
382	        "(content over 32000 chars is elided in the middle with `truncated` set). Errors with "
383	        "ENOENT if the file is not there."
384	    ),
385	)
386	async def read_file(path: str) -> dict:
387	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
388	    return await _dispatch(read_file_sync, ep_id, path)
389	
390	
391	@mcp.tool(
392	    name="write_file",
393	    description=(
394	        "Write a file by workspace-relative path. mode='overwrite' replaces it, mode='append' adds "
395	        "to the end. Returns bytes_written and the sha256 of the whole file after the write, so "
396	        "you can confirm what landed."
397	    ),
398	)
399	async def write_file(path: str, content: str, mode: str = "overwrite") -> dict:
400	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
401	    return await _dispatch(write_file_sync, ep_id, path, content, mode)
402	
403	
404	@mcp.tool(
405	    name="list_dir",
406	    description="List one directory by workspace-relative path ('.' is the repo root).",
407	)
408	async def list_dir(path: str = ".") -> dict:
409	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
410	    return await _dispatch(list_dir_sync, ep_id, path)

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:55.235Z
1	"""Grading: hidden tests + recovery checks, exactly as specified in GRADING.md.
2	
3	    score = 60 x tests_pass + 40 x SUM(ok_i * w_i) / SUM(w_i)
4	
5	Two independent sources of truth, on purpose:
6	
7	* **the workspace** answers "is the repo actually correct" — the hidden tests are uploaded into
8	  `/workspace/.faultline_eval`, run together with the visible suite, and deleted again, so they are
9	  never present while the agent is working and cannot be read, edited or gamed;
10	* **the ledger** answers "did the agent behave well when things broke" — did it retry a flaky read
11	  instead of guessing, did it stop hammering a denied write, did it read a file back after a lost
12	  acknowledgement before writing it again.
13	
14	The second half is the interesting one: a run can pass every test and still score badly because it
15	recovered by luck (e.g. appended the changelog twice and got away with it), and that difference is
16	precisely what this environment is built to measure.
17	"""
18	
19	from __future__ import annotations
20	
21	import json
22	import re
23	import time
24	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
25	from typing import Any, Callable
26	
27	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
28	from faultline_common.schemas import (
29	    CONTENT_CAP,
30	    Check,
31	    EvaluateResponse,
32	    LedgerEntry,
33	    TestsResult,
34	)
35	
36	from . import episodes, faults, scenarios
37	from .paths import EVAL_DIR, WORKSPACE, abs_path
38	from .scenarios import CheckSpec, ScenarioBundle
39	from .util import truncate_stdio
40	from .workspace import Workspace, WorkspaceError, tar_bytes
41	
42	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
43	
44	CONFIG_PATH = "config/settings.json"
45	README_PATH = "README.md"
46	LIMITS_PATH = "src/ratelimiter/limits.py"
47	CHANGELOG_PATH = "CHANGELOG.md"
48	VERSION_PATH = "src/ratelimiter/version.py"
49	
50	#: `-o addopts=` is load-bearing. The fixture's own pytest.ini already sets `addopts = -q`, so the
51	#: `-q` GRADING.md asks for would stack into `-qq`, and at that verbosity pytest prints the progress
52	#: dots and the FAILED lines but NO "N passed, N failed" summary at all — the grader would then read
53	#: every run, passing or failing, as "the suite did not run". Clearing addopts keeps the effective
54	#: command exactly `pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider <paths>` while restoring the summary line.
55	_PYTEST = "python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider -o addopts="
56	PYTEST_CMD = f"cd {WORKSPACE} && PYTHONPATH={WORKSPACE}/src {_PYTEST} tests {EVAL_DIR}"
57	PYTEST_TIMEOUT_S = 120
58	
59	FAULT_NEVER_FIRED = "fault never triggered"
60	
61	_SUMMARY_RE = re.compile(r"(\d+)\s+(passed|failed|errors?|skipped|xfailed|xpassed)\b")
62	_V020_RE = re.compile(r"^## \[0\.2\.0\]", re.M)
63	_VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"""__version__\s*=\s*['"]0\.2\.0['"]""")
64	
65	
66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- ledger predicates
67	# GRADING.md "Ledger semantics used by checks", implemented against LedgerEntry.
68	
69	def entry_touches(entry: LedgerEntry, path: str) -> bool:
70	    if entry.tool == "run_command":
71	        return faults.touches(path, "run_command", {"command": entry.command or ""})
72	    return faults.normalize_token(entry.path or "") == faults.normalize_token(path)
73	
74	
75	def entry_is_read(entry: LedgerEntry) -> bool:
76	    if entry.mutating:
77	        return False
78	    if entry.tool in ("read_file", "list_dir"):
79	        return True
80	    return faults.is_read("run_command", {"command": entry.command or ""})
81	
82	
83	def entry_is_failing(entry: LedgerEntry) -> bool:
84	    return entry.outcome in ("error", "short_circuit", "ack_lost")
85	
86	
87	def entry_mentions_pytest(entry: LedgerEntry) -> bool:
88	    return entry.tool == "run_command" and bool(re.search(r"\bpytest\b", entry.command or ""))
89	
90	
91	def find_fault(entries: list[LedgerEntry], kind: str, path: str,
92	               outcome: str | None = None) -> int | None:
93	    """Index of the first ledger entry where `kind` fired on `path` (-> None if it never did)."""
94	    for i, e in enumerate(entries):
95	        if not e.fault or e.fault.kind != kind:
96	            continue
97	        if faults.normalize_token(e.fault.path) != faults.normalize_token(path):
98	            continue
99	        if outcome is not None and e.outcome != outcome:
100	            continue
101	        return i
102	    return None
103	
104	
105	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- check context
106	
107	
108	@dataclass
109	class CheckContext:
110	    entries: list[LedgerEntry]
111	    ws: Workspace
112	    _cache: dict[str, str | None] = field(default_factory=dict)
113	
114	    def text(self, rel: str) -> str | None:
115	        """File contents from the sandbox, or None if it is not there. Cached per evaluation."""
116	        if rel not in self._cache:
117	            try:
118	                res = self.ws.read(rel, CONTENT_CAP)
119	            except WorkspaceError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - sandbox died mid-grade
120	                log.warn("grader.read_failed", str(exc), path=rel)
121	                self._cache[rel] = None
122	            else:
123	                self._cache[rel] = res.content if res.kind == "file" else None
124	        return self._cache[rel]
125	
126	
127	CheckFn = Callable[[CheckContext], tuple[bool, str]]
128	
129	
130	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the checks
131	
132	
133	def check_config_valid(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
134	    raw = ctx.text(CONFIG_PATH)
135	    if raw is None:
136	        return False, f"{CONFIG_PATH} does not exist"
137	    try:
138	        data = json.loads(raw)
139	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
140	        return False, f"{CONFIG_PATH} is not valid JSON: {exc}"
141	    wanted = {"capacity": 10, "refill_per_sec": 2.0, "burst_multiplier": 1.5}
142	    bad = []
143	    for key, want in wanted.items():
144	        if key not in data:
145	            bad.append(f"missing {key}")
146	        elif float(data[key]) != float(want):
147	            bad.append(f"{key}={data[key]!r} (want {want!r})")
148	    if bad:
149	        return False, "; ".join(bad)
150	    return True, "config/settings.json restored with the documented defaults"
151	
152	
153	def check_retried_transient_read(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
154	    idx = find_fault(ctx.entries, "missing_file", README_PATH, outcome="short_circuit")
155	    if idx is None:
156	        return False, FAULT_NEVER_FIRED
157	    for e in ctx.entries[idx + 1 :]:
158	        if e.outcome == "ok" and entry_is_read(e) and entry_touches(e, README_PATH):
159	            return True, f"ENOENT at step {ctx.entries[idx].step}, read again at step {e.step}"
160	    return False, f"ENOENT on {README_PATH} at step {ctx.entries[idx].step} was never retried"
161	
162	
163	def check_no_thrash(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
164	    run_len = 0
165	    worst = 0
166	    worst_digest = ""
167	    prev = None
168	    for e in ctx.entries:
169	        if entry_is_failing(e) and e.args_digest == prev:
170	            run_len += 1
171	        elif entry_is_failing(e):
172	            run_len = 1
173	        else:
174	            run_len = 0
175	        prev = e.args_digest if entry_is_failing(e) else None
176	        if run_len > worst:
177	            worst, worst_digest = run_len, e.args_digest
178	    if worst > 3:
179	        return False, f"{worst} consecutive identical failing calls (args {worst_digest})"
180	    return True, f"longest identical failing streak: {worst}"
181	
182	
183	def check_write_eventually_succeeded(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
184	    idx = find_fault(ctx.entries, "denied_write", LIMITS_PATH, outcome="short_circuit")
185	    if idx is None:
186	        return False, FAULT_NEVER_FIRED
187	    for e in ctx.entries[idx + 1 :]:
188	        if e.mutating and e.outcome == "ok" and entry_touches(e, LIMITS_PATH):
189	            return True, f"EACCES at step {ctx.entries[idx].step}, write landed at step {e.step}"
190	    return False, f"no successful write to {LIMITS_PATH} after the EACCES"
191	
192	
193	def check_bounded_retries(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
194	    attempts = [e for e in ctx.entries if e.mutating and entry_touches(e, LIMITS_PATH)]
195	    n = len(attempts)
196	    if n > 4:
197	        return False, f"{n} write attempts on {LIMITS_PATH} (budget 4)"
198	    return True, f"{n} write attempts on {LIMITS_PATH}"
199	
200	
201	def check_verified_after_fix(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
202	    first_ok = None
203	    for i, e in enumerate(ctx.entries):
204	        if e.mutating and e.outcome == "ok" and entry_touches(e, LIMITS_PATH):
205	            first_ok = i
206	            break
207	    if first_ok is None:
208	        return False, f"no successful write to {LIMITS_PATH}"
209	    for e in ctx.entries[first_ok + 1 :]:
210	        if e.outcome != "ok":
211	            continue
212	        if entry_mentions_pytest(e):
213	            return True, f"re-ran pytest at step {e.step}"
214	        if entry_is_read(e) and entry_touches(e, LIMITS_PATH):
215	            return True, f"re-read {LIMITS_PATH} at step {e.step}"
216	    return False, "the fix was never verified (no re-read and no pytest run afterwards)"
217	
218	
219	def check_verified_before_rewrite(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
220	    idx = find_fault(ctx.entries, "ack_lost", CHANGELOG_PATH)
221	    if idx is None:
222	        return False, FAULT_NEVER_FIRED
223	    after = ctx.entries[idx + 1 :]
224	    reads = [e for e in after if e.outcome == "ok" and entry_is_read(e) and entry_touches(e, CHANGELOG_PATH)]
225	    writes = [e for e in after if e.mutating and entry_touches(e, CHANGELOG_PATH)]
226	    if not writes:
227	        if reads:
228	            return True, f"verified {CHANGELOG_PATH} at step {reads[0].step} and did not rewrite it"
229	        return False, f"never read {CHANGELOG_PATH} back after the lost acknowledgement"
230	    if not reads:
231	        return False, f"rewrote {CHANGELOG_PATH} at step {writes[0].step} without reading it back"
232	    if reads[0].step < writes[0].step:
233	        return True, f"read at step {reads[0].step} before rewriting at step {writes[0].step}"
234	    return False, f"rewrote {CHANGELOG_PATH} at step {writes[0].step} before verifying it"
235	
236	
237	def check_no_duplicate_entry(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
238	    text = ctx.text(CHANGELOG_PATH)
239	    if text is None:
240	        return False, f"{CHANGELOG_PATH} does not exist"
241	    n = len(_V020_RE.findall(text))
242	    if n == 1:
243	        return True, "exactly one '## [0.2.0]' section"
244	    return False, f"found {n} '## [0.2.0]' headings (want exactly 1)"
245	
246	
247	def check_version_bumped(ctx: CheckContext) -> tuple[bool, str]:
248	    text = ctx.text(VERSION_PATH)
249	    if text is None:
250	        return False, f"{VERSION_PATH} does not exist"
251	    if _VERSION_RE.search(text):
252	        return True, '__version__ == "0.2.0"'
253	    return False, f"{VERSION_PATH} does not set __version__ to 0.2.0"
254	
255	
256	CHECKS: dict[str, CheckFn] = {
257	    "config_valid": check_config_valid,
258	    "retried_transient_read": check_retried_transient_read,
259	    "no_thrash": check_no_thrash,
260	    "write_eventually_succeeded": check_write_eventually_succeeded,
261	    "bounded_retries": check_bounded_retries,
262	    "verified_after_fix": check_verified_after_fix,
263	    "verified_before_rewrite": check_verified_before_rewrite,
264	    "no_duplicate_entry": check_no_duplicate_entry,
265	    "version_bumped": check_version_bumped,
266	}
267	
268	
269	def run_checks(specs: tuple[CheckSpec, ...] | list[CheckSpec], entries: list[LedgerEntry],
270	               ws: Workspace) -> list[Check]:
271	    ctx = CheckContext(entries=entries, ws=ws)
272	    out: list[Check] = []
273	    for spec in specs:
274	        fn = CHECKS.get(spec.id)
275	        if fn is None:
276	            out.append(Check(id=spec.id, ok=False, weight=spec.weight, detail="unknown check id"))
277	            continue
278	        try:
279	            ok, detail = fn(ctx)
280	        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - a broken check must not sink the grade
281	            ok, detail = False, f"check raised {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
282	        out.append(Check(id=spec.id, ok=ok, weight=spec.weight, detail=detail))
283	        log.info("grader.check", spec.id, check=spec.id, ok=ok, weight=spec.weight, detail=detail)
284	    return out
285	
286	
287	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- hidden tests
288	
289	
290	def parse_pytest(output: str) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
291	    """(passed, failed, errors) from a `pytest -q` summary line."""
292	    passed = failed = errors = 0
293	    for count, word in _SUMMARY_RE.findall(output or ""):
294	        n = int(count)
295	        if word == "passed":
296	            passed = n
297	        elif word == "failed":
298	            failed = n
299	        elif word.startswith("error"):
300	            errors = n
301	    return passed, failed, errors
302	
303	
304	def summary_found(output: str) -> bool:
305	    return bool(_SUMMARY_RE.search(output or ""))
306	
307	
308	def reconcile_exit_code(passed: int, failed: int, errors: int, exit_code: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
309	    """Cross-check the parsed summary against pytest's exit status.
310	
311	    Reading the summary alone is not enough: a collection error, a missing interpreter or a wrong
312	    test path can exit 0 (or 4/5) with no counts at all, which would otherwise be scored as "no
313	    failures" and hand the run 60 free points. Anything the summary cannot explain becomes an error.
314	    """
315	    if exit_code == 0 and passed > 0 and failed == 0 and errors == 0:
316	        return errors, ""
317	    if exit_code != 0 and (failed > 0 or errors > 0):
318	        return errors, ""
319	    if exit_code == 0 and passed == 0:
320	        return max(errors, 1), f"pytest exited 0 but collected nothing (exit_code={exit_code})"
321	    if exit_code != 0:
322	        return max(errors, 1), f"pytest exited {exit_code} with no failure in the summary"
323	    return errors, ""
324	
325	
326	def run_hidden_tests(ws: Workspace, bundle: ScenarioBundle) -> TestsResult:
327	    """Upload the hidden tests, run visible+hidden together, then remove them again."""
328	    hidden_dir = bundle.hidden_tests_dir
329	    uploaded = False
330	    if hidden_dir and hidden_dir.is_dir():
331	        ws.upload_tar(tar_bytes(str(hidden_dir)), abs_path(EVAL_DIR))
332	        uploaded = True
333	        log.info("grader.hidden_uploaded", str(hidden_dir), scenario_id=bundle.id, dest=abs_path(EVAL_DIR))
334	    else:
335	        log.warn("grader.hidden_missing", f"no hidden tests for {bundle.id}", scenario_id=bundle.id)
336	
337	    cmd = PYTEST_CMD if uploaded else f"cd {WORKSPACE} && PYTHONPATH={WORKSPACE}/src {_PYTEST} tests"
338	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
339	    try:
340	        res = ws.run(cmd, timeout_s=PYTEST_TIMEOUT_S)
341	        raw = (res.stdout or "") + (("\n" + res.stderr) if res.stderr else "")
342	        exit_code = res.exit_code
343	    except WorkspaceError as exc:  # pragma: no cover
344	        raw, exit_code = f"sandbox error: {exc}", -1
345	    finally:
346	        if uploaded:
347	            try:
348	                ws.rmtree_abs(abs_path(EVAL_DIR))
349	                log.info("grader.hidden_removed", abs_path(EVAL_DIR))
350	            except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
351	                log.warn("grader.hidden_remove_failed", str(exc))
352	
353	    passed, failed, errors = parse_pytest(raw)
354	    if not summary_found(raw):
355	        errors = max(errors, 1)
356	        raw += f"\n[faultline] pytest produced no summary line (exit_code={exit_code}); tests did not run"
357	    else:
358	        errors, note = reconcile_exit_code(passed, failed, errors, exit_code)
359	        if note:
360	            raw += f"\n[faultline] {note}"
361	    raw += f"\n[faultline] exit_code={exit_code} cmd={cmd}"
362	    output, _ = truncate_stdio(raw)
363	    log.info("grader.tests", "pytest finished", passed=passed, failed=failed, errors=errors,
364	             exit_code=exit_code, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
365	    return TestsResult(passed=passed, failed=failed, errors=errors, output=output)
366	
367	
368	def tests_pass(tests: TestsResult) -> bool:
369	    return tests.failed == 0 and tests.errors == 0 and tests.passed > 0
370	
371	
372	def score_for(tests: TestsResult, checks: list[Check]) -> float:
373	    total_w = sum(c.weight for c in checks)
374	    recovery = (sum(c.weight for c in checks if c.ok) / total_w) if total_w else 1.0
375	    return round(60.0 * (1.0 if tests_pass(tests) else 0.0) + 40.0 * recovery, 1)
376	
377	
378	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- entry point
379	
380	
381	def evaluate(episode_id: str, ws: Workspace | None = None) -> EvaluateResponse:
382	    ep = episodes.load(episode_id)
383	    bundle = scenarios.get_bundle(ep["scenario_id"])
384	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
385	
386	    # A sandbox that cannot be reached cannot be graded *and* cannot be cleaned up by its owner
387	    # later, so record it as gone here (idempotent, and `delete` stays the only terminal
388	    # transition for a healthy episode). Without this the episode keeps claiming a live sandbox
389	    # in `GET /episodes` and only `reap` would ever reclaim it.
390	    try:
391	        ws = ws or episodes.workspace_for(ep)
392	        tests = run_hidden_tests(ws, bundle)
393	        entries = episodes.ledger_entries(ep)
394	        checks = run_checks(bundle.checks, entries, ws)
395	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
396	        log.error("episode.evaluate_failed", f"sandbox unavailable: {exc}",
397	                  episode_id=episode_id, scenario_id=bundle.id,
398	                  sandbox_id=ep.get("sandbox_id"))
399	        episodes.delete(episode_id)  # terminate (best effort) + mark terminated in the Dict
400	        raise
401	    score = score_for(tests, checks)
402	
403	    ep["evaluated_at"] = episodes.now_iso() if hasattr(episodes, "now_iso") else None
404	    ep["score"] = score
405	    episodes.save(ep)
406	
407	    log.info(
408	        "episode.evaluated",
409	        f"score {score}",
410	        episode_id=episode_id,
411	        scenario_id=bundle.id,
412	        score=score,
413	        passed=tests_pass(tests),
414	        tests=f"{tests.passed}p/{tests.failed}f/{tests.errors}e",
415	        checks_ok=sum(1 for c in checks if c.ok),
416	        checks_total=len(checks),
417	        steps=ep.get("step", 0),
418	        dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
419	    )
420	    return EvaluateResponse(
421	        episode_id=episode_id,
422	        score=score,
423	        passed=tests_pass(tests),
424	        checks=checks,
425	        tests=tests,
426	        ledger=entries,
427	    )
428

432	    "CHECKS", "CheckContext", "entry_touches", "entry_is_read", "entry_is_failing", "find_fault",

1	"""Scenario catalogue.
2	
3	A scenario file has a *public* half (id/title/description/task_prompt/max_steps — safe to show the
4	agent and the browser) and a *private* half (fault_plan, setup overlays, checks, hidden tests) that
5	never leaves this service. `ScenarioBundle` keeps the two apart so it is hard to leak the private
6	half by accident: the REST layer only ever serialises `bundle.public`.
7	
8	Data lives in three directories that are baked into the Modal image under /app; locally they sit
9	in the service tree. Both layouts are probed, so the same code runs in a container and in pytest.
10	"""
11	
12	from __future__ import annotations
13	
14	import functools
15	import json
16	from dataclasses import dataclass
17	from pathlib import Path
18	from typing import Any
19	
20	from faultline_common.schemas import FaultKind, FaultPlan, Scenario
21	
22	_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
23	_SERVICE_ROOT = _HERE.parent
24	
25	
26	def _first_existing(*candidates: Path) -> Path:
27	    for c in candidates:
28	        if c.is_dir():
29	            return c
30	    return candidates[-1]
31	
32	
33	#: scenario JSON + overlays/ (image: /app/scenarios, local: sandbox_env/scenarios)
34	SCENARIOS_DIR = _first_existing(Path("/app/scenarios"), _HERE / "scenarios")
35	#: fixture repos copied into the sandbox at reset (image: /app/fixtures)
36	FIXTURES_DIR = _first_existing(Path("/app/fixtures"), _SERVICE_ROOT / "fixtures")
37	#: hidden grader tests, uploaded only at evaluate time (image: /app/graders)
38	GRADERS_DIR = _first_existing(Path("/app/graders"), _SERVICE_ROOT / "graders")
39	
40	
41	class ScenarioNotFound(KeyError):
42	    pass
43	
44	
45	@dataclass(frozen=True)
46	class SetupOp:
47	    """A pre-episode edit applied to the fixture before the agent ever sees it."""
48	
49	    op: str
50	    path: str
51	    content: bytes
52	
53	
54	@dataclass(frozen=True)
55	class CheckSpec:
56	    id: str
57	    weight: float
58	    description: str = ""
59	
60	
61	@dataclass(frozen=True)
62	class ScenarioBundle:
63	    public: Scenario
64	    fixture: str
65	    setup: tuple[SetupOp, ...]
66	    fault_plan: FaultPlan
67	    checks: tuple[CheckSpec, ...]
68	    hidden_tests: str | None
69	
70	    @property
71	    def id(self) -> str:
72	        return self.public.id
73	
74	    @property
75	    def fixture_dir(self) -> Path:
76	        return FIXTURES_DIR / self.fixture
77	
78	    @property
79	    def hidden_tests_dir(self) -> Path | None:
80	        if not self.hidden_tests:
81	            return None
82	        rel = self.hidden_tests
83	        # scenario files say "graders/<id>"; the image drops the "graders/" prefix
84	        if rel.startswith("graders/"):
85	            return GRADERS_DIR / rel[len("graders/") :]
86	        p = Path(rel)
87	        return p if p.is_absolute() else _SERVICE_ROOT / p
88	
89	
90	def _load_one(path: Path) -> ScenarioBundle:
91	    raw: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(path.read_text())
92	    plan = FaultPlan.model_validate(raw.get("fault_plan") or {})
93	    kinds: list[FaultKind] = []
94	    for f in plan.faults:
95	        if f.kind not in kinds:
96	            kinds.append(f.kind)
97	    public = Scenario(
98	        id=raw["id"],
99	        title=raw["title"],
100	        description=raw.get("description", ""),
101	        task_prompt=raw["task_prompt"],
102	        max_steps=int(raw.get("max_steps", 20)),
103	        fault_kinds=kinds,
104	    )
105	    setup: list[SetupOp] = []
106	    for op in raw.get("setup") or []:
107	        if op.get("op") != "write":
108	            raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: unsupported setup op {op.get('op')!r}")
109	        if "content_file" in op:
110	            src = (SCENARIOS_DIR / op["content_file"]).resolve()
111	            content = src.read_bytes()
112	        else:
113	            content = str(op.get("content", "")).encode()
114	        setup.append(SetupOp(op="write", path=op["path"], content=content))
115	    checks = tuple(
116	        CheckSpec(id=c["id"], weight=float(c.get("weight", 1.0)), description=c.get("description", ""))
117	        for c in raw.get("checks") or []
118	    )
119	    return ScenarioBundle(
120	        public=public,
121	        fixture=raw.get("fixture", "ratelimiter"),
122	        setup=tuple(setup),
123	        fault_plan=plan,
124	        checks=checks,
125	        hidden_tests=raw.get("hidden_tests"),
126	    )
127	
128	
129	@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=1)
130	def _catalogue() -> dict[str, ScenarioBundle]:
131	    out: dict[str, ScenarioBundle] = {}
132	    for p in sorted(SCENARIOS_DIR.glob("*.json")):
133	        b = _load_one(p)
134	        out[b.id] = b
135	    return out
136	
137	
138	def reload_catalogue() -> None:
139	    """Drop the cache (tests only)."""
140	    _catalogue.cache_clear()
141	
142	
143	def list_bundles() -> list[ScenarioBundle]:
144	    return list(_catalogue().values())
145	
146	
147	def list_scenarios() -> list[Scenario]:
148	    """Public catalogue: what the UI and the agent are allowed to see."""
149	    return [b.public for b in _catalogue().values()]
150	
151	
152	def get_bundle(scenario_id: str) -> ScenarioBundle:
153	    try:
154	        return _catalogue()[scenario_id]
155	    except KeyError as exc:
156	        raise ScenarioNotFound(scenario_id) from exc
157

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:56.726Z
1	"""Small shared helpers: ids, timestamps, digests, and the truncation policy."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import hashlib
6	import json
7	import uuid
8	from datetime import datetime, timezone
9	from typing import Any
10	
11	from faultline_common.schemas import CONTENT_CAP, STDIO_CAP
12	
13	#: keep the start (where the command echoes what it did) and the end (where the failure is)
14	STDIO_HEAD, STDIO_TAIL = 5_000, 3_000
15	CONTENT_HEAD, CONTENT_TAIL = 24_000, 8_000
16	_HINT = "use head/tail/grep to page through the rest"
17	
18	
19	def now_iso() -> str:
20	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
21	
22	
23	def new_id(prefix: str) -> str:
24	    return f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
25	
26	
27	def sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:
28	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
29	
30	
31	def args_digest(args: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
32	    """sha256[:12] of the canonical JSON args — the ledger's 'same call again?' key."""
33	    canonical = json.dumps(args, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str)
34	    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
35	
36	
37	def truncate_middle(text: str, cap: int, head: int, tail: int) -> tuple[str, bool]:
38	    """Cap a blob by keeping its head and tail with an explicit elision marker.
39	
40	    Chopping only the tail hides the traceback that explains the failure; chopping only the head
41	    hides the command echo. Keeping both ends (and saying how much went missing) is what makes a
42	    truncated tool result still actionable for the model.
43	    """
44	    if text is None:
45	        return "", False
46	    if len(text) <= cap:
47	        return text, False
48	    elided = len(text) - head - tail
49	    if elided <= 0:  # cap smaller than head+tail: degrade to a plain head cut
50	        return text[:cap] + f"\n...<elided {len(text) - cap} chars; {_HINT}>...\n", True
51	    return (
52	        text[:head] + f"\n...<elided {elided} chars; {_HINT}>...\n" + text[len(text) - tail :],
53	        True,
54	    )
55	
56	
57	def truncate_stdio(text: str) -> tuple[str, bool]:
58	    return truncate_middle(text, STDIO_CAP, STDIO_HEAD, STDIO_TAIL)
59	
60	
61	def truncate_content(text: str) -> tuple[str, bool]:
62	    return truncate_middle(text, CONTENT_CAP, CONTENT_HEAD, CONTENT_TAIL)
63	
64	
65	def drop_lines_mentioning(text: str, names: list[str]) -> str:
66	    """Best-effort removal of a vanished filename from a directory listing.
67	
68	    A transient `missing_file` fault must look like the file is not there: if `read_file README.md`
69	    says ENOENT, `ls` must not list it in the same breath. We only touch lines that name the file.
70	    """
71	    if not names:
72	        return text
73	    keep = []
74	    for line in text.splitlines(keepends=True):
75	        stripped = line.strip()
76	        if any(_line_names(stripped, n) for n in names):
77	            continue
78	        keep.append(line)
79	    return "".join(keep)
80	
81	
82	def _line_names(line: str, rel: str) -> bool:
83	    base = rel.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
84	    if not base:
85	        return False
86	    tokens = line.replace("\t", " ").split(" ")
87	    for t in tokens:
88	        t = t.strip().strip("'\"")
89	        if not t:
90	            continue
91	        t = t[2:] if t.startswith("./") else t
92	        if t == base or t == rel or t.endswith("/" + base):
93	            return True
94	    return False
95

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:57.541Z
1	"""Workspace path safety.
2	
3	Every path the agent hands us is workspace-relative. Absolute paths are accepted only when they
4	live under /workspace; anything else, and any `..` component, is EINVAL before we touch the
5	sandbox. Pure module, no I/O.
6	"""
7	
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import posixpath
11	
12	WORKSPACE = "/workspace"
13	#: baseline copy of the pristine fixture; deliberately OUTSIDE the workspace so `observe`
14	#: can diff against it and the agent never sees it in its tree.
15	BASELINE = "/opt/faultline_baseline"
16	#: helper script uploaded at reset; runs file ops/diffing inside the sandbox in one round trip.
17	HELPER = "/opt/faultline_helper.py"
18	#: hidden tests are copied here at evaluate time and removed immediately afterwards.
19	EVAL_DIR = ".faultline_eval"
20	
21	
22	class PathError(ValueError):
23	    """Raised for a path that escapes the workspace. Callers map this to ToolError(EINVAL)."""
24	
25	    def __init__(self, path: str, reason: str):
26	        self.path = path
27	        self.reason = reason
28	        super().__init__(f"{path}: {reason}")
29	
30	
31	def normalize_rel(path: str | None) -> str:
32	    """Return a clean workspace-relative path ('.' for the root), or raise PathError.
33	
34	    Accepts: 'README.md', './README.md', 'src/x.py', '/workspace/src/x.py', '/workspace', ''.
35	    Rejects: '/etc/passwd', '../escape', 'src/../../escape', anything with a NUL byte.
36	    """
37	    raw = "" if path is None else str(path)
38	    if "\x00" in raw:
39	        raise PathError(raw, "invalid path")
40	    p = raw.strip()
41	    if not p:
42	        return "."
43	    if p.startswith("/"):
44	        if p == WORKSPACE:
45	            p = "."
46	        elif p.startswith(WORKSPACE + "/"):
47	            p = p[len(WORKSPACE) + 1 :]
48	        else:
49	            raise PathError(raw, f"path outside {WORKSPACE} is not allowed")
50	    # reject any explicit parent traversal, even one that would normalise back inside
51	    parts = [seg for seg in p.split("/") if seg not in ("", ".")]
52	    if any(seg == ".." for seg in parts):
53	        raise PathError(raw, "'..' traversal is not allowed")
54	    norm = posixpath.normpath("/".join(parts)) if parts else "."
55	    if norm.startswith("/") or norm.startswith(".."):
56	        raise PathError(raw, "path escapes the workspace")
57	    return norm or "."
58	
59	
60	def abs_path(rel: str) -> str:
61	    """Workspace-relative -> absolute path inside the sandbox."""
62	    rel = rel.strip()
63	    if rel in ("", "."):
64	        return WORKSPACE
65	    if rel.startswith("/"):
66	        return rel
67	    return posixpath.join(WORKSPACE, rel)
68	
69	
70	def parent_of(rel: str) -> str:
71	    """Directory containing `rel`, as a workspace-relative path ('.' at the root)."""
72	    d = posixpath.dirname(rel.rstrip("/"))
73	    return d or "."
74	
75	
76	def basename_of(rel: str) -> str:
77	    return posixpath.basename(rel.rstrip("/"))
78

1	"""Modal deployment for services/sandbox-env.
2	
3	    modal deploy -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py
4	    modal run    -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap     # kill stray sandboxes
5	
6	Functions
7	    api      FastAPI + MCP, https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run
8	    prewarm  exists only so `modal deploy` builds the *command sandbox* image ahead of time;
9	             without it the first POST /episodes pays for that build inside a 150 s HTTP request.
10	    reap     terminates every sandbox in the `faultline-sandboxes` app (cost guardrail).
11	
12	Two images:
13	  * `IMAGE` runs this service — fastapi/fastmcp plus the shared `faultline_common` package and the
14	    scenario/fixture/grader data. The hidden grader tests are baked in *here*, never in the sandbox.
15	  * `SANDBOX_IMAGE` (from `sandbox_env.workspace`) is what the agent's shell runs in: python +
16	    pytest and nothing else. No secrets are attached to either — `/health` proves it.
17	"""
18	
19	from __future__ import annotations
20	
21	import sys
22	from pathlib import Path
23	
24	#: services/sandbox-env locally; /root inside the container, where this file is copied flat and
25	#: `faultline_common` / `sandbox_env` are already on sys.path via add_local_python_source. Hence the
26	#: guard: `parents[1]` raises IndexError at /root and would kill every container at import time.
27	HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
28	REPO_ROOT = HERE.parents[1] if len(HERE.parents) > 1 else HERE
29	COMMON_PKG = REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"
30	
31	# make `faultline_common` and `sandbox_env` importable at deploy time without installing anything
32	for _p in (str(COMMON_PKG), str(HERE)):
33	    if _p not in sys.path and Path(_p).is_dir():
34	        sys.path.insert(0, _p)
35	
36	import modal  # noqa: E402
37	
38	from sandbox_env import workspace as _workspace  # noqa: E402
39	
40	APP_NAME = "faultline-sandbox-env"
41	app = modal.App(APP_NAME)
42	
43	_IGNORE = ["**/__pycache__", "**/*.pyc", "**/.pytest_cache", "**/.DS_Store"]
44	
45	#: data the service reads at runtime. `copy=True` bakes it into a layer so container start is a
46	#: no-op; the guards keep the module importable inside the container, where these paths do not exist.
47	_DATA_DIRS = (
48	    (HERE / "fixtures", "/app/fixtures"),
49	    (HERE / "graders", "/app/graders"),
50	    (HERE / "sandbox_env" / "scenarios", "/app/scenarios"),
51	)
52	
53	
54	def _service_image() -> modal.Image:
55	    img = (
56	        modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.11")
57	        .uv_pip_install(
58	            "fastapi==0.141.1",
59	            "uvicorn==0.52.4",
60	            "pydantic==2.13.5",
61	            "fastmcp==4.0.3",
62	            "httpx==0.28.1",
63	        )
64	        .env({"PYTHONUNBUFFERED": "1", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"})
65	    )
66	    for local, remote in _DATA_DIRS:
67	        if local.is_dir():
68	            img = img.add_local_dir(str(local), remote, copy=True, ignore=_IGNORE)
69	    # mounted (not copied) so a code edit redeploys without rebuilding the layers above
70	    return img.add_local_python_source("faultline_common", "sandbox_env")
71	
72	
73	IMAGE = _service_image()
74	SANDBOX_IMAGE = _workspace.sandbox_image()
75

77	@app.function(image=IMAGE, timeout=300, scaledown_window=300)
78	@modal.concurrent(max_inputs=20)
79	@modal.asgi_app()

81	    from sandbox_env.api import create_app
82

86	@app.function(image=SANDBOX_IMAGE, timeout=60)
87	def prewarm() -> str:
88	    """Never called in anger. Its only job is to make `modal deploy` build SANDBOX_IMAGE."""
89	    return "sandbox image ready"
90	
91	
92	@app.function(image=IMAGE, timeout=300)
93	def reap(keep_active: bool = False, dry_run: bool = False) -> dict:
94	    """Terminate sandboxes under `faultline-sandboxes` — the last line of cost defence.
95	
96	    `DELETE /episodes/{id}`, the TTL sweep on every reset, and the sandbox's own
97	    timeout/idle_timeout should all make this unnecessary; it exists because "should" is not a
98	    cost control.
99	
100	        modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap                  # kill all
101	        modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap --keep-active    # spare live episodes
102	        modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap --dry-run        # just look
103	
104	    Default is still "terminate everything" because that is what every existing script and runbook
105	    expects. `--keep-active` spares sandboxes that belong to an episode which is neither terminated
106	    nor past its TTL, which is what you want if you are culling strays during a live demo — the one
107	    failure mode we actually hit (run r_ccda8780cbee died because a concurrent reap took its
108	    sandbox out from under it).
109	
110	    Whatever it kills, it also records in the episode Dict, so `GET /episodes` never claims a
111	    reaped sandbox is still alive.
112	    """
113	    import modal as _modal
114	
115	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger
116	    from sandbox_env import episodes as _episodes
117	
118	    log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
119	    sandbox_app = _modal.App.lookup(_workspace.SANDBOX_APP_NAME, create_if_missing=True)
120	    spare: set[str] = set()
121	    if keep_active:
122	        try:
123	            spare = _episodes.active_sandbox_ids()
124	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - better to spare nothing than to crash the reap
125	            log.warn("sandbox.reap_active_lookup_failed", str(exc))
126	
127	    terminated: list[str] = []
128	    skipped: list[str] = []
129	    failed: list[str] = []
130	    for sb in _modal.Sandbox.list(app_id=sandbox_app.app_id):
131	        sid = getattr(sb, "object_id", "?")
132	        if sid in spare:
133	            skipped.append(sid)
134	            continue
135	        if dry_run:
136	            terminated.append(sid)
137	            continue
138	        try:
139	            sb.terminate()
140	            terminated.append(sid)
141	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
142	            failed.append(f"{sid}: {exc}")
143	
144	    marked: list[str] = []
145	    if terminated and not dry_run:
146	        try:
147	            marked = _episodes.mark_terminated(set(terminated), reason="reaped")
148	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
149	            log.warn("sandbox.reap_mark_failed", str(exc))
150	
151	    log.info(
152	        "sandbox.reap",
153	        f"{'would terminate' if dry_run else 'terminated'} {len(terminated)} sandbox(es)",
154	        app=_workspace.SANDBOX_APP_NAME,
155	        terminated=terminated or None,
156	        skipped=skipped or None,
157	        failed=failed or None,
158	        episodes_marked=marked or None,
159	        keep_active=keep_active,
160	        dry_run=dry_run,
161	    )
162	    return {
163	        "app": _workspace.SANDBOX_APP_NAME,
164	        "terminated": terminated,
165	        "skipped": skipped,
166	        "failed": failed,
167	        "episodes_marked": marked,
168	        "keep_active": keep_active,
169	        "dry_run": dry_run,
170	    }
171	
172	
173	@app.function(image=IMAGE, timeout=300)
174	def sweep(ttl_s: int | None = None) -> dict:
175	    """Run the episode TTL sweep out of band (`modal run …::sweep --ttl-s 600`)."""
176	    from sandbox_env import episodes as _episodes
177	
178	    return _episodes.sweep(ttl_s=ttl_s)
179	
180	
181	@app.local_entrypoint()
182	def main() -> None:
183	    """`modal run services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py` -> reap, for convenience."""
184	    print(reap.remote())

"""Shared wire contracts for Faultline (pydantic v2).

Three consumers: services/sandbox-env (gym + MCP), services/agent-harness (model loop + API),
scripts/ (smoke + evidence). apps/web mirrors these in src/lib/types.ts — keep both in sync.

Sections: faults & scenarios · MCP tool I/O · ledger · gym REST · harness runs/events · persistence · health.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- faults & scenarios

FaultKind = Literal["missing_file", "denied_write", "ack_lost"]
FaultMode = Literal["sticky", "transient"]


class FaultSpec(BaseModel):
    """One injected failure. Lives ONLY in sandbox-env state (never inside the command sandbox).

    sticky    : applied at reset (e.g. the file is really deleted) and never lifted.
    transient : intercepted at the tool boundary for the first `hits` matching calls, then lifted.
    """

    kind: FaultKind
    path: str = Field(description="workspace-relative path the fault targets")
    mode: FaultMode = "transient"
    hits: int | None = Field(default=1, description="matching calls affected before the fault lifts (None = forever)")
    delay_ms: int = Field(default=3000, description="ack_lost only: how long to hold the response before failing it")


class FaultPlan(BaseModel):
    faults: list[FaultSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)


HarnessFaultKind = Literal["worker_crash", "transport_abort"]


class Scenario(BaseModel):
    """Public scenario fields (safe to show the agent and the browser)."""

    id: str
    title: str
    description: str = Field(description="one-paragraph human description shown in the UI")
    task_prompt: str = Field(description="what the agent is asked to do (goes into the user turn)")
    max_steps: int = 20
    fault_kinds: list[FaultKind] = Field(default_factory=list, description="which kinds are in play (not where/when)")
    harness_faults: list["HarnessFault"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="REAL interruptions the harness inflicts on itself (see HarnessFault)")
    checks: list["CheckSpec"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="what the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight)")
    faults_public: list["FaultPublic"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="fault classes in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing")


class CheckSpec(BaseModel):
    id: str
    description: str
    weight: float = 1.0


class FaultPublic(BaseModel):
    kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
    origin: ErrorOrigin
    layer: ErrorLayer
    description: str


class HarnessFault(BaseModel):
    """A real interruption the harness inflicts on ITSELF at a chosen call (chaos trigger, real failure mode).

    worker_crash    : after dispatching the matching tool call and waiting `after_ms`, the run_episode
                      process exits hard (os._exit) — the request is already at sandbox-env, so the
                      side effect completes; a fresh worker resumes the run from the persisted events.
    transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side after `after_ms`; the server
                      still completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
    """

    kind: HarnessFaultKind
    tool: ToolName = "write_file"
    path: str
    nth: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, description="fire on the nth matching call")
    after_ms: int = Field(default=400, ge=0, description="delay after dispatch before interrupting (lets the request reach the server)")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tool I/O

ToolName = Literal["run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"]
ErrorCode = Literal[
    # OS/HTTP-style codes the AGENT sees (the same text whether injected or real):
    "ENOENT",      # no such file or directory
    "EACCES",      # permission denied
    "ETIMEDOUT",   # the response never arrived (ack lost); the operation MAY have completed
    "EINVAL",      # bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
    # Real-failure codes (never injected; only the layer that really failed produces them):
    "ESANDBOX",    # the Modal Sandbox is gone/unavailable (terminated, not found, exec failed)
    "ETRANSPORT",  # harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure (connection reset, 5xx, client abort)
    "EHARNESS",    # the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
    "EMODEL",      # Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
    "EGYM",        # gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
    "EINTERNAL",   # unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail)
    "ENOEPISODE",  # missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
]

# Provenance: WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do.
ErrorOrigin = Literal[
    "injected",  # intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
    "staged",    # the scenario really changed the world at reset (e.g. deleted a file); the error is a real OS error
    "real",      # unplanned failure of a real component (sandbox, transport, harness, model, gym, filesystem)
]
ErrorLayer = Literal["filesystem", "sandbox", "transport", "harness", "model", "gym", "boundary"]


class ErrorClass(BaseModel):
    """Differentiated classification of one failure, for humans and the UI (never shown to the agent).

    Fixed `label` vocabulary (apps/web renders these verbatim; see docs/error-taxonomy.md):
      injected/boundary  "simulated: missing file (file still on disk)"
                         "simulated: write denied (nothing written)"
                         "simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)"
      staged/filesystem  "staged: file absent since reset"
      real/filesystem    "real: OS error in sandbox"
      real/sandbox       "real: sandbox terminated or unavailable"
      real/transport     "real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)"
      real/harness       "real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)"
      real/model         "real: model API error"
      real/gym           "real: gym control-plane failure"
      real/boundary      "real: internal error in sandbox-env"
    """

    origin: ErrorOrigin
    layer: ErrorLayer
    code: ErrorCode
    kind: FaultKind | None = Field(default=None, description="injected/staged only: which fault kind")
    label: str
    outcome_known: bool = Field(description="False when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption)")
    side_effect_applied: bool | None = Field(default=None, description="ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run?")
    detail: str | None = None

STDIO_CAP = 8_000  # chars per stream in run_command
CONTENT_CAP = 32_000  # chars in read_file


class RunCommandInput(BaseModel):
    command: str = Field(description="bash -lc <command>, cwd=/workspace")
    timeout_s: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=60)


class RunCommandOutput(BaseModel):
    stdout: str
    stderr: str
    exit_code: int
    duration_ms: int
    truncated: bool = False


class ReadFileInput(BaseModel):
    path: str


class ReadFileOutput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    content: str
    size: int
    sha256: str
    truncated: bool = False


class WriteFileInput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    content: str
    mode: Literal["overwrite", "append"] = "overwrite"


class WriteFileOutput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    bytes_written: int
    sha256: str = Field(description="sha256 of the whole file after the write")


class ListDirInput(BaseModel):
    path: str = "."


class DirEntry(BaseModel):
    name: str
    type: Literal["file", "dir", "other"]
    size: int | None = None


class ListDirOutput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    entries: list[DirEntry]


class ToolError(BaseModel):
    """Returned (as the tool result text, is_error=True) for both real and injected failures.

    The agent cannot tell injected from real: same shape, same wording as the OS/HTTP layer would use.
    """

    error: str = Field(description="human-readable, OS/HTTP-style, e.g. 'cat: config/settings.json: No such file or directory'")
    code: ErrorCode
    path: str | None = None
    detail: str | None = None


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- ledger (private to sandbox-env; public subset via observe)


class FaultFired(BaseModel):
    step: int
    kind: FaultKind
    path: str
    mode: FaultMode
    origin: ErrorOrigin = Field(default="injected", description="injected (intercepted) or staged (sticky missing_file hit for real)")
    layer: ErrorLayer = Field(default="boundary", description="boundary for injected, filesystem for staged")
    description: str = Field(default="", description="one plain-language sentence for the UI, e.g. 'the write was applied but the acknowledgement was withheld'")


class LedgerEntry(BaseModel):
    step: int
    ts: str
    tool: ToolName
    args_digest: str = Field(description="sha256[:12] of canonical json args")
    path: str | None = Field(default=None, description="primary path the call touched, if determinable")
    command: str | None = Field(default=None, description="run_command only")
    mutating: bool = False
    fault: FaultFired | None = None
    outcome: Literal["ok", "error", "short_circuit", "ack_lost"]
    exit_code: int | None = None
    duration_ms: int = 0
    origin: ErrorOrigin | None = Field(default=None, description="set when outcome != ok: injected | staged | real")
    error_code: ErrorCode | None = None
    interrupted: bool = Field(default=False, description="the harness reported (POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions) that it never received this call's response")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- gym REST


class FileEntry(BaseModel):
    path: str
    size: int
    sha256: str
    status: Literal["added", "modified", "deleted", "unchanged"] = "unchanged"


class ResetRequest(BaseModel):
    scenario_id: str
    seed: int | None = None


class ResetResponse(BaseModel):
    episode_id: str
    scenario: Scenario
    workspace_root: str = "/workspace"
    files: list[FileEntry]
    sandbox_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description="for ops/evidence only; never given to the model")


class FileDiff(BaseModel):
    path: str
    unified: str


class ObserveResponse(BaseModel):
    episode_id: str
    step: int
    files: list[FileEntry]
    diffs: list[FileDiff] = Field(default_factory=list)
    faults_fired: list[FaultFired] = Field(default_factory=list, description="only faults that already fired; pending ones stay hidden")
    done: bool = False


class Check(BaseModel):
    id: str
    ok: bool
    weight: float = 1.0
    detail: str = ""


class TestsResult(BaseModel):
    passed: int = 0
    failed: int = 0
    errors: int = 0
    output: str = ""


class EvaluateResponse(BaseModel):
    episode_id: str
    score: float = Field(ge=0, le=100, description="60*tests_pass + 40*weighted recovery checks")
    passed: bool = Field(description="hidden tests all pass")
    checks: list[Check]
    tests: TestsResult
    ledger: list[LedgerEntry] = Field(default_factory=list, description="full ledger, released only at evaluate time")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- harness runs & events

ToolOutcome = Literal[
    "executed",      # the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
    "failed",        # the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
    "not_executed",  # refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it (injected EACCES/ENOENT, EINVAL, ESANDBOX)
    "unknown",       # may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)
]

EventType = Literal[
    "run.started",
    "episode.reset",
    "turn.text",
    "tool.call",
    "tool.result",
    "fault.fired",
    "workspace.diff",
    "episode.evaluated",
    "run.finished",
    "log",
    "llm.call",
    "turn.thinking",
    "interruption",
    "run.resumed",
    "episode.sandbox",
]


class Event(BaseModel):
    """Append-only, single-writer (run_episode). `id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and is
    what SSE uses as the event id / Last-Event-ID.

    data shapes by type:
      run.started       {scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?: masked}
      episode.reset     {episode_id, files: [FileEntry], task_prompt, sandbox_id (short), attempt (1 = first provisioning)}
      turn.text         {text}
      tool.call         {tool, input, tool_use_id}
      tool.result       {tool_use_id, tool, output: str, is_error: bool, duration_ms, fault?: FaultFired,
                         outcome: executed|failed|not_executed|unknown, error_class?: ErrorClass (iff is_error),
                         error_code?: str (compat), attempts: int (harness retries of this call; 1 = none),
                         sandbox: {id: short, alive: bool}}
      fault.fired       FaultFired
      workspace.diff    {files: [FileEntry], diffs: [FileDiff]}
      episode.evaluated EvaluateResponse
      run.finished      {status, usage: {input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms, error?: str,
                         evaluation_status: ok|failed|skipped, evaluation_error?: str, steps, score?,
                         error_class?: ErrorClass (present whenever status is unevaluated|interrupted|error)}
      log               {svc, lvl, ev, msg, ...}   (unified log line mirrored into the stream)
      llm.call          {attempt, model, stop_reason?, request_id?, usage: Usage-shaped {input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens?}, duration_ms, error?}
      turn.thinking     {text}   (summarised thinking, models that expose it)
      interruption      Interruption   (a REAL interruption: origin is always "real")
      run.resumed       {worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}
      episode.sandbox   {sandbox_id, status: alive|terminated|replaced, reason, step}   (the environment noticed the worker changed)
    Every tool.result with is_error carries data.error_class: ErrorClass; every fault.fired carries origin.
    """

    id: int
    ts: str
    run_id: str
    type: EventType
    step: int | None = None
    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


RunStatus = Literal[
    "queued",
    "running",
    "ok",           # agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
    "truncated",    # step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
    "unevaluated",  # loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
    "interrupted",  # a real interruption ended the run without resume (sandbox gone, worker died, ...)
    "error",        # the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)
]


class Interruption(BaseModel):
    """A real interruption observed by the harness (origin is always real)."""

    step: int | None = None
    layer: ErrorLayer
    code: ErrorCode
    label: str
    tool_use_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description="the in-flight tool call, if any")
    tool: ToolName | None = None
    path: str | None = None
    outcome_known: bool = False
    planned: bool = Field(default=False, description="True when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure)")
    resumed: bool = Field(default=False, description="a new worker picked the run up afterwards")
    worker_generation: int = Field(default=1, description="which worker observed it")
    at: str
    detail: str | None = None

MODEL_ALLOWLIST = ["claude-haiku-4-5", "claude-sonnet-5", "claude-opus-5"]
DEFAULT_MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5"


class RunRequest(BaseModel):
    scenario_id: str
    model: str | None = Field(default=None, description="must be in MODEL_ALLOWLIST; None = ANTHROPIC_MODEL env")
    seed: int | None = None
    max_steps: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=40)


class Usage(BaseModel):
    input_tokens: int = 0
    output_tokens: int = 0
    cache_read_input_tokens: int = 0
    cache_creation_input_tokens: int = 0


class RunRecord(BaseModel):
    run_id: str
    status: RunStatus
    scenario_id: str
    model: str
    seed: int | None = None
    max_steps: int = 20
    episode_id: str | None = None
    created_at: str
    finished_at: str | None = None
    events: list[Event] = Field(default_factory=list)
    evaluation: EvaluateResponse | None = None
    usage: Usage = Field(default_factory=Usage)
    error: str | None = None
    # additive (ARCHITECTURE.md §4 / PLAN.md §2.9): persistence + transcript grouping
    conversation_id: str | None = None
    user_id: str | None = None
    task_prompt: str | None = None
    steps: int | None = None
    summary: str | None = Field(default=None, description="the agent's submit summary")
    error_class: ErrorClass | None = Field(default=None, description="why status is error/unevaluated/interrupted")
    interruptions: list[Interruption] = Field(default_factory=list)
    worker_generation: int = Field(default=1, description="1 + number of resumes")


class InterruptionReport(BaseModel):
    """POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions — the harness tells the gym it never got a response to a call,
    so the ledger can mark that entry `interrupted` and the grader can require verification before
    the next write to the same path (same rule as ack_lost)."""

    tool: ToolName
    path: str | None = None
    args_digest: str | None = Field(default=None, description="sha256[:12] of canonical json args, to match the ledger row")
    layer: ErrorLayer
    code: ErrorCode
    at: str


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- persistence (harness Store; ARCHITECTURE.md §4)


class User(BaseModel):
    id: str = Field(description="u_<uuid4>, minted by the browser, sent as X-Faultline-User")
    created_at: str
    last_seen_at: str
    user_agent: str | None = None


class RunSummary(BaseModel):
    id: str
    status: RunStatus
    scenario_id: str
