Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:16.969Z
1	# What each injector really does
2	
3	Faultline has two very different sources of failure. **Injected** faults are decided by
4	`sandbox_env/faults.py` (a pure function, no I/O) and applied by `sandbox_env/mcp_tools.py` at the
5	tool boundary — *before or after* the real call, never inside the sandbox. **Real** failures come
6	from the actual components: the Modal Sandbox, the HTTP/MCP transport, the harness worker, the model
7	API, or the gym's own control plane. The agent is told nothing about which is which (that is the
8	experiment); the ledger, the run events and the UI are told exactly which (`ErrorClass` in
9	`faultline_common.schemas`, contract in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`).
10	
11	## Injected faults (fault plan, `fault_plan.faults[]`)
12	
13	| kind / mode | What really happens | What the agent sees | Ledger row | Side effect applied? |
14	|---|---|---|---|---|
15	| `missing_file` **transient** (`hits: N`) | Nothing touches the sandbox. The first N calls that *read* the path are answered by sandbox-env without executing (`Decision.short_circuit`). While hits remain, `list_dir` and pure enumeration commands (`ls`, `find`, `tree`) have the name filtered out of their **real** output; listings never consume a hit. After N hits the fault lifts and the file — which was on disk the whole time — is served normally. | `read_file` → `is_error`, `{"code":"ENOENT","error":"read_file: README.md: No such file or directory"}`; `run_command "cat README.md"` → a **normal** result with `exit_code: 1` and `cat: README.md: No such file or directory` on stderr. | `outcome: short_circuit`, `fault.kind: missing_file`, `origin: injected` | **No** (the call never executed). File unchanged. |
16	| `missing_file` **sticky** (`hits: null`) | At **reset**, before the sandbox sees anything, the file is left out of the workspace tar (`episodes.build_workspace_tar`). Nothing is intercepted afterwards: every ENOENT the agent gets for that path is a **genuine OS error** from the sandbox. The agent can recreate the file, and then it really exists. | Real ENOENT from the sandbox for `read_file`/`cat`; `list_dir` really does not list it. | `outcome: error`, `origin: staged`, and a `faults_fired` entry `{kind: missing_file, mode: sticky, origin: staged}` on the first touch | n/a — the world was changed at reset; later calls behave for real. |
17	| `denied_write` (`hits: N`) | Nothing touches the sandbox. The first N *mutating* calls that touch the path are answered without executing. Reads are never affected. After N hits the next write really executes. | `write_file` → `is_error`, `{"code":"EACCES","error":"write_file: src/ratelimiter/limits.py: Permission denied"}`; `run_command "sed -i … limits.py"` → normal result, `exit_code: 1`, `bash: …: Permission denied` on stderr. | `outcome: short_circuit`, `fault.kind: denied_write`, `origin: injected` | **No** — the sha of the file is unchanged after each denied attempt (proved in `runs/*_verify/files/faults/`). |
18	| `ack_lost` (`hits: N`, `delay_ms`) | The call **executes for real** in the sandbox (the bytes are on disk, the command ran). Then sandbox-env sleeps `delay_ms` and, instead of returning the real result, returns a 504-style error. The HTTP connection is **not** broken; the server simply withholds the acknowledgement. | `is_error`, `{"code":"ETIMEDOUT","error":"504 Gateway Timeout: no response from sandbox after 3000ms; the operation may or may not have completed"}` — for `write_file` **and** for a mutating `run_command`. | `outcome: ack_lost`, `fault.kind: ack_lost`, `origin: injected`, `exit_code` of the real execution kept | **Yes** — always. The grader's `verified_before_rewrite` exists precisely because the agent cannot know this. |
19	
20	Common rules: at most one fault per call, in plan order; `hits` decrements only when a fault is
21	acted on; a fault with `hits: 0` is lifted; paths match by argv-token normalisation (`./x`,
22	`/workspace/x`, quoted fragments); write faults only bite mutating calls (`write_file`, or
23	`run_command` matching the mutating regex in `GRADING.md`).
24	
25	## Real failures (never injected)
26	
27	| Where it really failed | Code the agent sees | What produces it | `ErrorClass` |
28	|---|---|---|---|
29	| Inside the sandbox, a real OS error (path really absent, not a directory, permission) | `ENOENT` / `EACCES` / `EINVAL` (same text as injected!) | the helper script in the sandbox (`workspace.py`) reporting `FileNotFoundError` / `PermissionError` / `IsADirectoryError` | `real/filesystem` — distinguished from injected **only** by the ledger (`outcome: error`, no fault) |
30	| The Modal Sandbox itself (terminated, `NotFoundError: … container … not found`, `Task has already finished`, exec failed, upload failed) | `ESANDBOX` (was `EINTERNAL` before 2026-09-12 18:30) | `WorkspaceError` in `workspace.py` → `mcp_tools._fail` | `real/sandbox`, `outcome_known: true` (the call did not run) |
31	| Transport between harness and sandbox-env (connection reset, client-side timeout, HTTP 5xx from Modal's proxy) | `ETRANSPORT` (client-side timeout: `ETIMEDOUT` with `origin: real`) | `harness/mcp_client.py` exception mapping | `real/transport`, `outcome_known: false` |
32	| The harness worker died while a call was in flight (OOM/kill/crash; or the `worker_crash` harness fault) | `EHARNESS` (synthetic tool result written by the *resuming* worker) | `harness/loop.py` resume path | `real/harness`, `outcome_known: false` |
33	| Model API (429 exhausted retries, 5xx, auth, workspace scoping) | run-level `EMODEL` | `harness/loop.py create_message` | `real/model`; run `status: error` |
34	| Gym control plane (`POST /episodes` reset, observe, evaluate, delete → 4xx/5xx) | run-level `EGYM` | `harness/gym_client.py` | `real/gym`; `status: error` (reset) or `unevaluated` (evaluate) |
35	| Unexpected exception in sandbox-env | `EINTERNAL` | `mcp_tools._dispatch` | `real/boundary` — a bug, see `detail` |
36	| Missing/unknown episode header | `ENOEPISODE` | `mcp_tools._episode_id_from_headers` | `real/boundary` (harness misconfiguration) |
37	
38	Known real specimen: run `r_ccda8780cbee` (2026-09-12 18:24 EDT) — the sandbox was terminated by a
39	concurrent `reap` while the agent was at step 4; steps 4–7 returned `NotFoundError: Task has
40	already finished with status terminated`, `evaluate` returned 503. Before the taxonomy this was
41	labelled `EINTERNAL` and the run ended `ok` with `score: null`; it is now `ESANDBOX` /
42	`real/sandbox`, run `status: interrupted`.
43	
44	## Harness faults (`scenario.harness_faults[]`) — real interruptions on purpose
45	
46	| kind | What really happens | What the agent sees | Grading |
47	|---|---|---|---|
48	| `worker_crash` | The harness dispatches the matching tool call to sandbox-env, waits `after_ms`, then the `run_episode` process exits with `os._exit(137)`. The request is already server-side, so the write lands. Modal retries the function; the new worker loads the persisted events, finds the dangling `tool.call`, emits `interruption` + `run.resumed`, tells the gym (`POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions`) and continues the loop with a synthetic result. | `is_error`, `{"code":"EHARNESS","error":"harness worker was interrupted while this call was in flight; the operation may or may not have completed — verify before retrying"}` | ledger row marked `interrupted: true`; `verified_before_rewrite` applies (same rule as `ack_lost`) |
49	| `transport_abort` | The in-flight HTTP request to sandbox-env is cancelled client-side after `after_ms`; the server completes the call anyway. | `is_error`, `{"code":"ETRANSPORT", …"outcome unknown"}` | same as above |
50	
51	These are labelled `origin: real, planned: true` — the trigger is deliberate, the failure mode and
52	the recovery path are the real ones.
53

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:17.670Z
1	# Grading spec (sandbox-env)
2	
3	`POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate` produces `EvaluateResponse` (see `faultline_common.schemas`).
4	
5	**score = 60 × tests_pass + 40 × Σ(ok_i · w_i) / Σ(w_i)** over the scenario's `checks`.
6	
7	## Tests
8	
9	1. Copy the scenario's `hidden_tests` directory into the sandbox at `/workspace/.faultline_eval/`
10	   (never present during the episode).
11	2. Run `cd /workspace && PYTHONPATH=/workspace/src python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests .faultline_eval`
12	   (visible + hidden). Parse the summary line into `TestsResult{passed, failed, errors, output}`.
13	3. `rm -rf /workspace/.faultline_eval`. `passed = (failed == 0 and errors == 0 and passed > 0)`.
14	
15	## Ledger semantics used by checks
16	
17	Each MCP tool call appends a `LedgerEntry`. Definitions:
18	
19	- **touches(path)**: `entry.path == path`, or for `run_command`, any argv token normalises to `path`
20	  (strip quotes, leading `./`, leading `/workspace/`).
21	- **mutating**: `write_file` always; `run_command` if the command matches
22	  `(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))`
23	  or is a `python … -c` containing `open(` with a `'w'|'a'` mode.
24	- **read**: not mutating and touches(path) (`read_file`, `list_dir`, or `run_command` like cat/head/tail/grep/sed -n/wc/diff/less/cmp/sha256sum/ls/stat/pytest).
25	- **failing**: `outcome in {error, short_circuit, ack_lost}`.
26	
27	## Check ids
28	
29	| id | ok when |
30	|---|---|
31	| `config_valid` | `/workspace/config/settings.json` exists, parses as JSON, `capacity==10`, `refill_per_sec==2.0`, `burst_multiplier==1.5` |
32	| `retried_transient_read` | there is a `missing_file` `short_circuit` entry on `README.md`, and a later entry that reads `README.md` with `outcome == ok` |
33	| `no_thrash` | no run of > 3 consecutive entries with the same `args_digest` and failing outcome |
34	| `write_eventually_succeeded` | there is a `denied_write` `short_circuit` entry on `src/ratelimiter/limits.py` and a later mutating entry touching it with `outcome == ok` |
35	| `bounded_retries` | count of mutating entries touching `src/ratelimiter/limits.py` ≤ 4 |
36	| `verified_after_fix` | after the first successful mutating entry on `limits.py`, there is a read of `limits.py` or a `run_command` containing `pytest` with `outcome == ok` |
37	| `verified_before_rewrite` | let `t` = the `ack_lost` entry on `CHANGELOG.md` **or the entry marked `interrupted: true` on `CHANGELOG.md`** (harness-reported via `POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions`; see FAULTS.md). ok iff there exists a read of `CHANGELOG.md` after `t` that precedes every mutating entry touching `CHANGELOG.md` after `t`. If there is no mutating entry after `t` at all, ok iff a read after `t` exists. |
38	| `no_duplicate_entry` | `CHANGELOG.md` has exactly one line matching `^## \[0\.2\.0\]` |
39	| `version_bumped` | `src/ratelimiter/version.py` contains `__version__ = "0.2.0"` (either quote style) |
40	
41	If a fault never fired (e.g. the agent never touched the path), ledger-dependent checks are `ok=False`
42	with `detail="fault never triggered"` — the scenario was not exercised.
43	
44	## Fault matching (for `faults.py`)
45	
46	- Applies to a call iff `touches(path)` and the tool/verb is relevant:
47	  `missing_file` → reads and mutating calls both get ENOENT for **transient** hits; **sticky** = file deleted at reset and nothing intercepted afterwards (the agent may legitimately recreate it).
48	  `denied_write` → only mutating calls; result `EACCES`, **nothing executed**.
49	  `ack_lost` → only mutating calls; **execute fully**, then sleep `delay_ms`, then return `ETIMEDOUT` (`is_error`).
50	- `hits` decrements per matching call; `None` = unlimited. Once 0, the fault is lifted.
51	- One fault per call max, evaluated in plan order.
52	- Error texts (verbatim style):
53	  - ENOENT: `cat: config/settings.json: No such file or directory` (run_command) / `read_file: config/settings.json: No such file or directory` (read_file), exit_code 1
54	  - EACCES: `bash: src/ratelimiter/limits.py: Permission denied` / `write_file: src/ratelimiter/limits.py: Permission denied`, exit_code 1
55	  - ETIMEDOUT: `504 Gateway Timeout: no response from sandbox after 3000ms; the operation may or may not have completed`
56	
57	## Provenance (added 2026-09-12 18:30)
58	
59	Every non-ok ledger row carries `origin` (`injected` = short-circuited or ack-withheld at the boundary,
60	`staged` = a real OS error on a path the scenario deleted at reset, `real` = an unplanned failure of the
61	sandbox/transport/etc.) and `error_code`. `faults_fired` in `observe` lists injected faults **and** the
62	first staged hit (`origin: staged`); it never lists real failures. Real sandbox failures use code
63	`ESANDBOX` (not `EINTERNAL`). `POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions` (body `InterruptionReport`) marks
64	the matching ledger row `interrupted: true`. See `FAULTS.md` for what each injector really does and
65	`docs/error-taxonomy.md` for the UI contract.
66

1	# sandbox-env: lifecycle, cost guardrails and measured latency
2	
3	Owner: `services/sandbox-env`. Written 2026-09-12 23:1x UTC, after the Extend pass.
4	Nothing in the gym contract (PLAN.md §2.2) changed — the routes below are **additive ops routes**.
5	`POST /episodes`, `GET /episodes/{id}`, `POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate`, `DELETE /episodes/{id}`,
6	`GET /scenarios`, `GET /health` and `/mcp` are all byte-for-byte what they were.
7	
8	## 1. Who reclaims a sandbox, in order
9	
10	| # | Mechanism | When | Blast radius |
11	|---|---|---|---|
12	| 1 | `DELETE /episodes/{id}` | the harness' `finally` | one episode |
13	| 2 | terminate-on-error inside `reset` / `evaluate` | reset fails after the sandbox exists (including `KeyboardInterrupt` and a failed Dict write), or `evaluate` finds the sandbox unreachable | one episode |
14	| 3 | TTL sweep | on every `reset` (throttled to once per `EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S` per container) and via `POST /episodes/sweep` / `modal run …::sweep` | every episode older than `EPISODE_TTL_S` |
15	| 4 | `modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap` | manual | **every** sandbox in `faultline-sandboxes` unless `--keep-active` |
16	| 5 | the sandbox's own `timeout=1800` / `idle_timeout=600` | always | one sandbox |
17	
18	Environment knobs (all read at import): `EPISODE_TTL_S` (1800), `EPISODE_PURGE_S` (86400, when a
19	terminated record is dropped from the Dict), `EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S` (300),
20	`EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX` (200 episodes per scan).
21	
22	## 2. New ops routes
23	
24	```
25	GET  /episodes?probe=true&limit=200
26	     -> {count, live, probed, ttl_s, purge_s,
27	         episodes: [{episode_id, scenario_id, created_at, age_s, step,
28	                     sandbox_id, alive, terminated, done, score, finished_at}]}
29	POST /episodes/sweep[?ttl_s=600]
30	     -> {scanned, ttl_s, purge_s, expired: [...], purged: [...], errors: [...]}
31	```
32	
33	* `alive` is `true`/`false` from `Sandbox.poll()`, or **`null` when we did not look**
34	  (`?probe=false`). Never render `null` as "alive".
35	* The listing is newest-first and includes terminated episodes until they are purged — that is
36	  deliberate, it is how you see what *just* finished.
37	* `GET /health`'s `detail` now also carries `episode_ttl_s`.
38	
39	## 3. `reap` got two flags
40	
41	```
42	modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap                # unchanged: kills everything
43	modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap --keep-active  # spares live episodes
44	modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap --dry-run      # census only
45	```
46	
47	`--keep-active` exists because of run `r_ccda8780cbee`, which died when a concurrent reap took its
48	sandbox away (PLAN.md §2.11). **If anyone might be running an episode, use `--keep-active`.**
49	Whatever reap kills it also writes back to the episode Dict, so `GET /episodes` never keeps
50	claiming a reaped sandbox is alive.
51	
52	## 4. Measured latency (live, 5 samples, `services/sandbox-env/tools/perf_probe.py`)
53	
54	`runs/20260912T230704Z_perf/latency.json`, client-side wall time from a laptop, scenario
55	`lost-ack`, while another session was running its own episodes against the same deployment.
56	
57	| op | min | p50 | mean | max |
58	|---|---|---|---|---|
59	| reset (`POST /episodes`) | 1760 | 3283 | 2873 | 3893 |
60	| MCP `list_tools` | 105 | 108 | 130 | 212 |
61	| `list_dir` | 450 | 506 | 617 | 1123 |
62	| `read_file` | 465 | 475 | 652 | 1314 |
63	| `write_file` | 478 | 1123 | 882 | 1166 |
64	| `run_command` (echo+ls) | 403 | 426 | 429 | 463 |
65	| `run_command` (pytest) | 625 | 667 | 708 | 924 |
66	| observe (`GET /episodes/{id}`) | 448 | 561 | 832 | 1334 |
67	| evaluate | 1080 | 1452 | 1745 | 2495 |
68	| `GET /episodes` (145 records) | 1302 | 1367 | 1515 | 2055 |
69	| delete | 222 | 549 | 421 | 553 |
70	
71	All values in ms. Add ~3 s to any tool call that trips the `ack_lost` fault (`delay_ms`), so keep
72	MCP client timeouts above ~10 s. Nothing here is remotely near Modal's 150 s request cap.
73	
74	## 5. Two traps found while measuring
75	
76	1. **Scanning the episode Dict cost one RPC per episode.** With 125 stale episodes the sweep on the
77	   reset path added ~4.5 s to *every* reset (measured: reset p50 6.7 s → 3.3 s after the fix). The
78	   fix is `modal.Dict.items()` (one streamed call, 1.16 s for 125 records vs ~50 ms *per* `get`)
79	   plus the throttle. If you scan a Modal Dict anywhere else, do the same.
80	2. **`modal deploy` does not restart warm web containers.** `add_local_python_source(copy=False)`
81	   is mounted at container start, so a container that keeps receiving traffic keeps serving the old
82	   code — a new route 404'd for 9 minutes after a successful deploy while other sessions' polling
83	   kept the old container alive. Forcing a burst of concurrent requests (40+) starts new containers
84	   on the new build. Assert on `/health` (it now carries `episode_ttl_s`) before trusting a deploy.
85

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:18.910Z
1	# Error and status taxonomy (contract for the UI, the ledger and the evidence)
2	
3	Two questions must be answerable for every failure a run shows: **who caused it** (`origin`) and
4	**which layer really failed** (`layer`). The agent never gets these; the UI always does.
5	
6	## `ErrorClass` (on every `tool.result` with `is_error`, on `fault.fired`, on `RunRecord.error_class`)
7	
8	```json
9	{"origin": "injected|staged|real", "layer": "boundary|filesystem|sandbox|transport|harness|model|gym",
10	 "code": "ENOENT|EACCES|ETIMEDOUT|EINVAL|ESANDBOX|ETRANSPORT|EHARNESS|EMODEL|EGYM|EINTERNAL|ENOEPISODE",
11	 "kind": "missing_file|denied_write|ack_lost|null", "label": "<fixed string below>",
12	 "outcome_known": true|false, "side_effect_applied": true|false|null, "detail": "…"}
13	```
14	
15	| origin / layer | code(s) | label (render verbatim) | outcome_known | side effect |
16	|---|---|---|---|---|
17	| injected / boundary | ENOENT | `simulated: missing file (file still on disk)` | true | no |
18	| injected / boundary | EACCES | `simulated: write denied (nothing written)` | true | no |
19	| injected / boundary | ETIMEDOUT | `simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)` | **false** | **yes** |
20	| staged / filesystem | ENOENT | `staged: file absent since reset` | true | n/a |
21	| real / filesystem | ENOENT, EACCES, EINVAL | `real: OS error in sandbox` | true | no |
22	| real / sandbox | ESANDBOX | `real: sandbox terminated or unavailable` | true | no |
23	| real / transport | ETRANSPORT, ETIMEDOUT | `real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)` | **false** | unknown |
24	| real / harness | EHARNESS | `real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)` | **false** | unknown (ledger has truth after the fact) |
25	| real / model | EMODEL | `real: model API error` | true | — |
26	| real / gym | EGYM | `real: gym control-plane failure` | true | — |
27	| real / boundary | EINTERNAL, ENOEPISODE | `real: internal error in sandbox-env` | true | no |
28	
29	UI guidance: badge colour by `origin` (injected = amber "simulated", staged = amber "staged", real
30	= red "real"); show `label`; when `outcome_known: false` add "verify before retrying"; when the
31	ledger later resolves `side_effect_applied`, show it ("the write had landed").
32	
33	## Events
34	
35	- `fault.fired` — `FaultFired` + `origin` (`injected` or `staged`). Never `real`.
36	- `interruption` — `Interruption` (always real): `{step, layer, code, label, tool_use_id?, tool?, path?, outcome_known, planned, resumed, worker_generation, at, detail}`. `planned: true` means a scenario `harness_faults` entry triggered it (a deliberate real failure).
37	- `run.resumed` — `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}` emitted by the worker that picked the run up.
38	- `tool.result` — `data.error_class` present iff `is_error`; `data.fault` (FaultFired) present iff a fault fired for this call.
39	
40	## Run status
41	
42	| status | meaning | score |
43	|---|---|---|
44	| `ok` | the agent submitted or ended; evaluation succeeded | number |
45	| `truncated` | step budget exhausted; still evaluated | number |
46	| `unevaluated` | the loop finished but `evaluate` failed (`error_class.layer: gym` or `sandbox`) — **not** the agent's fault | null |
47	| `interrupted` | a real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it | null |
48	| `error` | the harness could not run the episode (model auth, reset failed, bug) | null |
49	
50	`RunRecord.interruptions[]` lists every real interruption, `worker_generation` is 1 + the number of
51	resumes; a resumed run can still end `ok` (that is the point of the `worker-crash` scenario).
52	
53	## Scenario metadata
54	
55	`Scenario.fault_kinds` (injected kinds in play) and `Scenario.harness_faults[]` (planned **real**
56	interruptions: `{kind: worker_crash|transport_abort, tool, path, nth, after_ms}`) are public so the
57	UI can show "includes a real harness interruption" on the card. The agent is never told either.
58	
59	## Ledger (released at evaluate)
60	
61	`LedgerEntry.origin` (`injected|staged|real`, only when `outcome != ok`), `error_code`, and
62	`interrupted: true` when the harness reported that it never received the response (`POST
63	/episodes/{id}/interruptions`). `verified_before_rewrite` treats `ack_lost` rows and `interrupted`
64	rows identically.

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:27.218Z
56	## 2. Design (decisions + research notes)
57	
58	### 2.1 Architecture and trust boundaries
59	
60	```
61	 browser (apps/web: Vite + shadcn + Beautiful UI, static on Modal Server; anonymous user id in localStorage + cookie mirror)
62	    │  HTTPS JSON + SSE (reconnecting, Last-Event-ID); every request carries X-Faultline-User
63	    ▼
64	 services/agent-harness  (Modal app "faultline-harness")
65	    ├─ api          : FastAPI @modal.asgi_app — /me, /conversations…, /runs…, GET /runs/{id}/events (SSE), /scenarios, /health
66	    │                 NO provider secret. Identifies the browser by X-Faultline-User. Spawns run_episode; reads/writes ONLY via Store.
67	    ├─ Store        : @app.cls(max_containers=1, volumes={"/data": faultline-db}) — the single SQLite writer (§2.9).
68	    │                 users / conversations / runs / events / messages / blocks / llm_calls. Hot DB on local disk,
69	    │                 consistent snapshots checkpointed to the Modal Volume. No secret, no fault plan.
70	    └─ run_episode  : @app.function(secrets=[anthropic-secret]) — the ONLY code that sees ANTHROPIC_API_KEY.
71	                      Owns the model loop (anthropic SDK, model from ANTHROPIC_MODEL / per-run override).
72	                      Talks to sandbox-env over MCP (streamable HTTP) + gym REST. Appends events to Store.
73	    │  HTTPS: gym REST (reset/observe/evaluate) + MCP /mcp (step)
74	    ▼
75	 services/sandbox-env  (Modal app "faultline-sandbox-env")   ← controller, fault plan, grader live HERE
76	    └─ api : FastAPI @modal.asgi_app with FastMCP mounted at /mcp. Stateless containers; episode state in
77	             modal.Dict "faultline-episodes" {sandbox_id, scenario, seed, fault_plan, ledger, baseline}.
78	             Every tool call: load episode → consult fault plan → (maybe) exec in the Modal Sandbox → record ledger.
79	    │  modal.Sandbox.from_id(sid).exec(...)   (block_network=True, no secrets, per-episode)
80	    ▼
81	 Modal Sandbox (the *command sandbox*): fixture repo at /workspace, python3 + pytest. Sees nothing else.
82	```
83	
84	Trust boundary summary (what each process can see):
85	
86	| Process | Provider key | Fault plan / grader | Workspace files | Network |
87	|---|---|---|---|---|
88	| browser | ✗ | ✗ (sees *events* about faults after the fact) | via observe API | harness only |
89	| harness `api` | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ | Store + spawn |
90	| harness `Store` | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ (only what events carry: diffs, outputs) | Volume only |
91	| harness `run_episode` | ✓ (secret `anthropic-secret`: `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`) | ✗ | via MCP only | Anthropic + sandbox-env |
92	| sandbox-env `api` | ✗ | ✓ | via Sandbox API | Modal Sandbox only |
93	| Modal Sandbox (agent's shell) | ✗ | ✗ | ✓ | **none** (`block_network=True`) |
94	
95	Why three deployables: the harness must be replaceable (different model/prompt) without touching
96	the environment; the environment must be gradeable without trusting the agent; the UI is static.
97	
98	### 2.2 Gym contract (sandbox-env)
99	
100	Gymnasium-style, but the *step* surface is MCP tools (so any MCP-speaking harness can play).
101	
102	REST (JSON) on `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run`:
103	
104	| Route | Purpose | Body → Response |
105	|---|---|---|
106	| `POST /episodes` (**reset**) | provision sandbox, copy fixture, apply sticky faults, store plan | `{scenario_id, seed?}` → `{episode_id, scenario: {id,title,task_prompt,max_steps}, workspace_root, files: [...]}` |
107	| `GET /episodes/{id}` (**observe**) | tree + diff vs baseline + public ledger (faults *fired* so far, without revealing pending ones) | → `{episode_id, step, files:[{path,size,sha,status:added/modified/deleted/unchanged}], diffs:[{path,unified}], faults_fired:[...], done}` |
108	| `POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate` | grader: upload hidden tests into sandbox, run pytest, remove; compute recovery metrics from ledger | → `{score, passed, checks:[{id,ok,detail}], tests:{passed,failed,output}, ledger:[...]}` |
109	| `DELETE /episodes/{id}` | terminate sandbox | → `{terminated:true}` |
110	| `GET /scenarios` | bundled scenario catalogue (public fields only) | |
111	| `GET /health` | | |
112	
113	MCP (streamable HTTP, stateless) at `/mcp`; episode selected by header `X-Faultline-Episode: <episode_id>`
114	(middleware → contextvar; tools never take the id as an argument so the model can't spoof another episode).
115	
116	| Tool | Input | Output (text + structured) | Faults that can apply |
117	|---|---|---|---|
118	| `run_command` | `{command: str, timeout_s?: int≤60}` | `{stdout, stderr, exit_code, duration_ms, truncated}` | `missing_file` (argv token match), `denied_write` (argv token match on mutating verbs) |
119	| `read_file` | `{path}` | `{content, size, sha256}` or ENOENT | `missing_file` |
120	| `write_file` | `{path, content, mode: "overwrite"\|"append"}` | `{bytes_written, sha256}` | `denied_write`, `ack_lost` |
121	| `list_dir` | `{path?}` | `{entries:[{name,type,size}]}` | `missing_file` (dir) |
122	
123	Every tool result is capped (`stdout`/`stderr` 8 KB each, `content` 32 KB) and marked `truncated`.
124	Every call is appended to the **ledger**: `{step, tool, args_digest, fault?:{kind,path,mode}, outcome, ts, duration_ms}`.
125	
126	**Fault plan schema** (per scenario, seeded; lives only in the Dict, never in the sandbox):
127	
128	```json
129	{"faults": [
130	  {"kind": "missing_file", "path": "config/settings.json", "mode": "sticky",    "hits": null},
131	  {"kind": "missing_file", "path": "README.md",            "mode": "transient", "hits": 1},
132	  {"kind": "denied_write", "path": "src/limits.py",        "mode": "transient", "hits": 2},
133	  {"kind": "ack_lost",     "path": "CHANGELOG.md",         "mode": "transient", "hits": 1, "delay_ms": 3000}
134	]}
135	```
136	
137	How each fault is realised **at the tool boundary** (sandbox-env, outside the shell):
138	
139	| Kind | Reset | On matching call | What the agent sees | Recovery we grade |
140	|---|---|---|---|---|
141	| `missing_file` sticky | file deleted from fixture before agent starts | — | `ENOENT` from cat/read | agent recreates it from the spec in README/tests; hidden tests pass |
142	| `missing_file` transient | nothing | first `hits` reads short-circuit with ENOENT (no exec) | flaky FS | agent retries/lists dir instead of giving up |
143	| `denied_write` | nothing | first `hits` writes short-circuit with `EACCES: Permission denied`; **write not performed** | locked file | agent retries; final content correct; ≤1 wasted retry |
144	| `ack_lost` | nothing | **write IS performed**, then sleep `delay_ms`, then return `is_error` "504: response timed out; operation may or may not have completed" | ambiguous timeout | agent **verifies** (read/cat/grep/sha) before retrying; **no duplicate append**; tests pass |
145	
146	**Score** = 60 · tests_pass + 40 · recovery (checks weighted equally per scenario), reported with a
147	per-check breakdown. Step budget `max_steps` (default 20) → `truncated` if exceeded.
148	
149	### 2.3 Harness loop (agent-harness/run_episode)
150	
151	- SDK: `anthropic` (Python). Client: `anthropic.Anthropic()` (reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from the secret).
152	- Model: `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` env (default `claude-haiku-4-5`), per-run override from an allowlist
153	  (`claude-haiku-4-5`, `claude-sonnet-5`, `claude-opus-5`). Thinking: adaptive on models that support it; Haiku 4.5 = off.
154	- Tools: discovered from MCP `list_tools` and converted to Anthropic tool defs (+ a local `submit` tool
155	  that ends the episode with a summary). Parallel tool calls executed in order, all results returned in one user message.
156	- Loop: manual `while stop_reason == "tool_use"` (no beta dependency), `max_steps`, per-call timeouts,
157	  typed error chain (`RateLimitError` → retry w/ backoff; `APIStatusError ≥500` → retry; else fail the run cleanly).
158	- System prompt strategy (recovery-oriented): one command per step, verify state after any error,
159	  after a timeout **read before re-writing**, prefer idempotent edits, run tests before submitting.
160	- Events appended to the Store (`Store().append_events.remote(run_id, [events])`, batched per step,
161	  idempotent on `(run_id, seq)`) as they happen. The loop also keeps them in memory and re-sends the
162	  full list at `run.finished`, so a Store restart self-heals (§2.9):
163	
164	```
165	run.started {run_id, scenario, model, seed}
166	episode.reset {episode_id, files}
167	turn.text {step, text}                       # assistant prose
168	tool.call {step, tool, input, tool_use_id}
169	tool.result {step, tool_use_id, output, is_error, duration_ms, fault?}   # fault echoed by sandbox-env public ledger
170	llm.call {step, attempt, model, stop_reason, usage:{input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens, cache_creation_input_tokens}, request_id, duration_ms}   # one per messages.create — the "LLM turn"
171	turn.thinking {step, text}                   # thinking block (summary) when adaptive thinking is on
172	fault.fired {step, kind, path, mode}         # emitted by harness after reading ledger delta
173	workspace.diff {step, files, diffs}          # from observe, after each mutating step
174	episode.evaluated {score, checks, tests}
175	run.finished {status: ok|error|truncated, usage:{input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms}
176	log {level, msg, ...}                        # verbose unified log lines mirrored into the stream
177	```
178	
179	Why not run the loop inside the SSE request: Modal web endpoints cap requests at **150 s**
180	(303-redirect polling after that breaks CORS). So `POST /runs` → `run_episode.spawn(...)` and the
181	browser tails `GET /runs/{id}/events` via SSE (the `api` polls `Store.events_after(run_id, after_seq)`
182	every 400 ms); the server closes each stream at ~110 s and the browser reconnects with
183	`Last-Event-ID` (= `events.seq`). `GET /runs/{id}` returns the whole record (also what we save as
184	evidence).
185	
186	### 2.4 Modal deployment shapes (verified against modal 1.5.5 locally)
187	
188	- Workspace `appliedlabsai`, environment `local` (web suffix `local`) → URLs
189	  `https://appliedlabsai-local--<app>-<fn>.modal.run` (label truncation at 63 chars).
190	- sandbox-env: `@app.function(image=IMG, timeout=300) @modal.concurrent(max_inputs=20) @modal.asgi_app()` → `api`.
191	  Sandboxes: `modal.Sandbox.create(app=modal.App.lookup("faultline-sandboxes", create_if_missing=True), image=SANDBOX_IMG, workdir="/workspace", timeout=1800, idle_timeout=600, block_network=True, cpu=1, memory=1024)`;
192	  exec via `sb.exec("bash","-lc",cmd, timeout=…)`; files via `sb.filesystem.write_text/read_text` (verify names against 1.5.5).
193	- harness: `api` as above (no secrets); `run_episode` = `@app.function(image=IMG, secrets=[modal.Secret.from_name("anthropic-secret")], timeout=900)`.
194	  Env: `SANDBOX_ENV_URL` (deploy-time env or `modal.Function.from_name("faultline-sandbox-env","api").get_web_url()` at runtime).
195	- harness store: `vol = modal.Volume.from_name("faultline-db", create_if_missing=True)` (v1 — v2 needs an explicit
196	  `version=2` and the 1.5.5 CLI still flags it experimental; it buys nothing for one file).
197	  `@app.cls(image=IMG, volumes={"/data": vol}, max_containers=1, min_containers=1, timeout=60) @modal.concurrent(max_inputs=32) class Store`
198	  with `@modal.enter` (restore snapshot → `/tmp`, migrate), `@modal.exit` (checkpoint), one `@modal.method` per operation.
199	  Same-app callers: `await Store().events_after.remote.aio(...)` from `api`, `Store().append_events.remote(...)` from `run_episode`.
200	  Local entrypoint `checkpoint_now` forces a snapshot for evidence/export (§5).
201	- web: `@app.server(image=WEB_IMG, unauthenticated=True, port=8000, min_containers=1)` class with `@modal.enter` → `python -m http.server -d /site 8000`.
202	  Image builds the Vite app: **Node 22** (Vite 8 needs `^20.19 || >=22.12`, vitest 5 needs `^22.12 || ^24`; `.nvmrc`=22) + pnpm installed in image, `add_local_dir("apps/web", copy=True)`, `run_commands("pnpm i --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build")` with `VITE_HARNESS_URL` baked in **and** a runtime `config.json` fallback so the URL can change without a rebuild.
203	  Fallback if Server misbehaves: `@modal.web_server(8000)`.
204	- Deploy order: sandbox-env → harness → web. Commands in §5.
205	- Secret: `anthropic-secret` (keys `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`), env `local`. Attached **only** to `run_episode`.
206	  Guard: `run_episode` logs `has_key=True/False` and `workspace=<masked>`; `api` asserts the key is *absent* in its env at startup.
207	
208	### 2.5 Unified logging
209	
210	One line per event, JSON, to stdout in every service (Modal captures → `modal app logs <app> -e local`),
211	mirrored to the browser console in the web app with the same shape:
212	
213	```
214	{"ts":"2026-09-12T14:03:22.114Z","svc":"sandbox-env","lvl":"info","run_id":"r_…","episode_id":"ep_…","step":4,"ev":"tool.result","tool":"write_file","fault":"ack_lost","dur_ms":3041,"msg":"write landed; ack withheld"}
215	```
216	
217	Fields: `ts svc lvl run_id episode_id step ev msg` + free extras. `LOG_LEVEL=debug` turns on
218	argument/output dumps (truncated). Shared implementation in `packages/common/faultline_common/log.py`
219	(added to each image via `add_local_python_source`).
220	
221	### 2.6 Scenarios (bundled fixture: tiny Python package `ratelimiter/` with pytest tests)
222	
223	| id | Title | Task prompt (abridged) | Faults | Recovery checks |
224	|---|---|---|---|---|
225	| `missing-config` | Restore the deleted config | tests fail because `config/settings.json` is gone; README documents its schema. Recreate it and make tests pass. | `missing_file` sticky on config; transient on README (1 hit) | recreated file valid; tests pass; agent retried README read |
226	| `locked-file` | Fix the off-by-one under a locked file | fix `src/limits.py` bug; the file is temporarily locked | `denied_write` ×2 on `src/limits.py` | eventual write succeeded; ≤3 write attempts; tests pass |
227	| `lost-ack` | Append the changelog entry | add a CHANGELOG entry + bump version; write ack is lost once | `ack_lost` on `CHANGELOG.md` | verification read between timeout and next write; exactly one entry; tests pass |
228	| `gauntlet` (stretch) | all three | | all | all |
229	
230	
231	### 2.7 Verified library facts (measured locally 2026-09-12; binding for implementation)
232	
233	- **MCP**: use `fastmcp==4.0.3` for both server and client. The official `mcp` 2.x package renamed `FastMCP`→`MCPServer` and moved the streamable-HTTP client; avoid it. Server: `FastMCP("…").http_app(path="/mcp", transport="http", stateless_http=True)` → Starlette app; mount into FastAPI with `FastAPI(lifespan=mcp_app.router.lifespan_context)`. Per-request header inside a tool: `fastmcp.server.dependencies.get_http_headers()`. Client: `fastmcp.Client(StreamableHttpTransport(url, headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": id}))`, `await c.call_tool(name, args, timeout=…, raise_on_error=False)` → `.content/.is_error/.structured_content`.
234	- **Anthropic SDK** `anthropic==1.5.0`: `anthropic.Anthropic()` reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; manual loop on `messages.create`; all tool results for a turn in one user message; Haiku 4.5 takes no `thinking` param; Sonnet 5 / Opus 5 → `thinking={"type":"adaptive"}`; never `budget_tokens`.
235	- **Modal 1.5.5**: `@app.server(image=…, unauthenticated=True, port=8000, min_containers=…, startup_timeout=…, name=…)`, `modal.Server.from_name(app, name).get_url()`; `Sandbox.create(app=, image=, workdir=, timeout=, idle_timeout=, block_network=, cpu=, memory=)`, `sb.exec(*argv, timeout=, workdir=, env=)`, `sb.open/mkdir/ls/rm`, `Sandbox.from_id`; `modal.Dict.from_name(…, create_if_missing=True)` with `get/put/contains/pop`; `Function.spawn` → `FunctionCall`; `Image.add_local_python_source`, `add_local_dir(copy=True)`, `uv_pip_install`. Web request cap **150 s** (303 redirect after; breaks CORS).
236	- **Secret**: `anthropic-secret` in env `local` (created by user 2026-09-12 17:11) exposes `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`. **The key is org-scoped: every request must carry the `anthropic-workspace-id: <ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE>` header or the API returns 400** (found by deploying; the client sets `default_headers`). Surfaced masked in run metadata. Older `anthropic-api-key` (2024) exists too; ignore it.
237	- **Research-pass gotchas (verified, sent to builders 17:45 EDT)**: FastMCP 4.x mount uses `FastAPI(lifespan=mcp_app.lifespan)` and needs `host_origin_protection=False` (DNS-rebinding guard rejects public hosts otherwise); MCP client URL needs the trailing slash `/mcp/`; raise `fastmcp.exceptions.ToolError` for injected faults (generic exceptions become opaque); a non-zero exit code is **not** `is_error`; keep the `missing_file` illusion consistent in listings; `sb.open()` is deprecated → `sb.filesystem.write_bytes/read_text`; never put secrets on `modal.App(...)`/`Image.env` (propagates to every function); `Secret.from_name(..., required_keys=["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"])`; `@app.server` has no `label`/`timeout` and 503s at zero containers → `min_containers=1`; Servers bypass the 150 s cap (Web Functions don't); `add_local_dir(copy=False)` mounts after build → prebuilt `dist/` path is faster; Haiku 4.5 rejects `effort` and adaptive thinking; cache the system prompt with `cache_control` and keep tool order stable.
238	- **Considered, not adopted**: per-episode bearer tokens for MCP (our header is set by the harness, never the model, and the sandbox has no network — equivalent trust); `mcp` 2.x official SDK (renamed APIs, fastmcp 4 is simpler); subprocess-in-container workspaces instead of Modal Sandboxes (cheaper, but real isolation is the point of the demo); grading Δpass@1 / PRR across seeds (stretch: batch mode).
239	- **Research synthesis**: see §2.8.

427	### 2.11 Failure provenance and real interruptions (added 2026-09-12 18:35 EDT)
428	
429	The agent keeps seeing OS/HTTP-style errors; everything else gets provenance. Canonical types in
430	`schemas.py` (`ErrorClass`, `ToolOutcome`, `Interruption`, `HarnessFault`, `InterruptionReport`,
431	run statuses `unevaluated` / `interrupted`), contract for the UI in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`,
432	truthful per-injector semantics in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.
433	
434	- **origin**: `injected` (short-circuited or ack-withheld at the boundary; nothing real failed) ·
435	  `staged` (the scenario really deleted the file at reset; the ENOENT is a real OS error) ·
436	  `real` (unplanned failure of sandbox / transport / harness / model / gym / filesystem).
437	- **outcome** per tool result: `executed | failed | not_executed | unknown`.
438	- **codes**: `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL` (agent-facing, same text either way) +
439	  `ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` (real only).
440	- **scenario `worker-crash`**: the harness worker really dies (`os._exit`) while its first
441	  changelog write is in flight; Modal retries `run_episode`; the new worker resumes from the
442	  persisted events, tells the gym (`POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions`) and hands the agent an
443	  `EHARNESS` result with outcome unknown. Grading reuses `verified_before_rewrite`.
444	- Real specimen that motivated this: run `r_ccda8780cbee` (sandbox killed by a concurrent `reap`)
445	  ended `ok` with `score: null` labelled `EINTERNAL` → must become `interrupted` / `ESANDBOX`.
446	- Checklist: [ ] sandbox-env provenance + `ESANDBOX` + staged `faults_fired` + interruptions route +
447	  scenario passthrough + safe `reap` · [ ] harness classification + statuses + resume + `worker_crash`

30	## 2. Topology and trust boundaries
31	
32	```
33	 browser  (apps/web on Modal Server)  ── anonymous id in localStorage + cookie mirror
34	    │  HTTPS JSON + SSE (reconnecting, Last-Event-ID); every request: X-Faultline-User: u_<uuid>
35	    ▼
36	 faultline-harness
37	    ├─ api          FastAPI @modal.asgi_app. No provider secret. Validates the user id, owns the HTTP
38	    │               contract (§5), spawns run_episode, and reads/writes state ONLY through Store.
39	    ├─ Store        @app.cls(max_containers=1, min_containers=1, volumes={"/data": faultline-db}).
40	    │               The single SQLite writer (§4). No secret, no fault plan, no network beyond Modal.
41	    └─ run_episode  @app.function(secrets=[anthropic-secret]). The ONLY code that sees ANTHROPIC_API_KEY.
42	                    Model loop over MCP tools; appends events to Store; evaluates; always deletes the episode.
43	    │  HTTPS: gym REST (reset / observe / evaluate / delete) + MCP streamable HTTP at /mcp (step)
44	    ▼
45	 faultline-sandbox-env
46	    └─ api          FastAPI + FastMCP. Stateless containers; episode state {sandbox_id, fault_plan,
47	                    ledger, baseline} in modal.Dict "faultline-episodes". Every tool call: load episode →
48	                    consult fault plan → (maybe) exec in the Sandbox → append to the ledger.
49	    │  modal.Sandbox.from_id(sid).exec(...)   block_network=True, no secrets, one per episode
50	    ▼
51	 Modal Sandbox      fixture repo at /workspace, python3 + pytest. Sees nothing else.
52	```
53	
54	What each process can see:
55	
56	| Process | Provider key | Fault plan / grader | Workspace files | Persistent DB | Network |
57	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
58	| browser | ✗ | ✗ (events about faults, after the fact) | via observe/events | via api only | harness only |
59	| harness `api` | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ | via Store RPC | Store + spawn |
60	| harness `Store` | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ (only what events carry) | ✓ (sole writer) | Volume only |
61	| harness `run_episode` | ✓ | ✗ | via MCP only | via Store RPC | Anthropic + sandbox-env |
62	| sandbox-env `api` | ✗ | ✓ | via Sandbox API | ✗ | Modal Sandbox only |
63	| Modal Sandbox (agent's shell) | ✗ | ✗ | ✓ | ✗ | none |
64	
65	Why three apps: the harness must be replaceable (different model / prompt) without touching the
66	environment; the environment must be gradeable without trusting the agent; the UI is static. The secret
67	boundary is enforced by Modal's per-function secrets: `api` asserts at startup that `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`
68	is *absent* from its environment and reports `has_provider_key: false` on `/health`.
69

373	### 5.2 Sandbox-env (gym + MCP)
374	
375	Unchanged from `PLAN.md` §2.2: REST `POST /episodes` (reset), `GET /episodes/{id}` (observe),
376	`POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate`, `DELETE /episodes/{id}`, `GET /scenarios`, `GET /health`; MCP tools
377	`run_command`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `list_dir` at `/mcp`, episode selected by the
378	`X-Faultline-Episode` header so the model can never name another episode.
379	
380	### 5.3 Event stream
381	
382	Event types and payloads are defined in `faultline_common.schemas.Event` (`run.started`, `episode.reset`,
383	`turn.thinking`, `turn.text`, `tool.call`, `tool.result`, `llm.call`, `fault.fired`, `workspace.diff`,
384	`episode.evaluated`, `run.finished`, `log`). `Event.id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and doubles
385	as the SSE id, the `events.seq` primary key, and the resume cursor. Events are produced by exactly one
386	writer per run (`run_episode`) and are never edited.
387	
388	### 5.4 Reliability event contract (confirmed with the harness lead 2026-09-12 ~22:45 EDT)
389	
390	Canonical: `packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py` (the `Event` docstring lists every data
391	shape), labels in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`, injector behaviour in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.
392	The UI is a ten-second reliability read — *task → fault (simulated or real, which layer) → did the
393	command execute (executed / failed / not executed / unknown are four different answers) → recovery
394	(agent actions vs harness retries; did the sandbox survive) → diff + independent checks* — and it
395	**never derives an outcome from error text**: anything absent renders as "unknown" with the raw
396	payload expandable. Fields (all additive):
397	
398	| Where | Field | Meaning |
399	|---|---|---|
400	| `tool.result.data` | `outcome: executed \| failed \| not_executed \| unknown` | executed = ran and succeeded; failed = ran and reported failure; not_executed = refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it; unknown = may or may not have run (ack lost, transport, worker interrupted) |
401	| `tool.result.data` | `error_class` (iff `is_error`): `{origin: injected\|staged\|real, layer: boundary\|filesystem\|sandbox\|transport\|harness\|model\|gym, code, kind?, label, outcome_known, side_effect_applied?, detail?}` | provenance and layer; `label` is a fixed string rendered verbatim; `error_code` kept for compatibility; raw text stays in `output` |
402	| `tool.result.data` | `attempts`, `sandbox: {id, alive}` | harness-level retries of this call (1 = none); identity/liveness of the workspace that answered |
403	| `fault.fired.data` | `origin` (injected \| staged, never real), `layer` (boundary for injected, filesystem for staged), `description` | one plain sentence per fault |
404	| `episode.reset.data` | `sandbox_id` (short), `attempt` | workspace identity; >1 when re-provisioned |
405	| `episode.sandbox` (new) | `{sandbox_id, status: alive\|terminated\|replaced, reason, step}` | the environment noticed the worker changed |
406	| `interruption` (new) | `Interruption {step?, layer, code, label, tool_use_id?, tool?, path?, outcome_known, planned, resumed, worker_generation, at, detail?}` | always real; `planned: true` when a scenario `harness_faults` entry triggered it |
407	| `run.resumed` (new) | `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}` | a fresh worker picked the run up |
408	| `run.finished.data` | `evaluation_status: ok\|failed\|skipped`, `evaluation_error?`, `steps`, `score?` | a run that could not be graded is `unevaluated`, never "ok" |
409	| `RunStatus` | `+ unevaluated`, `+ interrupted` | "ok" now implies a score |
410	| `RunRecord` | `error_class?`, `interruptions[]`, `worker_generation` | why the run ended as it did |
411	| `GET /scenarios` | `checks: [{id, description, weight}]`, `faults_public: [{kind, origin, layer, description}]`, `harness_faults: [{kind: worker_crash\|transport_abort, tool, path, nth, after_ms}]` | intended outcome and fault classes known before the run; the agent never sees them |
412	| codes (final) | `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` | `ETIMEDOUT` is ack-lost *or* a real client timeout — origin/layer tell them apart |
413	
414	Older records (e.g. `r_e9bc5c8c6739`) predate these fields; the UI resolves what it can from the
415	evaluate ledger (`outcome: ack_lost` ⇒ the write landed, `short_circuit` ⇒ nothing ran) and says
416	"unknown" otherwise. Web-side model: `apps/web/src/lib/reliability.ts` (`buildVerdict`), rendered by
417	`src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.tsx`.

526	## 8. Deployment and configuration
527	
528	| Item | Value |
529	|---|---|
530	| Workspace / environment | `appliedlabsai` / `local` → web functions at `https://appliedlabsai-local--<app>-<fn>.modal.run`; the static site is a Modal **Server**, whose URL is `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` (read it back with `modal.Server.from_name("faultline-web", "site").get_url()`; the `.modal.run` shape answers 404 for Servers) |
531	| Deploy order | sandbox-env → harness (creates Volume `faultline-db` on first deploy) → web. Live URLs (2026-09-12): harness `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run`, sandbox-env `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run`, web `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` |
532	| Secret | `anthropic-secret` (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`; name overridable via `FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET`), attached only to `run_episode` |
533	| Volume | `faultline-db` (v1), mounted at `/data` on `Store` only |
534	| Env | `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (default `claude-haiku-4-5`), `SANDBOX_ENV_URL`, `HARNESS_URL`, `LOG_LEVEL` (see `.env.example`) |
535	| Warm containers | web Server, harness `api`, harness `Store` (`min_containers=1`) |
536	| Web image | Node 22 + pnpm; `pnpm i --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build`; `config.json` written from `HARNESS_URL` at container start |
537	| Sandbox guardrails | `timeout=1800`, `idle_timeout=600`, `block_network=True`, delete in `finally`, `reap` helper |
538	| Export | `modal run … ::checkpoint_now` then `modal volume get faultline-db faultline.sqlite3 runs/` |
539	
540	## 9. Decision log
541	
542	| # | Decision | Alternatives considered | Why |
543	|---|---|---|---|
544	| 1 | Three Modal apps with a strict secret boundary | one app | harness swappable, environment gradeable without trusting the agent, static UI; the provider key exists in exactly one function |
545	| 2 | Faults injected at the tool boundary, not inside the shell | `chmod`, deleting files live, network faults | deterministic, seedable, invisible to the agent, gradeable from a ledger; ack-lost is only expressible at the boundary |
546	| 3 | `spawn` + event log + reconnecting SSE | run the loop inside the request | Modal web requests cap at 150 s |
547	| 4 | Persistence = SQLite on a Modal Volume | Modal Dict only; hosted Postgres; Modal Dict as hot path + SQLite archive | user asked for it; zero external services; a single file is trivial to export as evidence |
548	| 5 | Single-writer `Store` actor (`max_containers=1`) | every function opens the DB on the Volume; per-run DB files | Volumes have no distributed locking and last-write-wins; one process owning the file removes every consistency question |
549	| 6 | Hot DB on local disk, `VACUUM INTO` snapshots renamed atomically onto the Volume | run SQLite directly on the FUSE mount | WAL/locking on FUSE is undocumented, and background commits are not multi-file snapshots; Modal's own example copies-then-commits |
550	| 7 | Volume v1 | v2 | v2 needs an explicit flag and is marked experimental in the 1.5.5 CLI; no benefit for one file |
551	| 8 | `events` as source of truth + `messages`/`blocks`/`llm_calls` projections | events only; projections only | replay/SSE need the log; the UI and "what did the model see" need the message shape; projections are a cheap same-transaction write and a listed scope cut |
552	| 9 | Identity via `X-Faultline-User` header, minted in the browser, mirrored to a cookie | cookie-only; server-minted id | the API is cross-origin so cookies never reach it; localStorage is the reliable store; the cookie is a backup |
553	| 10 | Beautiful UI by copy-paste with lucide/shadcn substitutions | Vercel AI Elements (a real registry); shadcn only | matches the requested look; MIT; the private deps are small and swappable; scope cut #4 if it drags |
554	| 11 | Haiku 4.5 default, Sonnet/Opus 5 allowlisted | Sonnet default | cost and latency for a demo; the ack-lost scenario is more interesting when a cheaper model sometimes fails it |
555	| 12 | No client router; conversation/run/demo in query params | `react-router-dom` | the scaffold already does this and it is enough for three screens; one less dependency in a static SPA |
556	| 13 | Node 22 pinned (`.nvmrc`, `engines`, Modal image) | stay on the machine default 21.6 | Vite 8 and vitest 5 refuse to start on 21.6 (`styleText` missing from `node:util`) |
557	
558	## 10. Open questions
559	
560	- Should the composer allow editing the task prompt, or only choosing a scenario? Current answer: editable,
561	  stored in `runs.task_prompt`, because it makes the "conversation" framing honest. Revisit if it invites
562	  prompt-injection-shaped demos that distract from the reliability story.
563	- Do we need per-user rate limiting beyond `POST …/runs`? Probably not for review traffic.
564	- Checkpoint cadence (15 s) vs. cost of `VACUUM INTO` as the DB grows — measure once real runs exist and
565	  record the number in `PLAN.md` §0.

1	#!/usr/bin/env python
2	"""Prove the cost guardrails against the LIVE deployment. No model, no secrets.
3	
4	    services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python services/sandbox-env/tools/lifecycle_proof.py \
5	        --out runs/<ts>_lifecycle
6	
7	Four claims, each checked against the deployed service rather than a fake:
8	
9	    A  `GET /episodes` lists real episodes and tells the truth about sandbox liveness.
10	    B  the TTL sweep terminates an abandoned episode (one aged past EPISODE_TTL_S) and nothing else.
11	    C  `reap(keep_active=True)` terminates a stray and SPARES episodes that are still live —
12	       this is the safety property that the `r_ccda8780cbee` incident (a reap killing a running
13	       run's sandbox) says we need.
14	    D  a reaped sandbox is written back to the episode Dict, so the ops listing never keeps
15	       claiming a dead sandbox is alive.
16	
17	To age an episode out, the script edits ONLY the episodes it created itself, directly in the
18	`faultline-episodes` Dict — the same thing "an owner that never came back" produces, without
19	waiting 30 minutes for it. Every episode it creates is deleted at the end, whatever happens.
20	"""
21	
22	from __future__ import annotations
23	
24	import argparse
25	import json
26	import sys
27	import time
28	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
29	from pathlib import Path
30	from typing import Any
31	
32	import httpx
33	
34	HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
35	REPO_ROOT = HERE.parents[2]
36	for _p in (str(REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"), str(HERE.parent)):
37	    if _p not in sys.path:
38	        sys.path.insert(0, _p)
39	
40	from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402
41	
42	log = get_logger("script")
43	
44	DEFAULT_BASE = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run"
45	APP = "faultline-sandbox-env"
46	ENV = "local"
47	DICT_NAME = "faultline-episodes"
48	
49	
50	class Proof:
51	    def __init__(self, out: Path) -> None:
52	        self.out = out
53	        self.checks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
54	        self.record: dict[str, Any] = {"started_at": now_iso(), "checks": self.checks}
55	
56	    def check(self, name: str, ok: bool, detail: Any = "") -> bool:
57	        self.checks.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail})
58	        log.info("proof.check", f"{name}", check=name, ok=bool(ok), detail=str(detail)[:400])
59	        return bool(ok)
60	
61	    @property
62	    def passed(self) -> bool:
63	        return bool(self.checks) and all(c["ok"] for c in self.checks)
64	
65	
66	def now_iso() -> str:
67	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
68	
69	
70	def iso_ago(seconds: float) -> str:
71	    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=seconds)
72	    return ts.isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
73	
74	
75	def listing(http: httpx.Client, probe: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
76	    r = http.get("/episodes", params={"probe": str(probe).lower(), "limit": 500}, timeout=180)
77	    r.raise_for_status()
78	    return r.json()
79	
80	
81	def row_of(listing_body: dict[str, Any], episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
82	    for e in listing_body.get("episodes", []):
83	        if e["episode_id"] == episode_id:
84	            return e
85	    return None
86

88	def main() -> int:
89	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
90	                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
91	    ap.add_argument("--base", default=DEFAULT_BASE)
92	    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
93	    args = ap.parse_args()
94	
95	    stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ", time.gmtime())
96	    out = Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{stamp}_lifecycle"
97	    if not out.is_absolute():
98	        out = REPO_ROOT / out
99	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
100	    proof = Proof(out)
101	
102	    import modal
103	
104	    episodes_dict = modal.Dict.from_name(DICT_NAME, environment_name=ENV, create_if_missing=True)
105	    reap_fn = modal.Function.from_name(APP, "reap", environment_name=ENV)
106	    sweep_fn = modal.Function.from_name(APP, "sweep", environment_name=ENV)
107	
108	    created: list[str] = []
109	    http = httpx.Client(base_url=args.base, timeout=180)
110	    try:
111	        # ---- A: the ops listing sees a real, live episode -----------------------------------
112	        stray_a = http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack"}).json()
113	        created.append(stray_a["episode_id"])
114	        keeper = http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack"}).json()
115	        created.append(keeper["episode_id"])
116	        proof.record["episodes"] = {"swept": stray_a["episode_id"], "keeper": keeper["episode_id"]}
117	
118	        before = listing(http)
119	        proof.record["listing_before"] = {"count": before["count"], "live": before["live"],
120	                                          "ttl_s": before["ttl_s"]}
121	        ra, rk = row_of(before, stray_a["episode_id"]), row_of(before, keeper["episode_id"])
122	        proof.check("A.listing_has_both_episodes", ra is not None and rk is not None)
123	        proof.check("A.fresh_episodes_report_alive", bool(ra and ra["alive"]) and bool(rk and rk["alive"]),
124	                    {"stray": ra, "keeper": rk})
125	        proof.check("A.listing_reports_the_ttl", before["ttl_s"] > 0, before["ttl_s"])
126	
127	        # ---- B: the TTL sweep reclaims an abandoned episode ----------------------------------
128	        ep = episodes_dict.get(stray_a["episode_id"])
129	        ep["created_at"] = iso_ago(before["ttl_s"] + 600)  # "abandoned 10 min past the TTL"
130	        episodes_dict.put(stray_a["episode_id"], ep)
131	
132	        swept = sweep_fn.remote()
133	        proof.record["sweep"] = swept
134	        proof.check("B.sweep_expired_the_abandoned_episode",
135	                    stray_a["episode_id"] in swept["expired"], swept["expired"])
136	        proof.check("B.sweep_spared_the_live_episode",
137	                    keeper["episode_id"] not in swept["expired"], keeper["episode_id"])
138	
139	        after_sweep = listing(http)
140	        ra, rk = row_of(after_sweep, stray_a["episode_id"]), row_of(after_sweep, keeper["episode_id"])
141	        proof.check("B.swept_sandbox_is_dead", bool(ra) and ra["alive"] is False and ra["terminated"], ra)
142	        proof.check("B.keeper_sandbox_still_alive", bool(rk) and rk["alive"] is True, rk)
143	
144	        # ---- C/D: reap spares live episodes, kills strays, and records what it killed --------
145	        stray_c = http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": "missing-config"}).json()
146	        created.append(stray_c["episode_id"])
147	        proof.record["episodes"]["reaped"] = stray_c["episode_id"]
148	        ep = episodes_dict.get(stray_c["episode_id"])
149	        ep["created_at"] = iso_ago(before["ttl_s"] + 600)
150	        episodes_dict.put(stray_c["episode_id"], ep)
151	
152	        dry = reap_fn.remote(keep_active=True, dry_run=True)
153	        proof.record["reap_dry_run"] = dry
154	        proof.check("C.dry_run_targets_the_stray",
155	                    stray_c["sandbox_id"] in dry["terminated"], dry["terminated"][:10])
156	        proof.check("C.dry_run_spares_the_live_sandbox",
157	                    keeper["sandbox_id"] in dry["skipped"], dry["skipped"][:10])
158	
159	        live_before_reap = listing(http)["live"]
160	        reaped = reap_fn.remote(keep_active=True)
161	        proof.record["reap"] = reaped
162	        proof.check("C.reap_terminated_the_stray",
163	                    stray_c["sandbox_id"] in reaped["terminated"], reaped["terminated"][:10])
164	        proof.check("C.reap_spared_the_live_sandbox",
165	                    keeper["sandbox_id"] in reaped["skipped"], reaped["skipped"][:10])
166	        proof.check("D.reap_marked_the_episode_dict",
167	                    stray_c["episode_id"] in reaped["episodes_marked"], reaped["episodes_marked"])
168	
169	        after_reap = listing(http)
170	        rc, rk = row_of(after_reap, stray_c["episode_id"]), row_of(after_reap, keeper["episode_id"])
171	        proof.check("D.reaped_episode_reads_as_dead",
172	                    bool(rc) and rc["alive"] is False and rc["terminated"] is True, rc)
173	        proof.check("C.keeper_survived_the_reap", bool(rk) and rk["alive"] is True, rk)
174	        proof.record["listing_after"] = {"count": after_reap["count"], "live": after_reap["live"],
175	                                         "live_before_reap": live_before_reap}
176	
177	        # keeper still works after all of that
178	        obs = http.get(f"/episodes/{keeper['episode_id']}")
179	        proof.check("C.keeper_is_still_usable", obs.status_code == 200, obs.status_code)
180	    finally:
181	        deletes = {}
182	        for eid in created:
183	            try:
184	                r = http.delete(f"/episodes/{eid}")
185	                deletes[eid] = r.json() if r.status_code == 200 else r.status_code
186	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
187	                deletes[eid] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
188	        proof.record["deletes"] = deletes
189	        proof.check("cleanup.every_episode_deleted",
190	                    all(isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("terminated") for v in deletes.values()),
191	                    deletes)
192	        final = listing(http)
193	        mine_live = [e["episode_id"] for e in final["episodes"]
194	                     if e.get("alive") and e["episode_id"] in created]
195	        proof.check("cleanup.none_of_my_sandboxes_left_running", not mine_live, mine_live)
196	        proof.record.update(finished_at=now_iso(), PROOF=proof.passed,
197	                            other_sessions_live=[e["episode_id"] for e in final["episodes"]
198	                                                 if e.get("alive") and e["episode_id"] not in created])
199	        (out / "lifecycle_proof.json").write_text(json.dumps(proof.record, indent=2, default=str))
200	        http.close()
201	
202	    ok = sum(1 for c in proof.checks if c["ok"])
203	    print(f"\n{'LIFECYCLE PROOF PASS' if proof.passed else 'LIFECYCLE PROOF FAIL'} :: "
204	          f"{ok}/{len(proof.checks)} checks, out={out / 'lifecycle_proof.json'}")
205	    for c in proof.checks:
206	        if not c["ok"]:
207	            print(f"  ! {c['name']}: {c['detail']}")
208	    return 0 if proof.passed else 1

1	#!/usr/bin/env python
2	"""Measure sandbox-env latency against a live deployment. No model, no secrets.
3	
4	    services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python services/sandbox-env/tools/perf_probe.py \
5	        --samples 5 --out runs/<ts>_perf
6	
7	What it measures, per sample, from a plain HTTP/MCP client (so the numbers are what the *harness*
8	experiences, not what the server thinks it spent):
9	
10	    reset          POST /episodes                     sandbox create + fixture upload + baseline
11	    list_dir       MCP list_dir  {"path": "."}
12	    read_file      MCP read_file {"path": "README.md"}
13	    write_file     MCP write_file to a scratch path   (never a fault target)
14	    run_command    MCP run_command "echo … && ls"     one shell round trip
15	    run_pytest     MCP run_command "python -m pytest -q"
16	    observe        GET /episodes/{id}                 sha map + diff of the whole tree
17	    evaluate       POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate       upload hidden tests, run pytest, remove
18	    delete         DELETE /episodes/{id}              terminate the sandbox
19	    list_episodes  GET /episodes?probe=true           the ops listing (one poll per live episode)
20	
21	Each sample is a *fresh episode*, so `reset` is measured cold every time and the per-tool numbers
22	are not biased by a warm workspace someone else left behind. The scenario is `lost-ack`, whose only
23	fault targets CHANGELOG.md — every path this probe touches is fault-free, so the timings are the
24	system's real cost and not a 3 s injected delay.
25	
26	Every episode is deleted in a `finally`, and the probe re-lists at the end to prove none leaked.
27	"""
28	
29	from __future__ import annotations
30	
31	import argparse
32	import asyncio
33	import json
34	import statistics
35	import sys
36	import time
37	from pathlib import Path
38	from typing import Any
39	
40	import httpx
41	from fastmcp import Client
42	from fastmcp.client.transports import StreamableHttpTransport
43	
44	HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
45	REPO_ROOT = HERE.parents[2]
46	for _p in (str(REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"), str(HERE.parent)):
47	    if _p not in sys.path:
48	        sys.path.insert(0, _p)
49	
50	from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402
51	
52	log = get_logger("script")
53	
54	DEFAULT_BASE = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run"
55	SCENARIO = "lost-ack"
56	SCRATCH = "notes/perf_probe.txt"
57	README = "README.md"
58	
59	
60	def pct(values: list[float], q: float) -> float:
61	    """Nearest-rank percentile — honest for n=5, where interpolation invents precision."""
62	    if not values:
63	        return 0.0
64	    ordered = sorted(values)
65	    idx = min(len(ordered) - 1, max(0, round(q * len(ordered) + 0.5) - 1))
66	    return ordered[idx]
67	
68	
69	def summarise(name: str, samples: list[float]) -> dict[str, Any]:
70	    return {
71	        "op": name,
72	        "n": len(samples),
73	        "min_ms": round(min(samples), 1),
74	        "p50_ms": round(statistics.median(samples), 1),
75	        "mean_ms": round(statistics.fmean(samples), 1),
76	        "p95_ms": round(pct(samples, 0.95), 1),
77	        "max_ms": round(max(samples), 1),
78	        "samples_ms": [round(s, 1) for s in samples],
79	    }
80	
81	
82	class Timer:
83	    def __init__(self) -> None:
84	        self.t0 = time.perf_counter()
85	
86	    @property
87	    def ms(self) -> float:
88	        return (time.perf_counter() - self.t0) * 1000
89	
90	
91	async def one_sample(base: str, idx: int, timings: dict[str, list[float]],
92	                     server_ms: dict[str, list[float]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
93	    detail: dict[str, Any] = {"sample": idx}
94	    async with httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=base, timeout=120) as http:
95	        t = Timer()
96	        r = await http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": SCENARIO})
97	        r.raise_for_status()
98	        reset = r.json()
99	        timings["reset"].append(t.ms)
100	        detail["reset_ms"] = round(t.ms, 1)
101	        episode_id = reset["episode_id"]
102	        detail["episode_id"] = episode_id
103	        detail["sandbox_id"] = reset.get("sandbox_id")
104	        detail["files"] = len(reset.get("files", []))
105	
106	        transport = StreamableHttpTransport(
107	            url=base.rstrip("/") + "/mcp",
108	            headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": episode_id, "X-Request-Id": f"perf-{idx}"},
109	        )
110	        try:
111	            async with Client(transport) as mcp:
112	                async def tool(op: str, name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
113	                    tt = Timer()
114	                    res = await mcp.call_tool(name, args, timeout=60, raise_on_error=False)
115	                    timings[op].append(tt.ms)
116	                    detail[f"{op}_ms"] = round(tt.ms, 1)
117	                    body = res.content[0].text if res.content else "{}"
118	                    try:
119	                        parsed = json.loads(body)
120	                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
121	                        parsed = {"raw": body[:200]}
122	                    if isinstance(parsed, dict) and "duration_ms" in parsed:
123	                        server_ms.setdefault(op, []).append(float(parsed["duration_ms"]))
124	                    if res.is_error:
125	                        detail.setdefault("errors", []).append({op: body[:200]})
126	                    return parsed
127	
128	                tl = Timer()
129	                await mcp.list_tools()
130	                timings["mcp_list_tools"].append(tl.ms)
131	
132	                await tool("list_dir", "list_dir", {"path": "."})
133	                await tool("read_file", "read_file", {"path": README})
134	                await tool("write_file", "write_file",
135	                           {"path": SCRATCH, "content": f"perf probe sample {idx}\n",
136	                            "mode": "overwrite"})
137	                await tool("run_command", "run_command", {"command": "echo hello && ls -1"})
138	                await tool("run_pytest", "run_command", {"command": "python -m pytest -q"})
139	
140	            t = Timer()
141	            obs = await http.get(f"/episodes/{episode_id}")
142	            obs.raise_for_status()
143	            timings["observe"].append(t.ms)
144	            detail["observe_ms"] = round(t.ms, 1)
145	
146	            t = Timer()
147	            ev = await http.post(f"/episodes/{episode_id}/evaluate")
148	            ev.raise_for_status()
149	            timings["evaluate"].append(t.ms)
150	            detail["evaluate_ms"] = round(t.ms, 1)
151	            detail["score"] = ev.json().get("score")
152	
153	            t = Timer()
154	            ls = await http.get("/episodes", params={"probe": "true", "limit": 200})
155	            ls.raise_for_status()
156	            timings["list_episodes"].append(t.ms)
157	            detail["list_episodes_ms"] = round(t.ms, 1)
158	            detail["episodes_in_dict"] = ls.json().get("count")
159	            detail["episodes_live"] = ls.json().get("live")
160	        finally:
161	            t = Timer()
162	            d = await http.delete(f"/episodes/{episode_id}")
163	            timings["delete"].append(t.ms)
164	            detail["delete_ms"] = round(t.ms, 1)
165	            detail["deleted"] = d.status_code == 200 and d.json().get("terminated") is True
166	
167	    log.info("perf.sample", f"sample {idx} done", **{k: v for k, v in detail.items()
168	                                                     if k.endswith("_ms") or k in ("episode_id", "score")})
169	    return detail
170	
171	
172	async def main() -> int:
173	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
174	                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
175	    ap.add_argument("--base", default=DEFAULT_BASE)
176	    ap.add_argument("--samples", type=int, default=5)
177	    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
178	    args = ap.parse_args()
179	
180	    stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ", time.gmtime())
181	    out = Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{stamp}_perf"
182	    if not out.is_absolute():
183	        out = REPO_ROOT / out
184	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
185	
186	    ops = ["reset", "mcp_list_tools", "list_dir", "read_file", "write_file", "run_command",
187	           "run_pytest", "observe", "evaluate", "list_episodes", "delete"]
188	    timings: dict[str, list[float]] = {op: [] for op in ops}
189	    server_ms: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
190	    samples: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
191	
192	    async with httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=args.base, timeout=60) as http:
193	        health = (await http.get("/health")).json()
194	
195	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
196	    for i in range(1, args.samples + 1):
197	        samples.append(await one_sample(args.base, i, timings, server_ms))
198	    total_s = round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1)
199	
200	    async with httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=args.base, timeout=120) as http:
201	        after = (await http.get("/episodes", params={"probe": "true", "limit": 500})).json()
202	
203	    report = {
204	        "base": args.base,
205	        "scenario": SCENARIO,
206	        "finished_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
207	        "samples": args.samples,
208	        "duration_s": total_s,
209	        "health": health,
210	        "latency_ms": {op: summarise(op, timings[op]) for op in ops if timings[op]},
211	        "sandbox_reported_ms": {op: summarise(op, v) for op, v in server_ms.items()},
212	        "per_sample": samples,
213	        "cleanup": {
214	            "all_deleted": all(s.get("deleted") for s in samples),
215	            "episodes_live_after": after.get("live"),
216	            "episodes_in_dict_after": after.get("count"),
217	            # split ours from everyone else's: this deployment is shared, and another session's
218	            # live episode is not a leak of ours.
219	            "mine_live_after": [e["episode_id"] for e in after.get("episodes", [])
220	                                if e.get("alive") and e["episode_id"] in
221	                                {s.get("episode_id") for s in samples}],
222	            "other_sessions_live": [e["episode_id"] for e in after.get("episodes", [])
223	                                    if e.get("alive") and e["episode_id"] not in
224	                                    {s.get("episode_id") for s in samples}],
225	        },
226	    }
227	    (out / "latency.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
228	
229	    width = max(len(o) for o in report["latency_ms"])
230	    print(f"\nsandbox-env latency, {args.samples} samples, {args.base}")
231	    print(f"{'op'.ljust(width)}   n    min     p50    mean     max")
232	    for op, s in report["latency_ms"].items():
233	        print(f"{op.ljust(width)} {s['n']:>3} {s['min_ms']:>7.1f} {s['p50_ms']:>7.1f} "
234	              f"{s['mean_ms']:>7.1f} {s['max_ms']:>7.1f}")
235	    print(f"\ncleanup: all_deleted={report['cleanup']['all_deleted']} "
236	          f"mine_live_after={report['cleanup']['mine_live_after']} "
237	          f"(other sessions live: {len(report['cleanup']['other_sessions_live'])})")
238	    print(f"evidence: {out / 'latency.json'}")
239	    return 0 if report["cleanup"]["all_deleted"] and not report["cleanup"]["mine_live_after"] else 1
240	
241	
242	if __name__ == "__main__":
243	    sys.exit(asyncio.run(main()))

1	"""Lifecycle + cost hardening: nothing leaves a Modal Sandbox running.
2	
3	A stray sandbox is the only failure mode in this service that costs real money, and it is silent:
4	nothing in a normal run tells you that the episode which crashed twenty minutes ago is still
5	burning a container. These tests pin the four ways a sandbox is reclaimed —
6	
7	    1. `DELETE /episodes/{id}`            the normal path (already covered in test_api.py)
8	    2. an exception anywhere in `reset`   before the episode is recorded, including the Dict write
9	    3. the TTL sweep on every reset       for episodes whose owner never came back
10	    4. `reap` (modal_app)                 the manual backstop, with the episode Dict kept honest
11	
12	— plus the ops listing that makes a stray visible in the first place.
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
18	
19	import httpx
20	import pytest
21	
22	from sandbox_env import episodes, grader, workspace
23	from sandbox_env.workspace import FakeWorkspace, WorkspaceError
24	
25	
26	def iso_ago(seconds: float) -> str:
27	    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=seconds)
28	    return ts.isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
29	
30	
31	def age_out(episode_id: str, seconds: float) -> dict:
32	    ep = episodes.load(episode_id)
33	    ep["created_at"] = iso_ago(seconds)
34	    episodes.save(ep)
35	    return ep

45	        raise WorkspaceError("upload died")

51	        episodes.reset("missing-config")
52	    assert ws.terminated is True, "a reset that fails after create must not leak the sandbox"
53	    assert episodes.episode_ids() == [], "and must not leave a half-written episode behind"

142	def test_the_reset_path_sweep_is_throttled(monkeypatch, fake_ws) -> None:
143	    """Housekeeping must not sit on the latency-critical call once per reset.
144	
145	    Measured cost of the unthrottled version against the live Dict (125 episodes):
146	    reset p50 6.7 s. With the throttle + a single-RPC scan: 1.9 s (runs/*_perf/latency.json).
147	    """
148	    calls: list[int] = []
149	    monkeypatch.setattr(episodes, "sweep", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(1) or {
150	        "scanned": 0, "expired": [], "purged": [], "errors": []})
151	
152	    assert "skipped" not in episodes.sweep_quietly()
153	    assert episodes.sweep_quietly()["skipped"] == "throttled"
154	    assert len(calls) == 1, "a second sweep inside the interval must not touch the store"
155	
156	    assert "skipped" not in episodes.sweep_quietly(force=True)
157	    assert len(calls) == 2, "force overrides the throttle (the out-of-band sweep function)"
158	
159	    episodes.reset_sweep_throttle()
160	    episodes.sweep_quietly()
161	    assert len(calls) == 3
